That is what the next two blocks spend on runes (the nature, law and cosmic runes alone cost about 880,000 at Ali's fixed prices, with chaos runes and Aubury's water runes on top); the rest of the section's money comes from a second session after the Mage Training Arena. Expect to be around 76 Thieving.

            Done when you hold about 910,000 gp.

            Coins: about 910,000 gp · Slots: about 19/28 plus wines · Requirements: The Feud, Rune Mysteries and Gertrude's Cat for Rogue Trader; 20 Crafting and 25 Thieving for The Golem; 65 Thieving for Menaphite thugs

            Wiki references Rune Mysteries Rogue Trader The Golem Menaphite thug

            Ali Morrisane's runes

            The Mage Training Arena burns a lot of runes, and Ali Morrisane in Al Kharid sells nature, law, cosmic and chaos runes one step from the arena. Buying them in the Mage Arena bank in level 54 Wilderness would mean carrying 800,000 gp through the worst place in the game for a UIM, because a Wilderness death converts what you carry for the killer. Ali's stock per world is small, so this block is a long hop, but nothing in it can kill you.

            Gate in Rogue Trader's rune section.
            Carry in about 20/28 with the three new rune stacks and two spare staves.
            Exit the runes for the Mage Training Arena bought; a few hundred gp left.
            Carries forward the rune stacks (the arena).

            Aubury, Zaff and the way back

            From Pollnivneach, take the carpet to the Shantay Pass and walk north to Al Kharid. Break a Chronicle card and walk into Varrock.

            Talk to Aubury in his rune shop about Ali's business proposal. While you're there, buy about 1,000 water runes from his stock. Walk to Zaff's staff shop and buy a staff of earth and a staff of fire; with the staff of air you already wear, they cover every elemental rune the arena needs.

            Aubury's shop is in south-east Varrock, where you first took him the research package in 1.1. The water runes do more than the arena: they are the Water Bolt runes for Sir Mordred and the outlaws later in the section, which is why they go in the rune pouch when you get it.

            Walk out of Varrock's east gate and south past the Al Kharid mine to the north entrance of Al Kharid, then back to Ali.

            Done when Ali has sent you on and you are back.

            Open the casket and buy

            Talk to Ali again and ask to see his runes. Choose to examine the lock on the large casket and solve the runedoku; solving it once unlocks Ali's standard stock of non-elemental runes for good. Buy every nature, law and cosmic rune he has, hop worlds, and repeat. The chaos runes wait until after the arena, when you have the money for them.

            Only the examine option works on the large casket: forcing it or picking the lock always fails, and each failure drains a few levels of Strength and Agility or Thieving. The runedoku is a grid of runes in which no rune may repeat in any row, column or 3x3 block. Ali's prices never rise as his stock falls, so clearing all 100 of a rune costs the same per rune as buying one: there is no reason to leave any on a world.

            Your targets are about 2,300 nature, 1,050 law and 800 cosmic runes (about 1,000 if you will still be below 57 Magic in the Enchanting Chamber, see below). Ali stocks only 10 cosmic runes per world, against 100 of each of the others, so the cosmics take about 80 hops: buy them on every hop while you clear the natures and laws. That is more nature runes than a basic run needs, and the extra is deliberate: the arena's Creature Graveyard rewards free inventory slots, a UIM has fewer of them, and nature runes are how you pay for that.

            At Ali's prices the natures are 225 gp each, the laws 300 and the cosmics 62, which is where the 880,000 gp from the blackjacking goes. Each rune type stacks, so the 4,150 runes take only three slots.

            While you're in Al Kharid, sell the two cut diamonds from the Waterfall Quest to the gem trader and the two gold bars to the general store. Nothing in this chapter uses the diamonds, and The Dig Site gives you two fresh gold bars when your jewellery needs them; together that is four slots back before the arena.

            Done when the rune stacks are bought.

            Spend down before the arena

            The Alchemist's Playground doesn't let you bring coins in, and the only coin store a UIM can use this early is the Last Man Standing coffer in the Ferox Enclave (see the storage glossary), a detour that isn't worth it for the few coins you would keep here; Nightmare Zone's coffer needs five of its quests. Plan the last purchases so you walk into the arena with only a few hundred gp; anything left over you spend on extra nature runes or drop at the door.

            The ban covers coin pouches too, so make sure none are left over from blackjacking.

            Done when a few hundred gp remain.

            Coins: a few hundred gp · Slots: about 20/28 with the new rune stacks and two spare staves · Requirements: Rogue Trader (rune section)

            Wiki references Rogue Trader Ali's Discount Wares Magic shops Ultimate Ironman Mode

            The Mage Training Arena

            This block earns Bones to Peaches and a rune pouch from the Rewards Guardian. Since August 2025 the rune pouch is sold there for 150 Telekinetic, 150 Graveyard, 1,500 Enchantment and 200 Alchemist pizazz points, so a UIM no longer needs a Last Man Standing win for it. Together with Bones to Peaches (200, 200, 2,000 and 300) and one mud rune (1, 1, 15 and 1 points; a 1.4 quest uses it), your targets are:

            Gate in 21 Magic for Low Alchemy, 33 for Telekinetic Grab, 15 for Bones to Bananas, 60 to cast Bones to Peaches.
            Carry in the arena runes and staves: about 22/28 with a few hundred coins.
            Exit the rune pouch and Bones to Peaches; about 5,000 gp.
            Carries forward the rune pouch (core kit).
            RoomPoints
            Telekinetic Theatre351
            Alchemist's Playground501
            Enchanting Chamber3,515
            Creature Graveyard351

            The rooms are done in the order that keeps the most slots free for the one that needs them, the Graveyard, which comes last and after the rune pouch.

            The arena is north-east of Al Kharid, north of the Emir's Arena, and you no longer need a pizazz progress hat to enter the rooms. Only the Creature Graveyard can damage you, and there only lightly.

            Inventory for the arena

            Before you go in, drop the steel axe (Bob in Lumbridge sells another for 200 gp when you next need one) and the mind runes. What stays is: hammer, knife, adamant pickaxe, costume needle, the noted steel bars, the willow blackjack, the swordfish and seaweed stacks, the plank and oak plank stacks, the marks of grace, the Golem's strange implement, your rune stacks (nature, law, cosmic, chaos and water) and the two spare staves, with the staff of air and the Chronicle worn. With the few hundred coins that is 20 slots, which leaves eight free for the first three rooms. Keep the staff of fire until the Enchanting Chamber is done if you are below 57 Magic there (Lvl-3 Enchant takes fire runes); at 57 or more, drop it after the Alchemist's Playground.

            Clue scrolls and caskets are banned in every room, so if a clue has turned up from blackjacking, finish or drop it before you go in. Bob's axe is bought back in the Lumbridge block for Lost City's tree, so dropping this one costs nothing but the walk.

            Done when you enter the arena with only the kit above.

            Alchemist's Playground

            Wield the staff of fire. Low Alchemy needs 21 Magic and High Alchemy 55; switch as soon as you can for the extra Magic XP: the coins, and so the points, are the same either way. Alchemise only the item the board marks as free or worth 30 coins, deposit the coins in the chute whenever you hold a few hundred, and stop at 501 points.

            The board's values and the item positions change once a minute, so search the cupboards for the marked item and alchemise as many as you can before the change. Every 100 coins you deposit is one point and pays 10 coins back to you, which is where the 5,000 gp in this block's coin line comes from.

            Done when your Alchemist points are earned.

            Enchanting Chamber

            Wield the staff of earth for Lvl-4 Enchant (57 Magic, the target), or the staff of fire for Lvl-3 if you are below 57. Cast the highest Enchant spell your Magic allows (a Wizard's mind bomb boosts you early, but nothing near the arena sells one, so buy it at the Rising Sun in Falador or another Asgarnian pub before you come) on the shapes the room shows as bonus, and drop the shapes you don't enchant. Dragonstones that spawn are worth double. Deposit the orbs in the hole in the middle for the extra runes, and stop at 3,515 points.

            Each enchant is worth as many points as the spell's level, and every tenth shape counts double, so Enchant Level 4 earns twice what Enchant Level 2 does for the same cosmic rune, which is why the guide pushes for it. The wiki averages 4.4 points a cast at Lvl-4 and 3.6 at Lvl-3, so 3,515 points take about 800 casts at Lvl-4 or about 980 at Lvl-3, one cosmic rune each. Every 20 orbs you deposit pays back three blood, death or cosmic runes.

            Done when your Enchantment points are earned.

            Telekinetic Theatre

            Wield the staff of air. Each maze is a statue you move with Telekinetic Grab (33 Magic, one law rune per cast) until it rests on the target square; every fifth maze in a row pays 10 law runes and 1,000 Magic XP back. Stop at 351 points.

            Each solved maze is worth 2 points and 250 Magic XP, and the fifth in a row adds a bonus of 8 points and refills your run energy.

            Done when your Telekinetic points are earned.

            Creature Graveyard, the rune pouch and Bones to Peaches

            Wield the staff of earth again. Grab bones from the piles, cast Bones to Bananas, and deposit the fruit in the chutes; every 16 fruit is a point, and the bones that fall from the ceiling hurt, so eat a banana when you need to.

            The falling bones hit for 2 and can be dodged by stepping off the tile they land on. You can't bring bananas or peaches into the room, so the bananas you eat are ones you have just made, at 2 Hitpoints each. Each point also earns one bonus rune (water, earth, nature, death or blood), paid when you leave the room. This is the only room where you can take damage, so keep an eye on your Hitpoints between casts.

            At 150 Graveyard points, go upstairs and buy the rune pouch from the Rewards Guardian. Put your law, nature and water runes in it. The pouch takes a slot itself, so that frees two, which is where the Graveyard's extra speed comes from: with about ten free slots, grab only as many bones as your free slots can hold as fruit. Carry on to 351 points, then buy Bones to Peaches (the purchase has no level, but casting it needs 60 Magic; a Wizard's mind bomb, bought beforehand, covers the gap if the arena hasn't) and one mud rune for a 1.4 quest. Skip the mist runes, which nothing in this guide uses, and the soul runes: the Falador, Lumbridge and Varrock teleports cover the Kourend trips. Keep the staff of earth on until the palace cast below, then drop it.

            The Rewards Guardian is on the top floor of the arena. The mud rune is for The Eyes of Glouphrie at the start of 1.4, where it is ground into magic glue; it is one slot until then. Before any planned death from here on, check the rune pouch's death setting, as the guide's rules at the start of the chapter describe.

            On your way out, kill an Al Kharid warrior in the palace for its bones and cast Bones to Peaches there once for the diary, then drop the staff of earth.

            Done when you own the rune pouch and Bones to Peaches.

            Coins: about 5,000 gp from the Playground's 10% coin return · Slots: about 18/28 with the rune pouch · Requirements: 21 Magic for Low Alchemy, 33 for Telekinetic Grab, 15 for Bones to Bananas, 60 to cast Bones to Peaches

            Wiki references Mage Training Arena Rewards Guardian (Mage Training Arena) Rune pouch Bones to Peaches

            The second blackjacking session

            You leave the arena with about 5,000 gp from the Alchemist's Playground and the rest of the section still to buy: Excalibur's replacement fee, a rune sword, quest items, sawmill planks, shop food, the Hosidius move back to Kourend and the Rellekka move. This session funds all of it plus a buffer for 1.4.

            Gate in 65 Thieving; the rogue outfit.
            Carry in the core kit: about 18/28.
            Exit about 600,000 gp after the chaos runes.
            Carries forward the coins for the rest of 1.3.

            Take the carpet from the Shantay Pass to Pollnivneach, wear the rogue outfit and blackjack the Menaphite thugs at the southern end of town to 630,000 gp. You reach it at about 80 Thieving, and blackjacking pauses there until the end of 1.4, where it takes you from 80 to 85 for the Chapter 2 money. Drop the willow blackjack when you're done. On the way back through Al Kharid, buy about 300 chaos runes from Ali (about 34,000 gp): they are the Bolt runes for the rest of the section. Buy about 40 earth runes from him too (20 a world, so hop once; 5 gp each): Marlo's house tablets are gone and the staff of earth was dropped at the palace, so from here on you cast Teleport to House (40 Magic: a law from the pouch, an air from the staff of air and one earth rune). Then cast Teleport to House to Taverley, put the rogue outfit in the armour case, and walk to Jatix's Herblore Shop: buy a pestle and mortar and two vials for The Dig Site and The Golem, which are next, and an eye of newt for the Lumbridge cook in Recipe for Disaster. Nowhere near Varrock or the Digsite sells vials.

            Ali sells chaos runes at 112 gp each, 100 per world, so the 300 take three hops. At Jatix's, a vial costs 2 gp, the pestle and mortar 4 and the eye of newt 3. The outfit can come out of the armour case again whenever you want it, because the set went in complete; at the end of 1.4 you take it out for the last session.

            Your core kit from here on is: coins, rune pouch (law, nature and water), cosmic, chaos, earth and mud runes (cast Teleport to House with the earth runes, never with the mud rune, which The Eyes of Glouphrie needs), hammer, knife, adamant pickaxe, costume needle, the noted steel bars, the swordfish, seaweed, plank and oak plank stacks, the marks of grace and the Golem's strange implement, with the staff of air and the Chronicle worn. That is 17 slots; the pestle and mortar, the two vials and the eye of newt make it 21. The rogue outfit goes into the armour case on the Taverley stop above. Each block below adds only what its own quests use and drops it when they are done.

            Done when you hold about 600,000 gp after the chaos runes.

            Coins: about 630,000 gp, about 600,000 after the chaos runes · Slots: about 21/28 with the Jatix items · Requirements: 65 Thieving

            Wiki references Blackjacking Ultimate Ironman Guide/Thieving

            The Barbarian Outpost, the Gnome Stronghold and Merlin's Crystal

            This block starts four things in Kandarin that cost nothing to carry: Alfred Grimhand's Barcrawl, Dwarf Cannon, The Grand Tree and Merlin's Crystal. Merlin's Crystal starts here because its next steps, the Lady of the Lake in Taverley and the beggar in Port Sarim, sit on later routes; its ritual and Excalibur come back to Camelot near the end of the section. The only new carries are the barcrawl card, which is stamped bar by bar through Chapter 2, and the greenman's ale for the Lumbridge cook.

            Gate in 45 Magic for Camelot Teleport.
            Carry in about 23/28 with the barcrawl card and the ale.
            Exit Alfred Grimhand's Barcrawl, Dwarf Cannon, The Grand Tree and Merlin's Crystal started; the greenman's ale; about 595,000 gp.
            Carries forward the barcrawl card (stamped through Chapter 2), the ale (Recipe for Disaster in Lumbridge).

            The barcrawl and Rasolo

            Use the Barbarian Assault minigame teleport and talk to the barbarian guard by the agility course entrance to start Alfred Grimhand's Barcrawl. The barcrawl card is one slot until the last stamp in Chapter 2. Each bar charges a few coins for its drink, and some drinks drain stats for a short while.

            Never drop the card: if you lose it, the barcrawl starts again from the first bar. A few drinks also take Hitpoints rather than stats, the worst being the Heart Stopper in Ardougne's Flying Horse Inn, which can take 20 to 30 percent of your health, so drink those with your Hitpoints high. The finished barcrawl opens the Barbarian Outpost agility course, which a Kandarin medium diary task and later quests need.

            Walk south past Baxtorian Falls to Rasolo, the wandering merchant, and buy one greenman's ale. Keep it for Recipe for Disaster, which starts in Lumbridge three blocks from now.

            Rasolo stands south of the falls and west of the Fishing Guild. The ale costs 4 gp, and he holds only one at a time, restocking it every five minutes, so if another player has just bought it, wait or hop.

            Done when the barcrawl card is started and you hold the ale.

            Dwarf Cannon and The Grand Tree

            Talk to Captain Lawgof at the dwarven camp to start Dwarf Cannon, and follow it, repairing the railings with your hammer and the railings he gives you, until he asks you to find Lollk. That part of the quest waits for Chapter 1.4, when it comes back on the Ardougne route.

            The camp is between the coal trucks and the Fishing Guild. There are six bent railings; with your hammer and his railings in your inventory, use Inspect on each one to fix it (using the hammer on the railing does nothing). A failed attempt costs a point or two of Hitpoints or briefly lowers your Strength or Crafting, so keep trying. Then check the watchtower to the south for Gilob, bring his remains back to Lawgof, and he sends you after Lollk, who is in the Goblin Cave next to the Fishing Guild.

            Walk into the Gnome Stronghold and talk to King Narnode Shareen in the Grand Tree to start The Grand Tree; its next step is Hazelmere, which you reach by fairy ring in 1.4. Climb to the first floor and buy the barcrawl drink from Blurberry. Don't buy vodka or Grand Tree groceries here: Bone Voyage's two vodka are bought in Civitas illa Fortis at the end of the section, and nothing else in this section uses the groceries.

            Have two free slots when you talk to the king: he takes you under the tree and hands you a translation book and a bark sample. You don't carry them: drop both once the dialogue ends, because he gives you a new pair when The Grand Tree resumes in 1.4. Blurberry's Fire Toad Blast costs Hitpoints rather than stats.

            The Kandarin diary's fire giant is in the Waterfall dungeon. Since a 2017 update the dungeon no longer needs Glarial's amulet once the Waterfall Quest is complete, but the fire giants there are level 86, and the task waits for a later visit.

            Done when Dwarf Cannon and The Grand Tree are started.

            Merlin's Crystal to Morgan Le Faye

            Teleport to Camelot and talk to King Arthur to start Merlin's Crystal. Talk to Sir Gawain and Sir Lancelot, then walk to Catherby and hide in Arhein's crate to reach Keep Le Faye. Inside, climb to Sir Mordred (level 39). Fight him with Water Bolt (water runes from the pouch, air from your staff, and chaos runes); a melee player can flinch him, but magic can't flinch. When he is nearly dead, Morgan Le Faye stops the fight and tells you how to free Merlin. The next step is Excalibur, from the Lady of the Lake in Taverley, and it waits until you pass Port Sarim with a loaf of bread for the beggar. Don't kill a giant bat here: the bat bones are for the black candle ritual at the end of the section, and you collect them on that visit.

            Sir Lancelot is on the upper floor of Camelot Castle; Gawain is in the castle or its courtyard. In Catherby, the crates are behind the Candle Shop, just north of the dock between the bank and the shop; climb out when the game asks. In the keep, ignore the renegade knights and take the stairs to the top floor, where Mordred is. When Morgan Le Faye appears and begs for his life, don't pick the option to kill him and don't walk away from the dialogue: either one means fighting him again. She explains the magic symbol, the bat bones, the black candle and Excalibur that the rest of the quest needs. To leave, take the stairs down to the ground floor and walk out of the doors; the crates are the only way back in, so don't leave before she has spoken.

            Done when Merlin's Crystal waits for Excalibur.

            Coins: about 595,000 gp · Slots: about 23/28 with the barcrawl card and the ale · Requirements: 45 Magic for Camelot Teleport

            Wiki references Alfred Grimhand's Barcrawl Rasolo Dwarf Cannon The Grand Tree Merlin's Crystal

            Varrock: A Ruff Situation, The Dig Site and Priest in Peril

            This block completes A Ruff Situation and The Dig Site, takes Priest in Peril to its essence step, collects The Golem's statuette and fills your kudos. The Dig Site's reward is the turning point for Mining, 15,300 XP, which is what lets you mine coal and essence later in the section. Nothing you pick up here outlives the next block except two gold bars and one charcoal.

            Gate in 10 Agility, 10 Herblore and 25 Thieving for The Dig Site; 15 Crafting for A Ruff Situation.
            Carry in the core kit, the Jatix items, the barcrawl card and the ale: about 23/28.
            Exit A Ruff Situation and The Dig Site done; Priest in Peril at its essence step; Demon Slayer and Dragon Slayer I started; about 95 kudos; the statuette; about 590,000 gp.
            Carries forward the statuette and one vial (The Golem), two gold bars (a ring of dueling), one charcoal (the goblin generals' slop).

            Free slots matter in this block: The Dig Site hands you a specimen jar, brush, panning tray, trowel and rock pick, and two ropes. Drop each tool the moment its exam is passed, and drop all of them when the quest completes.

            Varrock

            The Chronicle lands you at the Champions' Guild. Talk to the Guildmaster to start Dragon Slayer I; the map pieces and Crandor are Chapter 2. Don't buy the rune sword yet: the Temple Guardian below is flinched with your adamant pickaxe, which you can wield at 30 Attack (31 Mining to mine with it), and the sword is bought when the Black Knight Titan needs it.

            In Varrock, buy the barcrawl drink in the Blue Moon Inn, do one lap of the Varrock rooftop course for the diary, and buy a tinderbox and a bucket at the general store (The Dig Site needs the tinderbox, Priest in Peril the bucket). Steal a cup of tea from the tea stall in east Varrock; the Digsite's panning guide wants it.

            The Blue Moon's drink, Uncle Humphrey's Gutrot, costs 50 gp and briefly lowers several combat stats and your Hitpoints, so don't drink it just before the Temple Guardian.

            Talk to Gypsy Aris in the square to start Demon Slayer, then to Sir Prysin in the palace. The three keys and Delrith come later, in one visit, so none of them rides in your inventory.

            Don't buy a stack of clothes and armour in Varrock here (priest gowns, a brown apron, a black platebody, green d'hide). Each is used in 1.4 or Chapter 2 and sold in the same shop when that step comes; buy them then.

            Done when the Varrock purchases are done and Demon Slayer is started.

            A Ruff Situation

            Buy a fur from Baraek and pick a grain from any wheat field. Walk out to the Dog Shelter south-west of Edgeville Monastery and talk to Talia (twice) to start A Ruff Situation, then follow it back to Gertrude in west Varrock. When the quest asks for a stuffed dog, use the grain on the fur with your costume needle in your inventory. The last step is a fight with two outlaws (levels 22 and 23); Water Bolt handles both. The quest needs 15 Crafting.

            The costume needle stands in for a needle and thread, which is one more reason it stays in the core kit. The stray dog leads you from Gertrude's to a house west of the clothes shop, then through the bank to the field north of the Cooks' Guild, where Picklenose wants the stuffed dog; the wheat there will do for your grain if you haven't picked one yet. If you teleport away in the middle, the stray and her puppy wait near Picklenose or at their den north of the Apothecary, so you can pick up where you left off. Return both puppies to the shelter to finish: 1 quest point and 1,000 Crafting XP.

            Done when A Ruff Situation is complete.

            The Dig Site and Priest in Peril

            Walk east to the Digsite and start The Dig Site by talking to the examiner, who sends you back to the curator for a letter of recommendation. Find Elissa in the north-eastern corner of the site for The Golem and read Varmen's notes on the south-eastern bookshelf in the Exam Centre; she sends you to the museum.

            The examiner is in the Exam Centre at the south of the site. Fail the first exam on purpose: the students around the site give you the answers in return for small favours (a teddy from a bush north of the centre, a special cup from panning, and an animal skull from pickpocketing the workmen), and you have to talk to each student twice.

            Walk to the Paterdomus temple north-east of the Digsite and continue Priest in Peril. Take the trapdoor north of the temple and kill the Temple Guardian (level 30) in the mausoleum in melee with your adamant pickaxe wielded, since magic can't hurt it, then return to King Roald in Varrock. Back at the temple, kill the hooded Monk of Zamorak (level 30) for a golden key, and swap it for the iron key on the right monument (the golden key doesn't open the gate). Since 30 June 2026 the Monks of Zamorak there are aggressive. Fill your bucket with murky water at the well in the monument room, use the iron key on Drezel's cell door upstairs so he blesses the water, and pour it on the coffin outside the cell. Drezel's last request is 50 unnoted essence, which you bring at the start of the Morytania block. Drop the bucket.

            The same update left only one level 30 monk in the temple, the one with the key, and he fights with magic; the spot just south of a pew lets you flinch him. No monument is marked as the right one, so study each until you find the iron key. Drezel's cell is on the top floor. He takes rune or pure essence, mixed, in as many batches as you like, which is how the Morytania block delivers it with only a few free slots.

            In the Varrock Museum, ask Curator Haig Halen about the missing statuette for The Golem, pickpocket him for the display cabinet key (25 Thieving), unlock Display Case 30 on the upper floor and take the statuette. Get the curator's letter while you're there, return to the Digsite and complete The Dig Site. Its steps need a specimen jar, brush and rock pick (found on the site), two ropes for the winches (pickpocket the Digsite workmen), the cup of tea, the pestle and mortar and one vial, a tinderbox, a charcoal and an opal or uncut opal, which you may find while panning. The charcoal comes from the specimen tray north of the tent, where the wiki gives it as a quest source; search until you have two, because the second one is for the goblin generals' slop later. When the unidentified liquid is identified, every vial in your inventory turns into nitroglycerin, so put your second vial on the ground for that step and pick it up again afterwards. The reward is 2 quest points, 15,300 Mining XP, 2,000 Herblore XP and two gold bars. If you find polished buttons while digging, keep them: Animal Magnetism needs them in Chapter 2.

            Display Case 30 is in the first row of the upper floor's display section, the fourth case. If you ever lose the statuette before The Golem is done, the display cabinet key opens the empty case for a replacement. The chemicals are the one real danger in the quest. Never drop the unidentified liquid or the finished compound: dropping the liquid costs 25 Hitpoints and dropping the compound 65 (neither can kill you), and either way you have to collect the ingredients again. Use the liquid on Terry Balando to have it identified rather than emptying it. The winches need 10 Agility; use the rope on the west winch first for the arcenia root, then the north-east winch for Doug Deeping. Level 22 skeletons wait in the room with the stone tablet, and the wiki advises avoiding them below combat level 45, so take the tablet and leave.

            Done when The Dig Site is complete and Priest in Peril waits at its essence step.

            Kudos

            Keep the Level 3 certificate from The Dig Site and don't hand it to the curator yet: he takes it in exchange for a free drink, the fruit blast for the Lumbridge cook, and the wiki says the certificate is used up then and can't be got back, so you give it to him on the way to the cook, after the desert. Go down to the basement and take the natural history quiz with Orlando Smith: 2 kudos per display, 28 in all, plus 1,000 Slayer and 1,000 Hunter XP. Upstairs, tell Historian Minas about your completed quests for their kudos. Leave Shield of Arrav for later: its antique lamp from Minas (1,000 XP) needs 20 in the chosen skill, and your Herblore is about 18 here. Tell Minas about it on the Demon Slayer visit in 1.3, after Shades of Mort'ton has taken Herblore to 21, and use the lamp on Herblore then.

            Each of the 14 quiz displays needs three correct answers on its plaque. Historian Minas wanders the upper floor, and most of the quests he asks about are worth 5 kudos each; Rune Mysteries, Shield of Arrav and Hazeel Cult are among the ones you have done, and Priest in Peril, Merlin's Crystal, Demon Slayer and The Grand Tree add theirs when you finish them later in the chapter.

            At the museum's dig area, take rocks from the crates, clean them at the specimen table with the tools there, and hand the finds to the museum staff until each of the five display finds (the pottery, the old and ancient symbols and the old and ancient coins) has been identified by an archaeologist and placed in its display case: 10 kudos each, the full 50. If a clean necklace turns up, give it to an archaeologist to learn the digsite pendant enchantment. Drop the rest of the junk as it appears. At 51 kudos the information clerk gives you 1,000 Mining XP.

            The cleaning area is at the southern end of the museum's ground floor. The kudos matter beyond the XP: Bone Voyage, which opens Fossil Island at the end of this section, needs 100, and the quests you finish between here and there take you past it.

            Done when you have about 88 kudos (28 from the quiz, 50 from the finds, and 10 from Minas for Rune Mysteries and Hazeel Cult).

            Coins: about 590,000 gp · Slots: 28/28 with the statuette, the gold bars, the charcoal, the tinderbox and the Level 3 certificate; The Dig Site's tools come and go within the quest · Requirements: 10 Agility, 10 Herblore and 25 Thieving for The Dig Site; 15 Crafting for A Ruff Situation

            Wiki references The Dig Site Priest in Peril The Golem A Ruff Situation Varrock Museum Kudos Charcoal

            The desert: The Golem, your ring of dueling and 43 Mining

            This block completes The Golem, makes your ring of dueling, and trains Mining from about 35 to 43 on sandstone. It also makes the molten glass the workshop's crafting table needs and the silver sickle for Nature Spirit, so that nothing for them has to be bought later where it isn't sold.

            Gate in 20 Crafting and 25 Thieving for The Golem; 27 Crafting and 27 Magic for the ring of dueling; 35 Mining for sandstone (iron ore first).
            Carry in 28/28 with the Level 3 certificate; 24/28 after the Al Kharid stop.
            Exit The Golem done; a ring of dueling worn; 43 Mining; a molten glass and a silver sickle; about 570,000 gp.
            Carries forward the molten glass (the workshop's crafting table), the silver sickle (Nature Spirit).

            The Golem

            Walk to Al Kharid and make room before you buy anything. Sell the spare gold bar at the general store and drop the tinderbox (the Lumbridge general store sells another for 1 gp when the next block needs one). Then make the ring of dueling now rather than after the quest: buy one cut emerald from the gem trader and a ring mould from Dommik's crafting shop, make an emerald ring at the furnace from the other gold bar and the emerald (27 Crafting), cast Enchant Level 2 on it for a ring of dueling and wear it. Drop the ring mould and the cosmic runes: nothing else in this section enchants. Buy a papyrus from Ali Morrisane (10 gp). At the Shantay Pass, buy a Shantay pass, a desert shirt, robe and boots and two waterskins, and walk east across the desert to the Ruins of Uzer, as you did to start the quest. On the way, steal a phoenix feather from the Desert Phoenix near the desert clay mine by Eagle's Eyrie.

            You arrive from Varrock with 28 items once the Level 3 certificate is counted, and this trip adds a papyrus, two waterskins and the feather, which is why the gold bars, the tinderbox and the cosmic runes go first: they bring you down to 24, and you reach Uzer with 28. Put on each desert piece as you buy it, so none of them needs a slot, and buy the Shantay pass last; the gate takes it from you. The Shantay pass costs 5 gp. Stealing the feather needs 25 Thieving, far below yours.

            At Uzer, pick a black mushroom by the stairs and finish The Golem following the wiki's steps: place the statuette in the alcove downstairs, turn the statues to face north and go through the portal, talk to the golem, grind the black mushroom into your vial with the pestle and mortar, make the phoenix quill pen from the feather and the dye, write the golem program on the papyrus, and use the strange implement and the program on the golem. The quest gives 1 quest point, 1,000 Thieving and 1,000 Crafting XP, and opens the carpet between the Shantay Pass and Uzer. Drop the pestle and mortar. The two gems on the throne are optional; leave them.

            The statuette goes in the central alcove. Turn every statue to face the double doors to the north: examined, the eastern ones should face right and the western ones left, and when they are all correct they lock and the doors open. The vial must be empty when you grind the mushroom, which is why you kept the second vial on the ground through the nitroglycerin step at the Digsite.

            Done when The Golem is complete.

            Al Kharid: the ring of dueling and the silver sickle

            Ride the Uzer carpet to the Shantay Pass and walk to Al Kharid. Your ring of dueling is already on your finger from the start of this block. At Dommik's crafting shop buy a sickle mould. Mine one silver ore in the Al Kharid mine. At the Al Kharid furnace, smelt the silver ore and cast it into a silver sickle with the sickle mould (18 Crafting); Nature Spirit blesses it in the next-but-one block. Drop the mould.

            The blessing happens in the pool in the nature spirit's grotto once Nature Spirit is done.

            From here on you wear the ring of dueling. Its Castle Wars teleport is next to the Observatory, and its Ferox Enclave teleport is a UIM's best friend: the enclave's pool restores run energy and its bank booths unnote.

            A full ring holds eight teleports, and the last one turns it to dust, so count your rubs: each one tells you how many are left. The ring's other destinations are the Emir's Arena, next to the Mage Training Arena, and the Fortis Colosseum once you have earned the Hero title there.

            Done when you wear a ring of dueling and hold the silver sickle.

            Sandstone to 43 Mining

            Mine an iron ore in the Al Kharid mine for the diary and drop it, then keep mining iron until you reach 35 Mining (about 140 ores, dropped as you go: The Dig Site and the information clerk leave you at about 31): the sandstone needs it. Do one lap of the Al Kharid rooftop course for the diary.

            Take the carpet from the Shantay Pass to Pollnivneach and walk to the quarry; bring your adamant pickaxe and four or five waterskins, since the quarry is in the desert heat (the Shantay guards at the Ruins of Unkah, just south of the quarry, sell full waterskins if you run short). Mine sandstone and grind it in Drew's grinder as you go, rather than dropping it; the grinder keeps the sand for you. Stop at 43 Mining, then buy a bucket from the Pollnivneach general store, bring it to Drew and claim a bucket of sand for 50 gp. Drew can keep your ground sand for later, but this chapter needs only this one.

            The quarry is south of the Bandit Camp, west of Pollnivneach: walk to the south end of the town and head west. Wear the desert clothes there too: Pollnivneach is a town and free of the heat, but the quarry is not. The grinder is just west of the quarry. Bigger blocks give more XP (30 for a 1 kg block up to 60 for 10 kg) and more sand.

            Take the carpet back to the Shantay Pass. In Al Kharid, unnote one seaweed at the bank booth and burn it on the range for soda ash, then make a molten glass at the furnace from the soda ash and the sand. Drop the bucket, the rest of the seaweed stack, the waterskins and the desert clothes once you're out of the desert.

            The molten glass goes into the workshop's crafting table at the start of 1.4, where it upgrades the crafting table 1 to a crafting table 2; it is one slot until then.

            From here on, whenever a tier 4 or lower shooting star lands near your route, mine it. You need 2,000 stardust in total by the celestial ring in Chapter 2's ice gloves and Entrana block (2.1), and you can spread it out.

            Done when you are 43 Mining and hold the molten glass.

            Coins: about 570,000 gp · Slots: about 21/28 with the molten glass, the sickle and the eye of newt · Requirements: 20 Crafting and 25 Thieving for The Golem; 27 Crafting and 27 Magic for the ring of dueling; 35 Mining for sandstone

            Wiki references The Golem The Golem/Quick guide Ring of dueling Silver sickle Sandstone Drew Molten glass

            Lumbridge and Draynor: Recipe for Disaster, Lost City and Vampyre Slayer

            This block starts Recipe for Disaster with all four of the cook's ingredients in one hand-in, completes Cook's Assistant, The Ides of Milk and Vampyre Slayer, starts Lost City, and picks up the ghostspeak amulet Nature Spirit needs next. The three Varrock items it uses are collected at the start of the block, not carried from the Digsite.

            Gate in 10 Cooking for Recipe for Disaster.
            Carry in about 21/28, then the stake, the rotten tomato and the fruit blast: about 24/28.
            Exit Cook's Assistant, The Ides of Milk and Vampyre Slayer done; Recipe for Disaster and its first three subquests started; Lost City started; the ghostspeak amulet; about 560,000 gp.
            Carries forward the ghostspeak amulet (Nature Spirit), the spice and the charcoal (the goblin generals' slop).

            Varrock errands

            Break a Chronicle card. In Varrock, buy a beer in the Blue Moon Inn and give it to Dr Harlow for the stake Vampyre Slayer needs, buy a rotten tomato (1 gp) at the Pelters' Veg Stall by the pillory cages for the Lumbridge cook, and claim the free drink from the Varrock Museum curator with your Level 3 certificate: it is a fruit blast, and a fruit blast with ashes added is the dirty blast the cook asks for.

            The beer costs 2 gp. Dr Harlow sits in the Blue Moon Inn, so you can buy and hand it over without moving. The wiki warns that a premade fruit blast won't work for the cook; the curator's fruit blast is one of the sources it lists.

            UIM risk When the curator takes your certificate, pick the fruit blast before you do anything else: if you close the dialogue after he has the certificate and before you choose, the reward is lost.
            Done when you hold the stake, the rotten tomato and the fruit blast.

            Recipe for Disaster begins

            Cast Lumbridge Home Teleport. Buy a steel axe from Bob and a tinderbox from the general store (1 gp), chop a log, and burn it with the tinderbox for ashes; add the ashes to the fruit blast for the dirty blast.

            Complete Cook's Assistant (an egg from the chicken farm, a bucket of milk from the dairy cow and a pot of flour from the windmill); hand each ingredient to the cook as you get it, so they never take three slots at once. Talk to the cook again to start Recipe for Disaster: he wants an eye of newt, a greenman's ale, a rotten tomato and a dirty blast, and you give him all four now. The Culinaromancer's Chest in the cellar is now open to you; buy one spice from it for the goblin generals' slop in a later block.

            The egg is in the coop north-west of Farmer Fred's house. For the milk, take the empty bucket from the castle cellar or buy one at the general store, and use it on the dairy cow, the one wearing a cowbell, in the Lumbridge cow pasture. For the flour, pick a grain in the wheat field, take it to the top floor of Mill Lane Mill, put it in the hopper and pull the lever, then fill an empty pot (one lies on the kitchen table) from the flour bin on the ground floor. Cook's Assistant gives 300 Cooking XP. The chest appears only after you have first entered the dining hall and watched its cutscene; the spice costs about 300 gp there.

            In the dining room, inspect the frozen dwarf, the goblin generals and Pirate Pete to start Freeing the Mountain Dwarf, Freeing the Goblin generals and Freeing Pirate Pete, and talk to the cook about each so the recipes are learnt. Pirate Pete then waits for the glassblowing in Chapter 2: he needs a fishbowl, which takes 42 Crafting.

            The goblin generals' slop comes together at the Goblin Village later in the section: the cook there, Mudknuckles, takes the charcoal first, then wants bread, orange slices, a blue, black, green or purple dye, the spice, fishing bait and a bucket of water. That is why the spice and the charcoal ride with you from here.

            Done when Recipe for Disaster is started.

            The Ides of Milk, Lost City and the ghostspeak amulet

            Cassius, by the pond south of the windmill, starts The Ides of Milk. Gillie then gives a cowbell amulet and a 1,000 XP lamp for any combat skill: use the lamp on Prayer and drop the amulet.

            UIM risk Its bull, Brutus, is fought in an instance: his melee can't kill you during the quest, but his specials can and ignore protection prayers, so step two tiles sideways (or one back) when he snorts and two tiles sideways when he growls and charges, and leave by the south-west gate if it goes badly.

            Before the fight, the quest walks you between Gillie Groats in the cow field, her father Seth by the chicken coop (search the shelves on his house's southern wall for The Groats Principles) and Duke Horacio in the castle, and asks you to drink the milk sample once: it costs a point of Hitpoints but doesn't poison you. Brutus's specials start after his third melee hit and scale with your Hitpoints, so expect to lose a third of your health or more to a hit you fail to dodge, and eat before each one.

            UIM risk Items left on the ground in the instance are lost, so pick nothing up and drop nothing there.

            Walk to the adventurers' camp in the north-west corner of Lumbridge Swamp, just south of Draynor, and start Lost City by talking to the Warrior at the camp and chopping the tree that has only a "Chop" option (not "Chop down") with the steel axe to find Shamus, the leprechaun. Continue until you're told to go to Entrana; that trip is in the Kandarin block. Sell the steel axe at the Lumbridge general store on your way back: Entrana bans axes, and the bronze axe you need there drops inside.

            Ask the Warrior why he is camped there, then who Zanaris is and how he plans to find it: the last answer points you at the leprechaun's tree. Shamus tells you about the shed in the swamp and the dramen staff that opens it, and the dramen tree grows only in the Entrana Dungeon. The bronze axe comes from the level 25 zombies down there. The dramen tree itself needs 36 Woodcutting and the staff 31 Crafting, both of which the Entrana block's gate checks.

            If you dropped your ghostspeak amulet in 1.1, walk south to Father Urhney's house in the swamp and ask him for a new one; Nature Spirit needs it in the next block.

            Done when The Ides of Milk is complete, Lost City waits for Entrana and you hold the ghostspeak amulet.

            Vampyre Slayer

            Walk west to Draynor. Talk to Morgan in his house to start Vampyre Slayer and take the garlic from the cupboard upstairs. Walk north to Draynor Manor and kill Count Draynor in the basement with the stake and your hammer in your inventory and the garlic on you; the quest gives 3 quest points and 4,825 Attack XP. Pickpocket Martin the Master Gardener in the market for the Lumbridge & Draynor diary.

            Morgan's house is just north of Ned's. In the manor, take the large staircase on the east side down and open the coffin in the middle of the room to wake the Count. The garlic weakens him only when melee combat starts, and he regenerates far faster than most monsters; if the fight drags on he returns to his coffin at full health, so keep up a steady stream of Water Bolts. Without the hammer you can't drive the stake in, so check it is in your inventory before you open the coffin.

            Done when Vampyre Slayer is complete.

            Coins: about 560,000 gp · Slots: about 21/28 with the ghostspeak amulet, the spice and the charcoal · Requirements: 10 Cooking for Recipe for Disaster

            Wiki references Recipe for Disaster Cook's Assistant The Ides of Milk Lost City Vampyre Slayer Culinaromancer's Chest

            Priest in Peril, Morytania and the graceful outfit

            This block finishes Priest in Peril, opens Morytania, completes Nature Spirit, turns your marks of grace into the full graceful outfit on the Canifis Rooftop Course, and finishes with Shades of Mort'ton, once the marks and the saw no longer take slots. Priest in Peril has to come first, because Nature Spirit, Shades of Mort'ton and the Canifis course all require it. It has the section's only unplanned danger: Mort Myre and Mort'ton can kill you, and for a UIM that means a deathpile in a swamp. Take your best food, keep your Hitpoints high, and leave if a fight turns.

            Gate in 15 Herblore, 20 Crafting and 5 Firemaking for Shades of Mort'ton; 18 Crafting for Nature Spirit's sickle; 40 Agility for Canifis; about 30 marks of grace from the 1.2 rooftops.
            Carry in about 22/28 with the saw.
            Exit Priest in Peril, Nature Spirit and Shades of Mort'ton done; the full graceful outfit worn and a magic wardrobe built; about 62 Agility; about 535,000 gp.
            Carries forward the graceful outfit (worn, or stored whole in the magic wardrobe); the grimy marrentill (Bone Voyage).

            Fifty essence, a few at a time

            Break a Chronicle card and walk north to the Varrock estate agent's house in north-east Varrock: take the saw that spawns inside, for the magic wardrobe at the end of this block. Priest in Peril's Drezel wants 50 essence, and he accepts them in batches, so you never need 50 free slots. He takes rune and pure essence alike, as long as it is unnoted. Have Aubury teleport you to the rune essence mine; at 43 Mining you get pure essence. Fill your free slots (about six), leave through the portal, walk east to the Paterdomus temple and hand them over, and repeat until Drezel has all 50, which completes Priest in Peril. It takes about eight short trips. The reward includes a wolfbane dagger. Drop it: nothing in this chapter uses it, and Chapter 2 collects a new one from Drezel under the temple for its werewolf kill.

            At this stage Drezel is out of his cell and waits in the mausoleum under the temple: go down into the dungeon and through the gate in the north-east corner of the monument room to reach him. Since the 30 June 2026 update the monks of Zamorak in the temple are aggressive. None of them hits above 5, so they are a nuisance on eight round trips rather than a threat, but eat before your Hitpoints get low instead of pushing through.

            Done when Priest in Peril is complete.

            Nature Spirit

            Talk to Drezel to start Nature Spirit, and walk through the temple's back door into Morytania. He hands you three meat pies and three apple pies meant for Filliman; you never actually give them to him, so eat them whenever you need to. Enter the swamp through its gate: the wiki notes that Filliman doesn't appear if you come in another way. Head south along the western river to the Grotto Tree, on an island reached by jumping a broken bridge. The jump can fail for 1 to 7 damage, so never take it on low Hitpoints.

            In Mort Myre, do the quest with the ghostspeak amulet, the silver sickle you cast in Al Kharid and the druid pouch it gives you. The swamp itself takes 3 Hitpoints from you every two minutes. The ghasts in the swamp rot your food; bring more than you think. A ghast misses completely seven times in ten; when it does connect, it rots food in your inventory, and with no food left it hits you for up to 3 instead. Wine and most other alcoholic drinks can't be rotted.

            The steps that hold people up are in the grotto. Lift the washing bowl off the bench for the mirror and use it on Filliman, then search the Grotto Tree for his journal. The spell step uses Prayer points; if you run short, recharge at the altar on the temple's ground floor. Cast the spell next to a rotting log for a mushroom and keep the used spell: the mushroom goes on the light-brown stone, the used spell on the grey stone, and then you stand on the orange stone and tell Filliman you've solved it. If a ghast rots the mushroom on the way, Filliman gives you another spell.

            UIM risk Anything you drop before your last talk with the Nature Spirit is lost when the old grotto changes, so don't use its floor to make room.

            Once the druid pouch is filled, kill level 30 ghasts until one drops a grimy marrentill (about 1 in 21 kills): Bone Voyage's Potion of Sealegs needs it at the end of the section, and this is the closest source to your route. A ghast stays invisible until it attacks you or you use the filled pouch on it, which costs a charge each time. Refill the pouch by casting Bloom with the blessed sickle next to a log or bush (1 to 6 Prayer a cast) and filling it with the pears, fungi or stems that grow. At the end, restore your Prayer at the nature grotto's altar for the diary; it is an easy Morytania task, and it only counts when your Prayer is below full, which the Bloom casts take care of. Drop the ghostspeak amulet, the druid pouch and the blessed sickle: Father Urhney gives a new amulet whenever a later quest needs one, and Chapter 2 blesses a new sickle and collects a new pouch at the grotto before the Myreque quests.

            Done when Nature Spirit is complete.

            Canifis rooftops and the graceful outfit

            The Canifis Rooftop Course starts in Canifis and needs 40 Agility and Priest in Peril. You climb the tall tree just north of the Canifis bank and finish by jumping down off the north side of the bank, so each lap ends a few steps from the next one's start. A lap is 240 XP and takes about 44 seconds, roughly 19,700 XP an hour at best, and gives about 16 to 18 marks an hour: Canifis spawns a mark twice as often as most courses, and unlike the others its rate doesn't drop once you are 20 levels above it. You need 260 marks for the whole set and bring about 30, so this is about 230 more: roughly 13 to 14 hours of laps, which take you from about 53 to about 62 Agility. Your first lap also completes a medium Morytania diary task. Only one obstacle can fail, the third gap jump between the tanner and the general store, and it stops failing at 64 Agility; keep a few food for it. It doesn't have to be one sitting: if you would rather spread the laps out, run part of them now and the rest once the fairy rings open in 1.4 (code CKS lands next to Canifis).

            Take the marks to Grace in the Rogues' Den in Burthorpe, the same den where you collected the rogue outfit. Her shop is outside the maze, and the den outside the maze has no entry requirements, so you don't need an empty inventory this time. The pieces cost 35 marks (hood), 55 (top), 60 (legs), 30 (gloves), 40 (boots) and 40 (cape), 260 in all. Buy all six and wear them; the set bonus needs the whole outfit. With all six on, your run energy comes back 30% faster and the outfit takes 25 kg off your weight.

            From now on graceful is your travel outfit. It takes the head, body, legs, hands, feet and cape slots. The cape replaces the forestry kit in your cape slot: destroy the kit, since it has no drop option (the Friendly Forester in Draynor gives a new one if you ever want Forestry again). When a step swaps one or two of those slots for something else, such as climbing boots or a helmet, swap just those pieces and carry them until you put graceful back on. When a step needs most of them, such as armour for a fight or the rogue outfit, store the whole set.

            Store it in an oak magic wardrobe: unnote four oak planks at the Canifis bank, cast Teleport to House to Taverley and build the wardrobe in your costume room (42 Construction, 4 oak planks, a hammer and the saw), then drop the saw. A UIM can only take graceful back out of a magic wardrobe when the full set of the same colour is stored, so always put all six pieces in together, cape included. Each colour counts as a separate set, so don't recolour single pieces later.

            Done when you wear the full graceful outfit and the magic wardrobe is built.

            Shades of Mort'ton

            Break a Chronicle card and walk east through the Paterdomus temple and south to Mort'ton. Do Shades of Mort'ton there. The town has about 25 aggressive loar shades wandering its streets, so arrive with full Hitpoints. Do the whole quest on the world you are on. The wiki mentions world 377, the official Shades world, only as optional help for the temple repair, where other players speed it up; you don't need it, and it also warns that building materials can cost much more there because players buy them up. You have no herbs at this point (the Reward Cart's herbs come in 1.4), and you don't need to leave Morytania for them: search the smashed table in the south-westernmost house in Mort'ton for two grimy tarromins (it also gives a grimy rogue's purse; drop it). The quest starts in the same house, from the diary on its shelf. Empty vials spawn in the same house; fill them at the sink in the easternmost building, north-east of it. Clean the tarromin at 11 Herblore and the marrentill from the ghasts at 5. The wiki's item list is 1,000+ coins, two tarromin potions (unf) or two vials of water and two tarromin, two ashes, a tinderbox with logs or an axe, a hammer, at least one dose of serum 207, five swamp paste (the wiki suggests buying 10 to 20) and olive oil from Razmire's general store, with limestone bricks, timber beams and swamp paste from his builders' merchant; the loar remains come from five loar shades. The wiki recommends 11 free slots; with Nature Spirit's items, the marks and the saw gone you have about nine, which is enough if you buy Razmire's building materials in two rounds as the repair asks for them. The wiki also notes that a plain hammer can't rebuild the temple alone on an empty world, so buy the Flamtaer hammer from Razmire (13,000 gp) if the world is quiet. The quest needs 15 Herblore, 20 Crafting and 5 Firemaking and gives 2,000 Herblore and 2,000 Crafting XP. Drop every leftover Shades item at the end.

            Three things in the quest catch people out. Loar shade kills only count once Razmire, in the general store, has asked you for the remains, so use the serum on him first and kill the five shades after. When you give Ulsquire Shauncy, in the house south-east of Razmire, his serum and a shade's remains, take both dialogue options, about what he found in the remains and about the temple: if you skip either, the temple only tells you that you're not sure how to fix it. And the temple, on the small island north-east of the general store, needs at least 10% sanctity before you can make sacred oil from the olive oil, which you build up by repairing the walls. The wiki strongly recommends using your last serum 207 on the flame at 20% sanctity for serum 208: it cures Razmire and Ulsquire for good, and without it they can turn afflicted again before you finish. Burn the shade on one of the funeral pyres west of the general store, take your reward from the stone pillar beside it, and go back to Ulsquire to complete the quest. The quest also opens the Shades of Mort'ton minigame teleport, which 1.4 uses to reach the nature grotto for the magic secateurs.

            Don't chop a hollow tree for bark, fill the Morytania compost bin or buy seven raw chickens here; the bark and chickens belong to 1.4. Don't buy a raw shark for the Fremennik Trials yet either: Olaf's lyre, which needs it, is a 1.4 step.

            Done when Shades of Mort'ton is complete.

            Coins: about 535,000 gp · Slots: about 19/28 with the marrentill · Requirements: 15 Herblore, 20 Crafting and 5 Firemaking for Shades of Mort'ton; 18 Crafting for the sickle; 40 Agility and Priest in Peril for Canifis

            Wiki references Priest in Peril Nature Spirit Shades of Mort'ton Canifis Rooftop Course Graceful outfit Grace Oak magic wardrobe

            Taverley, Port Sarim and the Goblin Village: Excalibur and the slop

            This block gets Excalibur back for Merlin's Crystal, makes the goblin generals' slop and hands it in, and bakes Rohak's dwarven rock cake. Every ingredient is bought or caught on the loop it is used on, so the peak is the rock cake's eight items just before Rohak.

            Gate in Merlin's Crystal at its Excalibur step; the charcoal and spice; 33 Magic for Telekinetic Grab.
            Carry in about 19/28, 20 with the vial.
            Exit Excalibur; Freeing the Goblin generals done; the rock cake (handed in on the Lumbridge stop of the next block); the wine of Zamorak diary task; about 545,000 gp.
            Carries forward Excalibur (Merlin's Crystal, then the Black Knight Titan), the rock cake (the Mountain Dwarf), the vial (Bone Voyage).

            The Lady of the Lake and the willows

            Cast Teleport to House to Taverley. At Jatix's Herblore Shop, buy one vial for Bone Voyage's Potion of Sealegs at the end of the section (nothing nearer the barge sells one). Talk to the Lady of the Lake, on the lake peninsula south of the Taverley balloon, for Merlin's Crystal. She wants you to help a beggar before she returns Excalibur; that happens in Port Sarim. The Falador diary's willow task (chop and burn a willow log) waits for a visit when you carry an axe.

            Walk north to the Chaos Temple and cast Telekinetic Grab on the wine of Zamorak inside for the diary; it is a medium Falador task. Casting the spell turns the monks on the ground floor aggressive, so cast from where you can walk straight out. Picking it up by hand is only safe once every monk in the temple is dead, and they respawn two and a half minutes later. Drop the wine.

            Done when the Lady of the Lake step and the wine of Zamorak are done.

            Port Sarim: Excalibur

            Walk south through Falador. In the park, buy two woad leaves from Wyson (offer 20 gp to get two at once) for the blue dye. In the Rising Sun Inn, stamp the barcrawl card and pay Emily 200 gp to learn how to make Asgoldian ale; the ales themselves come later, right before Rohak.

            In Port Sarim, buy two pots of flour at Wydin's food store, an orange at Trader Stan's on the dock and a fishing bait at Gerrant's. In Rimmington, buy a bucket at the general store and fill it at the well, make two bread doughs from the flour and water, and bake them on the range in the house next to the general store. Walk back to Port Sarim and give a bread to the beggar outside Grum's gold shop for Merlin's Crystal. He turns out to be the Lady of the Lake, who hands you Excalibur. Read her dialogue to the end: if you close it early, you need another bread to repeat the step, and your second bread is the soggy bread for the slop, so you would have to bake a third. Keep it: the crystal and the Black Knight Titan both need it.

            Done when you hold Excalibur.

            The goblin generals' slop

            Walk north to Draynor and use the two woad leaves on Aggie for a blue dye (5 gp). Walk north to the Goblin Village and climb down the ladder in the building just south of the generals. Give Mudknuckles the charcoal, then make the ingredients: slice the orange with your knife and dye the slices blue, use the spice on the fishing bait, and use the bucket of water on the bread. The charcoal makes his cauldron explode in a short cutscene; it is part of the quest and does you no harm. Only blue, black, green or purple dye works on the slices; red, yellow, orange and pink don't. Talk to Mudknuckles again for the slop of compromise.

            Cast Lumbridge Home Teleport and give the slop to the goblin generals in the castle dining room to complete Freeing the Goblin generals (1 quest point and 1,000 Cooking, Farming and Crafting XP). While you're in the castle, buy a bucket of milk, a pot of flour and an egg from the Culinaromancer's Chest, take the bowl from the kitchen and fill it at the sink: those four are Rohak's cake ingredients.

            Done when the goblin generals are freed and you hold the cake ingredients.

            Rohak's rock cake

            Cast Falador Teleport and buy four Asgarnian ales in the Rising Sun Inn (or collect them free at the Toad and Chicken in Burthorpe, next to the tunnel), and use a coin on each to make Asgoldian ale. With the cake ingredients that is your fullest moment of the section, about 27 slots. Walk into the dwarven tunnel under White Wolf Mountain from Taverley. Give Rohak, the old dwarf, Asgoldian ales until he agrees to bake a dwarven rock cake, then talk to him three times; finish every dialogue, or you'll need new ales. Hand the ales over one after another without stopping: if he sobers up before the cake, you need more, and four is all you carry. Pay him 100 gp and give him the ingredients; he puts the cake on the table. Pick it up with your graceful gloves on so it doesn't burn you. It is still hot, so carry it to Ice Mountain and kill an icefiend while you hold it to cool it; dropping and re-picking it only cools it with ice gloves. The icefiends are level 13 and stand on Ice Mountain, west of the Edgeville Monastery. Never put the hot cake on the ground: the wiki warns that it will probably despawn, which costs you the ingredients and the ales again. Keep it for the dwarf in Lumbridge, which is on the next block's route.

            Done when you hold the cooled rock cake.

            Coins: about 545,000 gp · Slots: about 20/28 with Excalibur, the rock cake and the vial; about 27/28 just before Rohak · Requirements: 33 Magic for Telekinetic Grab

            Wiki references Merlin's Crystal Recipe for Disaster/Freeing the Goblin generals Recipe for Disaster/Freeing the Mountain Dwarf Rohak Lady of the Lake Wine of Zamorak

            Varrock, Lumbridge and Camelot: Merlin's Crystal and Elemental Workshop I

            This block finishes Merlin's Crystal and Elemental Workshop I, frees the Mountain Dwarf with the rock cake, starts Holy Grail, and clears the Seers' Village diary stops on the way. The black candle, the bat bones and the thread are all made or bought within a few minutes of being used.

            Gate in Excalibur and the rock cake; 20 Attack for Holy Grail; 30 Mining for coal.
            Carry in about 20/28.
            Exit Merlin's Crystal, Freeing the Mountain Dwarf and Elemental Workshop I done; Holy Grail started; three Seers' Village diary tasks; the rune sword; about 520,000 gp.
            Carries forward Excalibur and the rune sword (the Black Knight Titan), the holy table napkin (Holy Grail).

            Varrock and Lumbridge

            Break a Chronicle card. In the Champions' Guild, buy a rune sword (not the longsword) from Scavvo: it is your melee weapon from the Black Knight Titan until well into Chapter 2. Walk into Varrock, buy one thread from the Fancy Clothes Store (Elemental Workshop I's bellows), and read the inscription on the altar in the Zamorak temple in south-east Varrock: the words go on the black candle ritual. Tell Historian Minas about Priest in Peril and Vampyre Slayer for their kudos.

            Cast Lumbridge Home Teleport and give the rock cake to the frozen dwarf in the castle dining room to complete Freeing the Mountain Dwarf (1 quest point, 1,000 Slayer and 1,000 Cooking XP).

            Done when you hold the rune sword and the thread, the inscription is read and the Mountain Dwarf is freed.

            Merlin's Crystal

            Teleport to Camelot and walk to Catherby. Pick up the insect repellent from the table in Perdu's house, north of the Catherby bank, buy a bucket at the general store, and fill it with wax at the beehives in the enclosure at Catherby. Use the repellent on a hive before you take the wax. The candle maker turns the bucket of wax into a black candle; drop the repellent. He lives in the house west of the Catherby bank, and you have to ask him about black candles before he takes the wax. Hide in Arhein's crate again to reach Keep Le Faye and kill a giant bat for its bat bones, then leave the way you came. The giant bats fly around outside the keep, beyond its ground-floor doors, and the crates in Catherby are the only way back in if you leave before you have the bones.

            With the black candle, the bat bones and Excalibur, walk to the magical symbol just inside the fence on the north-east side of Camelot's castle garden. Light the black candle with your tinderbox, drop the bat bones in the circle (don't bury them), and recite the words from the Varrock altar, Snarthon Candtrick Termanto, to summon Thrantax, who frees Merlin. A wrong phrase makes Thrantax (level 92) attack you instead, and you need a new black candle to try again, so choose the words exactly. Smash the crystal in Camelot with Excalibur to complete Merlin's Crystal.

            Talk to King Arthur to start Holy Grail, and continue until you're sent to Entrana. Merlin is upstairs, by way of the staircase in the castle's south-east room, and appears once you walk into that room. Run to Galahad's house west of McGrubor's Wood, take the holy table napkin for Holy Grail, and have him make you a cup of tea for the diary. Tell him you're after an item from the Fisher Realm for the napkin, and ask whether he ever gets lonely for the tea. Holy Grail needs 20 Attack.

            Done when Merlin's Crystal is complete and Holy Grail waits for Entrana.

            Elemental Workshop I and Seers' Village

            Cross the log shortcut to the Coal Trucks and mine four coal (one of them also counts for the diary). In Seers' Village, start Elemental Workshop I from the bookcase in the house just south-west of the bank and follow it into the workshop, using your knife, hammer and thread: the costume needle works on the workshop's bellows only if you carry real thread. The bookcase gives a battered book; cut it with your knife for the key, which leaves a slashed book. Keep that slashed book: the shield can't be made without it, and if the workbench still refuses, the wiki's fix is to destroy the book and take a new one from the bookcase. The key opens the odd-looking wall in the northern building with the anvil icon, and stairs lead down from there. In the northern chamber, set the east water control first and then the west one, and pull the lever by the water wheel; in the eastern chamber, fix the bellows with the thread and a piece of leather and pull the lever beside them. The workshop's crates hold leather and a needle if you need them.

            Kill one of each elemental for the diary; the earth elemental drops the elemental ore. It is the one that wakes up when you mine an elemental rock in the western cavern (20 Mining); the elementals already walking about don't count for the quest. For the diary it is the other way round: only a roaming earth elemental counts, so kill one of those as well. The elementals hit hard for your level, so take your best food and use Water Bolt, which the fire elementals are weak to. Smelt one elemental bar at the workshop's furnace from the ore and the four coal, and make the elemental shield to finish the quest: 5,000 Crafting XP and 5,000 Smithing XP, which takes Crafting past 30. Before the furnace works, fill a stone bowl from the boxes north-east of the central hatch with lava from the southern trough and use it on the furnace; then use the ore on it, not the coal. If the furnace still refuses, check that both the water wheel and the bellows are running. The shield is made at the central workbench with the slashed book in your inventory. Elemental Workshop II, which needs a book from the Digsite, is a 1.4 quest.

            Play the church organ in Seers' Village for the diary, stamp the barcrawl card in the Forester's Arms, and buy one stew there for the diary. Talk to Sherlock east of Camelot.

            Done when Elemental Workshop I is complete and the Seers' stops are done.

            Coins: about 520,000 gp · Slots: about 21/28 with the rune sword, Excalibur, the napkin and the vial · Requirements: 20 Attack for Holy Grail; 30 Mining for coal

            Wiki references Merlin's Crystal Holy Grail Elemental Workshop I Recipe for Disaster/Freeing the Mountain Dwarf Thread Kandarin Diary

            Entrana, Lost City, Demon Slayer and the Black Knight Titan

            This block makes the section's second planned death so you can visit Entrana with nothing it bans, then finishes Lost City, Demon Slayer and Holy Grail's Black Knight Titan in one sweep south. Entrana's monks search you before the boat leaves and turn away most weapons and armour with combat bonuses, and that includes your staff, rune sword, Excalibur and pickaxe. Runes, a knife and food are allowed, and so are most jewellery, gloves and boots. Rather than check every item you own against the wiki's list, the guide takes a short kit that is plainly allowed.

            Gate in 31 Crafting and 36 Woodcutting for Lost City; Rune Mysteries for Enter the Abyss; a Falador Castle deathpile for Entrana.
            Carry in about 21/28.
            Exit Lost City and Demon Slayer done; Holy Grail past the Black Knight Titan, with the magic gold feather; Enter the Abyss started; the dramen staff and a spare branch; about 495,000 gp.
            Carries forward the dramen staff (Zanaris and the fairy rings), a dramen branch (Koschei), the magic gold feather, and one magic whistle (Holy Grail in 1.4).

            Planned death: Falador Castle again

            In Port Sarim, buy about 300 air runes and about 200 fire runes from Betty, because your staff of air can't come to Entrana and the tree spirit there is weak to fire. Make sure your rune pouch still holds law, nature and water runes, which with the air runes cast Falador Teleport, carry the chaos runes loose for Fire Bolt, keep your raw fish noted, and walk to Falador Castle. Don't cook food before a planned death: the wiki's UIM guide warns that un-noted food and potions disappear from a deathpile. This death destroys the Chronicle, as every death does; you buy it back in Draynor later in this block, and nothing before then uses it. Take off everything, attack a White Knight with auto-retaliate off, and die as you did before the Rogues' Den.

            You respawn in Lumbridge. Before you go back to the pile, start Enter the Abyss while you hold nothing worth losing: use the Soul Wars minigame teleport, step through the portal to Edgeville, walk north across the Wilderness ditch to the Mage of Zamorak at level 5, and talk to him to start Enter the Abyss. Walk back south out of the Wilderness and on to Falador. This is the only Wilderness step in the chapter, and you take it with an empty inventory on purpose.

            At your deathpile, pick up only the Entrana kit: the rune pouch, the air, fire and chaos runes, your knife and the noted raw fish. The pouch and the air runes already hold Falador Teleport. Unnote the fish at the Falador east bank booth (you get one per free slot), cook a dozen of them on the range just east of that bank and drop the extra raw ones, then carry the noted rest along; it costs one slot. Leave everything else.

            Done when your pile is in the courtyard.

            Entrana

            Walk to Port Sarim and take the monks' boat to Entrana. Talk to the High Priest for Holy Grail. He is in the island's church; after him, an old crone appears and tells you the Fisher King is in trouble, which points you to the magic whistles later in this block. The dungeon is in the north-west of the island: walk east around the church and north over the bridge, and the cave monk stands just west of it. Talk to him and climb down. Your Prayer drops to almost 0 inside and there is no way back up the ladder, so you leave by teleport (the magic door to the east leads to level 32 Wilderness; don't use it). Kill level 25 zombies until one drops a bronze axe (50/128 a kill); the mushrooms against the west wall give you a safespot. Pass the greater demons and chop the Dramen tree; the tree spirit (level 101) appears. Chop the tree from the south, then stand between the three mushrooms to its south-west: that safespots the spirit. It has a 35% fire weakness, so cast Fire Bolt (3 air, 4 fire and 1 chaos rune a cast); Water Bolt works too, more slowly. The kill only counts if at least some of the damage is yours. Chop the tree for two dramen branches and drop the axe: one becomes the staff that finishes Lost City, and one goes into Koschei's fight. Chapter 2 takes its own branch for Sir Amik Varze on its Entrana trip. Leave both branches uncut for now: the staff waits for Lumbridge, and Koschei's branch has to go into his arena as a branch.

            Cast Falador Teleport and pick up the rest of your deathpile. From death to pile should take about 35 minutes. Drop the leftover fire runes.

            Done when you hold the dramen branches and your pile is collected.

            Lost City

            Cast Lumbridge Home Teleport. Use your knife on a dramen branch to make a dramen staff (31 Crafting). Walk to the shed in the Lumbridge Swamp, wield the staff and walk in to reach Zanaris and complete Lost City: 3 quest points, 1,500 Woodcutting and 1,500 Crafting XP. The shed is in the middle of the swamp; you open its door with the staff wielded. Lost City also lets you buy and wield the dragon longsword and dragon dagger from 60 Attack, and the dramen staff is your key to the fairy rings from 1.4 on. Wield your staff of air again and drop the leftover air and fire runes.

            Done when Lost City is complete.

            Demon Slayer and the magic whistles

            Wizard Traiborn wants 25 bones for his part of Demon Slayer, and he takes them in batches, so a UIM never carries all 25. Walk to the Wizards' Tower and talk to Traiborn in the tower to agree to help. He is on the first floor; ask him whether he has any keys knocking around. Walk to the chicken farm north-west of Lumbridge, kill chickens for as many bones as you have free slots (about five), and walk back to hand them in; five trips do it. He gives you the first of the three silver keys. If you die while you hold his key, he wants another 25 bones, so take no risks until all three keys are with Sir Prysin.

            Walk north to Draynor. In the market, buy the Chronicle back from Diango for 300 gp (its teleport charges come back with it). In Draynor Manor, take the two magic whistles for Holy Grail; the table only gives them while the holy table napkin is in your inventory, and that is the napkin's only job, so drop it now. They are on the top floor, in the room south of Professor Oddenstein. If you leave them on the ground they despawn, but they come back when you walk into the room again.

            Break a Chronicle card. In the palace, climb to Captain Rovin on the top floor for the second key. He is in the north-west corner, two flights up; tell him Sir Prysin sent you and promise not to let Prysin kill the demon. Buy a bucket at the general store, fill it, pour it down the drain outside the palace kitchen, then climb into the Varrock Sewers and pick up the key that washed out below it. The drain is just north of the kitchen doorway, and the water is not optional: the key doesn't appear until you pour it. The manhole into the sewers is south of the palace's east wall; head north-west to the stream and examine the muddy patch beside the pile of skeletons. Give all three keys to Sir Prysin, in the south-west part of the palace's ground floor, for Silverlight. Walk to the stone circle south of Varrock and fight Delrith (level 27) in melee with Silverlight wielded; when he is down, say the incantation Gypsy Aris gave you to complete Demon Slayer. Pick the words carefully: a wrong incantation puts him back to full health, and Aris repeats it if you have forgotten. If you take too long, Delrith vanishes and you have to walk back into the circle. Tell Historian Minas about it and about Shield of Arrav (use its lamp on Herblore), then drop Silverlight and the bucket: The Shadow of the Storm needs Silverlight in 1.4, and the wiki says Sir Prysin sells a replacement for 500 coins until that quest is done, which is cheaper than a slot for the rest of the section.

            Done when Demon Slayer is complete and you hold the whistles and the Chronicle.

            Karamja and the Black Knight Titan

            Cast Falador Teleport, walk to Port Sarim and charter a ship to Brimhaven. Stamp the barcrawl card in the Dead Man's Chest. Take the rope swing to Moss Giant Island and back for the Karamja diary.

            At the gold rocks on Karamja's north-west peninsula (the North Brimhaven mine), mine a gold ore for the diary and drop it. The scorpions around the mine are poisonous, so don't linger among them.
