Blow a magic whistle under the guard tower just north of them to reach the Fisher Realm's entrance for Holy Grail, and fight the Black Knight Titan (level 120) with your rune sword. He has 142 Hitpoints, hits with crush for up to 15 and never moves from his tile, and his Magic and Ranged defence are so high that only melee works. You can flinch him with the sword for the whole fight, but the final blow must come from Excalibur, so swap when he's nearly dead; around a tenth of his health is the point the wiki gives. If anything else lands the killing hit, he heals to full and you start again. Then cross the bridge, follow the river south to the fisherman and ask how to get into the castle; pick up the Grail bell south-west of him and ring it in front of the bricks; take the western stairs and give the Fisher King both options, "I seek the Holy Grail" and "You don't look too well". Don't blow the whistle before both, or you fight the Titan again. Drop Excalibur afterwards: nothing else needs it.

            Walk east to Musa Point, stamp the barcrawl card in the Karamja Spirits bar, and take the boat to Port Sarim. Teleport to Camelot and talk to King Arthur for the magic gold feather; keep it and one whistle, and drop the second whistle. Holy Grail finishes in 1.4, through Sir Percival in the Goblin Village and the BJR fairy ring. There the feather guides you to the sacks in the east house, and Percival takes your whistle.

            Done when the Black Knight Titan is dead and you hold the magic gold feather.

            Coins: about 495,000 gp · Slots: about 23/28 with the dramen staff and branch, the feather and one whistle · Requirements: 31 Crafting and 36 Woodcutting for Lost City; Rune Mysteries for Enter the Abyss

            Wiki references Entrana Lost City Enter the Abyss Holy Grail Black Knight Titan Demon Slayer Silverlight Ultimate Ironman Guide/Quests

            Rellekka: the Fremennik Trials, Peer the Seer and Koschei

            This block takes the Fremennik Trials as far as they go before Olaf's lyre and Manni's keg, which wait for 1.4, and clears the Fremennik diary tasks that don't need the Trials finished. It has the section's third planned death, because Peer the Seer's task needs an empty inventory and nothing worn, and because Koschei's arena bans weapons, armour and runes. The trial items come and go in Rellekka itself.

            Gate in a planned death at the Rellekka rock crabs.
            Carry in about 23/28.
            Exit the Fremennik Trials up to the lyre and keg; the Fremennik diary tasks that don't need the Trials finished; about 485,000 gp.
            Carries forward nothing new.

            The Fremennik Trials

            Use the Barbarian Assault minigame teleport and walk north to Rellekka. Talk to Brundt in the longhall to start The Fremennik Trials, then follow the wiki quick guide's order for the votes that don't need Peer, Koschei, Olaf or Manni: each trial hands you a single-use item that goes straight to the next person, so hand each over before you take the next. Olaf's lyre needs 40 Woodcutting for the swaying tree's branch, 40 Crafting to cut it and 25 Fletching to string it with golden wool, and Manni's vote needs a low alcohol keg from Seers' Village, so both wait for 1.4, along with Lalli's golden fleece for the lyre (he trades it for a stew that needs Askeladden's pet rock).

            UIM risk Sigmund's chain is the long one, and the wiki warns that losing any of its items sends you back to Askeladden to start it again, so don't drop anything mid-chain to make room.
            Done when the votes you can get now are done, except Peer's and Thorvald's.

            Planned death: the Rellekka rock crabs

            Make sure you have a knife and the dramen branch somewhere you can pick them out of a pile; you don't need food for Koschei (see below). Walk to the rock crabs on the coast north of Rellekka, take everything off, turn auto-retaliate off, and stand among the rocks until the crabs wake. The death destroys the Chronicle; you buy it back in Draynor at the start of the next block, before you next need it. Each crab hits for 1 at most, every 2.4 seconds, but several at once wear down an unarmoured player within a few minutes. They turn tolerant after ten minutes; if they stop attacking, walk about halfway back towards the village entrance to reset them and come back.

            You respawn in Lumbridge. Use the Barbarian Assault minigame teleport and walk north to Rellekka.

            Done when your pile is placed.

            Peer the Seer and Koschei

            With nothing worn and nothing in your inventory, do Peer the Seer's task in his house for his vote. For other accounts Peer offers to bank everything you hold; a UIM has no bank for him to use, which is why the pile comes first. His riddle's answer is one of Mind, Tree, Life, Fire, Time or Wind. Inside, search the south-west cupboard (bucket), the unicorn head (red disk), the bull head (wooden disk), the southern chest (jug) and the northern bookcase (red herring). Use the red herring on the range and the sticky red goop on the wooden disk, go down the east trapdoor and put both red disks on the abstract mural; upstairs, fill the bucket at the tap and pour between the bucket and the jug until you have 4/5 of a bucket, and put it on the northern chest's scale. Fill the vase, put the lid on, use it on the frozen table, use the frozen key on the range, and leave by the eastern trapdoor.

            Walk back to the beach and pick up only the knife and the dramen branch. Weapons and armour aren't allowed in Koschei's arena and a rune pouch only if it's empty, which is why the rest stays in the pile. In the longhall, talk to Thorvald and enter the arena below. When Koschei appears, cut the dramen branch into a dramen staff with the knife inside the arena, wield it on the aggressive style and fight all four forms. The first three hit for up to 3, 5 and 7; the fourth has 255 Hitpoints and hits every tick for 1 at most. The wiki lists every death in this fight as safe, and dying to (or beating) the fourth form is what earns Thorvald's vote, so you don't need food. A cutscene ends the fight just before the fourth form would kill you, and since 2020 a death in the first three puts you back outside the room. Leaving after the third fight resets your progress, so don't climb the ladder. Don't stand in a safespot for long either: if a phase lasts ten minutes, Koschei disappears and the fight resets. Items can't be dropped inside the arena.

            Pick up the rest of the pile and drop the second dramen staff; you already carry one.

            Done when Peer and Koschei are done.

            The Fremennik diary

            For the Fremennik diary, take Jarvald's boat to Waterbirth Island and pick up five snape grass (you can pick one up and drop it again). Until the Trials are complete, Jarvald charges 1,000 gp for the trip. Three other Rellekka tasks look as if they belong here but need The Fremennik Trials completed, not just started: browsing Yrsa's Accoutrements (her shoe store is gone; browsing the clothes shop counts), stealing from the fish stall (medium, 42 Thieving) and mining coal in the Rellekka mine (medium, 30 Mining). They wait for 1.4, straight after the Trials end. The tiara task and the oak log wait for a visit when you carry a tiara mould and an axe. The tiara has to be made from scratch in Rellekka, silver ore from the Rellekka mine smelted and cast at the furnace in the westernmost building, and the oaks grow near the Rellekka house portal. Askeladden's pet rock doesn't complete the medium pet rock task: that one is picking the rock up from a menagerie in your house (37 Construction, a 30,000 gp room and four oak planks), so it waits. When you do it, you place the rock on the pet house and then pick it up off the floor, outside building mode.

            Done when only the lyre, the keg and the golden fleece remain for the Trials.

            Coins: about 485,000 gp · Slots: about 22/28 · Requirements: none beyond the planned death

            Wiki references The Fremennik Trials Koschei the deathless Peer the Seer Rock crab Fremennik Diary

            Bone Voyage

            This block completes Bone Voyage, which opens Fossil Island, and moves your house to Hosidius, where 1.4 starts. Its items are a chain: the sawmill proposal becomes the agreement, and the lucky charm and the Potion of Sealegs go to the barge crew the same hour you make them. The Museum Camp, the bird houses and the Reward Cart's herb batch open 1.4, once this block's long carries are gone.

            Gate in 100 kudos and The Dig Site for Bone Voyage; the grimy marrentill and the vial.
            Carry in about 22/28.
            Exit Bone Voyage done; the house in Hosidius; about 455,000 gp.
            Carries forward nothing new.

            Varrock and Kourend

            Cast Falador Teleport and walk to Draynor: buy the Chronicle back from Diango in the market (the Rellekka death destroyed it), then break a Chronicle card. In the Varrock Museum, talk to Curator Haig Halen to start Bone Voyage (it needs 100 kudos and The Dig Site; your quests have taken you past 100). Go to the barge foreman north of the Digsite, and take the sawmill proposal he sends you for from the sawmill operator at the Lumber Yard east of Varrock. The foreman needs redwood planks for the barge, and the proposal and the agreement are what get them cut.

            Cast Falador Teleport and walk south to Port Sarim. Ask Veos to take you to Port Piscarilius. Walk to the Woodcutting Guild in Hosidius, give the sawmill proposal to the sawmill operator there and take the sawmill agreement back. The guild's entrance needs 60 Woodcutting, which you have had since Wintertodt. Pay the Hosidius estate agent 8,750 gp to move your house to Hosidius: 1.4 starts at the Tithe Farm next door. Ask Veos to take you back to Port Sarim.

            Done when you hold the sawmill agreement and your house is in Hosidius.

            The barge and the Potion of Sealegs

            Break a Chronicle card, walk to the Lumber Yard and give the Varrock sawmill operator the sawmill agreement, then go to the barge north of the Digsite, board it and talk to the Lead Navigator. He calls the voyage cursed and won't sail until his crew feels safe. He sends you to the Rusty Anchor Inn in Port Sarim: cast Falador Teleport and walk south to talk to Jack Seagull ("Ever made any cursed voyages?"), who suggests a lucky charm, while Ahab cuts in with a Potion of Sealegs. Stamp the barcrawl card in the Rusty Anchor while you're there.

            Break a Chronicle card and take the quetzal from Regulus Cento to Civitas illa Fortis; buy two vodka at The Flaming Arrow, which the wiki lists among vodka's sellers, and fly back. Go back to the barge, board it through the barge guard and talk to the Lead Navigator again; he wants the potion, and the Junior Navigator wants the charm, which is why you make both. Then walk west, north and east to the limestone mine and ask the Odd Old Man for a lucky charm ("I'm making a cursed voyage"; a bone charm). He lives in the hills beside the mine, in northern Silvarea, north-east of Varrock. Clean the grimy marrentill (5 Herblore), fill your vial at a fountain and make a marrentill potion (unf), and give the Apothecary in Varrock the potion and the two vodka ("Talk about something else", then "Talk about Bone Voyage", twice) for the Potion of Sealegs. His shop is south-west of Varrock Square. At the barge give the bone charm to the Junior Navigator and the potion to the Lead Navigator, then steer the barge to Fossil Island (when the top arrow tilts left click the right arrow, and the other way round) to complete Bone Voyage. The potion knocks out everyone but the Junior Navigator, so the steering is yours. Keep the sails fully out for speed; if you drift too far or take too long, the barge gets lost and the crew wakes, and you start the crossing again by talking to the Junior Navigator.

            Done when Bone Voyage is complete.

            Coins: about 455,000 gp · Slots: about 20/28 · Requirements: 100 kudos and The Dig Site for Bone Voyage

            Wiki references Bone Voyage Vodka Veos Estate agent

            End of section 1.3

            You finish 1.3 on Fossil Island's barge landing with Bone Voyage done, about 80 Thieving, 46 Construction, 43 Mining, about 60 Magic, a rune pouch, Bones to Peaches, a costume room holding the rogue and angler outfits, the full graceful outfit and about 62 Agility from Canifis, and about 455,000 gp. The Museum Camp, the bird houses and the Reward Cart's herb batch open 1.4, which starts in Hosidius, where your house now stands. Before you leave the island, check that the chaos runes and the mud rune are still in your inventory: 1.4's first block uses both.

In progress — The Fremennik Trials (Olaf's lyre, Manni's keg and the golden fleece left), Rogue Trader (runes done), Alfred Grimhand's Barcrawl, Dwarf Cannon, The Grand Tree, Recipe for Disaster (Pirate Pete), Holy Grail, Enter the Abyss, Dragon Slayer I, Rag and Bone Man I, Romeo & Juliet, A Porcine of Interest, Below Ice Mountain, Prince Ali Rescue, Family Crest. Moved whole to 1.4, so nothing for them is carried here — The Shadow of the Storm, Jungle Potion and Tai Bwo Wannai Trio, Creature of Fenkenstrain, Mountain Daughter, The Lost Tribe, Fairytale I, The Giant Dwarf and Forgettable Tale of a Drunken Dwarf, Elemental Workshop II, Observatory Quest and The Depths of Despair. Rag and Bone Man I's bones wait for Chapter 2.

            1.4: 62 Farming, Desert Treasure and the Chapter 2 money

            This section is a long questing loop that ends with 62 Farming from the Tithe Farm, Desert Treasure and 43 Prayer, with blackjacking paying for Chapter 2. The rest doesn't:

            • Herblore comes first. Eadgar's Ruse and the herb potions want Herblore before the Tithe Farm's seed box exists, so the Reward Cart's remaining rolls are split: its herbs now (batch 2), everything else after the Tithe Farm (batch 3).
            • The earth orbs are gone. They need 1,600 molten glass, a looting bag and repeated Wilderness deaths, the three things a UIM handles worst. Chapter 2's money comes from blackjacking Menaphite thugs from about 80 to 85 Thieving in the rogue outfit instead, a level target that the wiki's rates turn into about 1.1 million coins. Stealing artefacts pays too little (500 to 1,000 coins each) to fund a chapter.
            • Every Wilderness or Abyss step is grouped into planned deathpile trips, so you never carry your inventory past the ditch.
            • Stockpiles become single items. Don't buy four inventories of gnome food, 45 lizardkickers, 26 garden pies, 25 vial packs, a full Slayer kit, 14 watering cans and 104 pieces of jewellery here. A UIM buys each where it's used, and keeps three or four teleport jewellery pieces at a time.

            This section's planned deathpiles are the Death Plateau hand-in, the Al Kharid mine pile for Sleeping Giants, the Death Plateau path pile for Eadgar's Ruse, the Edgeville Monastery pile that serves the Abyss trip and every Wilderness dragon trip, and the Falador Castle pile for Temple of Ikov and Desert Treasure's trip to Entrana. Each is written into its block, and each destroys the Chronicle, which Diango sells back for 300 gp with its charges. Every block's slot count was worked out item by item against the kit you carry, and anything that failed that test was moved to Chapter 2; the list is at the end of the chapter.

            The Grand Tree and The Eyes of Glouphrie

            Section 1.3 left you on Fossil Island with the chaos runes and the mud rune from the Mage Training Arena still in your bag. Both are for this block, so it comes first: The Grand Tree's black demon is a Fire Bolt fight, and The Eyes of Glouphrie needs the mud rune, which no shop sells. Doing them now means neither stack is carried through the section. The Grand Tree also opens the gnome gliders, which Chapter 2 uses.

            Gate in 25 Agility for The Grand Tree; The Grand Tree, 5 Construction and 46 Magic for The Eyes of Glouphrie; 35 Magic for Fire Bolt.
            Carry in about 20/28.
            Exit The Grand Tree and The Eyes of Glouphrie done; the gnome gliders open; about 452,000 gp; about 18/28.
            Carries forward nothing new. The chaos runes, the mud rune and the costume needle are gone.

            Taverley and Varrock

            Take the barge back to the Digsite. Drop the costume needle: its last job in this chapter, the diary coif, is sewn later with a plain needle. Cast Falador Teleport, walk to Taverley and buy a pestle and mortar at Jatix's Herblore Shop. Cast Varrock Teleport, buy about 200 fire runes and, if your chaos stack is under 150, more chaos runes from Aubury, and take the spirit tree at the Grand Exchange to the Tree Gnome Stronghold.

            Done when you are in the Stronghold with the pestle and mortar and the runes.

            The Grand Tree

            Talk to King Narnode Shareen at the foot of the Grand Tree with two free slots: he gives you the translation book and the bark sample again. Hazelmere lives on the peninsula east of Yanille, and the fairy rings are not open yet, so use the ring of dueling's Castle Wars teleport, walk east through Yanille and on to his house. He takes the bark sample and gives you a scroll.

            Cast Varrock Teleport, ride the spirit tree back and follow the wiki's steps:

            1. Give the king Hazelmere's message, then talk to Glough in his tree house south-east of the Grand Tree, and let him have you arrested.
            2. Talk to Charlie on the top floor of the Grand Tree until the conversation ends, then search the cupboard in Glough's house for his journal.
            3. The king sends you out by the glider on top of the tree. It crashes on Karamja: run east past the level 53 jogres to the shipyard gate, tell the worker Glough sent you and give the password Ka-Lu-Min.
            4. Talk to the foreman on the southern dock for the lumber order; his three questions are answered "Sadly his wife is no longer with us!", "He loves worm holes." and "Anita."
            5. Cast Varrock Teleport and ride the spirit tree back. The king doesn't believe the order. Talk to Charlie again, get Glough's key from Anita (up the stairs west of the swamp, north-west of the Grand Tree), open Glough's chest for the invasion plans and take them to the king.
            6. He gives you four stick letters (four free slots). In the watchtower in Glough's house, put them on the pillars to spell TUZO, and go down the trapdoor.
            7. After the cutscene, run to the tile directly north of the ladder: the black demon (level 172) gets stuck behind the rocks there. Kill it with Fire Bolt. It despawns after 10 minutes; if that happens, leave by the central ladder and come back through the trapdoor.
            8. Follow the passage to the king, search the roots for the last daconia rock and talk to him to finish: 5 quest points, 18,400 Attack, 7,900 Agility and 2,150 Magic XP.

            A few of these steps go wrong in known ways. The glider is next to the cells at the top of the tree, and it lands just west of the shipyard; the jogres around the crash site are aggressive, which is why you run rather than fight. In the trapdoor's cutscene, let Glough's conversation play out without clicking anything else, or you have to sit through it again. The black demon hits for up to 16, so if it reaches you before you get to the tile, step back onto it and eat. When you search the roots, keep a free slot for the daconia rock; if you lose it, it comes back in the root where you first found it. The watchtower in step 6 is reached up the tree on the east side of the first floor of Glough's house, and it needs the 25 Agility in the gate line.

            Drop whatever chaos runes are left. The next Fire Bolt fight is seven blocks away, and Aubury is on the way to it.

            Done when The Grand Tree is complete.

            The Eyes of Glouphrie

            Talk to Brimstail in his cave in the south-west of the Stronghold to start The Eyes of Glouphrie, inspect the singing bowl in the west of the cave, try Oaknock's machine to the north and talk to Brimstail again. He sends you to Hazelmere: ring of dueling to Castle Wars and the same walk as before. Watch the cutscenes upstairs until you receive the violet pentagon. Let his dialogue run to the very end: the wiki warns that skipping part of it leaves him unable to be understood later in the quest.

            The quest also wants a saw, an oak log and a maple log, and this is the moment to get them. Cast Camelot Teleport and chop one maple log in Seers' Village. Cast Varrock Teleport, walk to the Lumber Yard for a saw (13 gp) and an oak log, buy a bucket at the general store, and ride the spirit tree back. Outside Brimstail's cave, use your knife on the evergreen-shaped tree with the bucket in your inventory for a bucket of sap.

            Grind the mud rune with the pestle and mortar and add it to the sap for magic glue, and click Oaknock's machine twice to repair it. Grind only the mud rune: pure essence ground the same way gives weird gloop, which is a dead end. Tell Brimstail, and talk to him twice more for the discs. From here the quest is a counting puzzle, and it is where your free slots go (the wiki suggests eight): unlock the front panel with a disc whose value matches the green number, fill the three sections inside the machine with discs that add up to each target, exchanging discs at the machine when you need other values, and ask Brimstail for more discs whenever you run out; dropping discs you can't use and asking again is how you keep the puzzle inside your free slots. Drop the pestle and mortar first to make room. The yellow circle never turns up, so use a red triangle whenever you need a 3. Brimstail doesn't hand out circles or red shapes, so low values come from the exchanger, which takes at most 35 points at a time. Every slot has to be filled before the machine opens; if a set doesn't fit, split a high-value disc into smaller ones of the same total at the exchanger.

            When the machine runs, kill the six evil creatures it reveals, waiting for each death animation: by Brimstail, outside the main gate (kill it inside the walls), by the agility course entrance, by King Narnode, at the top of the Grand Tree by the prison, and by the tortoises north-west of the tree. Don't climb a ladder straight after a kill, or it may not count; if your count goes wrong, the last creature appears at the Stronghold's entrance. Talk to King Narnode to finish: 2 quest points, 12,000 Magic, 2,500 Woodcutting, 6,000 Runecraft and 250 Construction XP, and a crystal saw seed.

            Drop the seed, the saw, the leftover logs and the bucket. Chapter 2 uses the crystal saw once, for one piece of furniture, and collects it then; carrying it from here would cost a slot for two chapters.

            Take the spirit tree to the Grand Exchange and walk to the Digsite barge for the next block.

            Done when The Eyes of Glouphrie is complete.

            Coins: about 452,000 gp · Slots: about 18/28 at the end; about 27/28 with the puzzle discs · Requirements: 25 Agility for The Grand Tree; 46 Magic and 5 Construction for The Eyes of Glouphrie

            Wiki references The Grand Tree The Eyes of Glouphrie Fire Bolt Spirit tree

            Fossil Island and the Hosidius workshop

            Take the barge from the Digsite back to Fossil Island. This block opens the island's mushtrees, builds the workshop that turns your steel bars into clockworks, trains Herblore on the Reward Cart's first herbs, starts the compost line, and places your first four bird houses. It is a block of building and processing, so it starts with your kit at its lightest.

            Gate in Bone Voyage; 22 Mining for volcanic ash; 25 Construction for the crafting table 2; 8 Crafting for clockworks and 5 for bird houses; 22 Herblore for compost potions; 25 Thieving for the fruit stall and 38 for master farmers.
            Carry in about 18/28.
            Exit the mushtrees; a workshop with a crafting table 2; 27 Herblore; the compost line started; four bird houses on Fossil Island; about 430,000 gp; about 21/28.
            Carries forward the rune axe, the ranarr (Eadgar's Ruse), the golovanova fruit top (the botanical pie), the barley seeds, the noted plank and oak plank stacks (the Museum Camp, Mountain Daughter and the Observatory), fire runes.

            Fossil Island

            In the Museum Camp's general store, buy a chisel (1 gp) and an empty bucket pack (650 gp), and open the pack: it gives 100 noted buckets in one slot. The camp is just east of the barge landing. Climb the House on the Hill and use its mushtree once, then walk to the Verdant Valley mushtree (south of the camp) and the Mushroom Meadow one (north-west of the Mushroom Forest) and try each: a mushtree only works once you've found it. The Sticky Swamp one, inside the Tar Swamp, isn't needed. Reaching it takes an axe and a rake to clear the way, and a level 132 tar monster lives in that swamp.

            Mine about 150 volcanic ash at the volcano in the north-east (22 Mining). It stacks, and it is the "ash" in this block's compost potions and ultracompost. Each pile gives one ash at 22 Mining and one more for every 15 levels above that, so at 43 Mining you get two a pile.

            Cast Teleport to House.

            Done when you hold the chisel, the noted buckets and the ash, and the three mushtrees work.

            The workshop

            Talk to Lord Kandur Hosidius in his house to start The Depths of Despair (18 Agility), and to Chief Farmer Phabelle at the vinery as he asks; the quest's library and cave steps come in the Kourend blocks. At the Hosidius allotment just north of your house, hand the noted buckets to the tool leprechaun, who stores them, and fill the compost bin with 15 compostable items (weeds from the patches, or produce from the Hosidius stalls); close it so it rots while you work.

            Walk to the Woodcutting Guild. In Perry's Chop-chop Shop by the entrance, buy a rune axe (41 Woodcutting to use): you dropped your steel axe in 1.3, and from here the bird house logs, Mountain Daughter, Sleeping Giants and 70 Woodcutting all need an axe. Buy a saw from the sawmill operator (13 gp), unnote four oak planks at the guild's bank chest, and teleport to your house. Build a workshop (level 15, 10,000 gp), build a crafting table 1 in it from the four oak planks (16 Construction), and upgrade it to a crafting table 2 with your molten glass (25 Construction). Drop the saw. Keep the steel bars noted for now: the clockworks come at the end of the block, so they don't take four slots during the potion loads.

            Done when the crafting table 2 is built.

            Herblore from the Reward Cart, batch 2

            Eadgar's Ruse needs 31 Herblore (a botanical pie boosts 4). You are at about 22. Walk to the Wintertodt camp, search the Reward Cart until you hold about 60 grimy harralander and one grimy ranarr (the number of searches that takes is an estimate and varies per account; it is the largest use of the cart's remaining rolls); drop everything else, including the other herbs and the seeds, which come back in batch 3 once you have the seed box. The ranarr is for Eadgar's Ruse's troll potion: the potion made from it needs 30 Herblore and the troll potion 31, so you make both later with the botanical pie.

            Back in Hosidius, buy vials of water from Horace's general store and make compost potions: unnote a few harralanders at a bank, clean them, make harralander potions (unf) and add volcanic ash (22 Herblore, 60 XP each). With your kit, the noted harralanders and the ranarr you have about eight free slots, so work in loads of three or four: three herbs and three vials, then the ash. Drop each finished potion except one. One dose turns a whole bin of compost into supercompost, so one potion is all the bin needs. About 60 compost potions take you from 22 to 27 Herblore; stop at 27.

            Done when you are 27 Herblore with one compost potion and the ranarr.

            Ultracompost

            When the bin has rotted, open it and use the compost potion on it for supercompost, then use 25 volcanic ash on it for ultracompost. Take buckets back from the leprechaun a few at a time (he returns them unnoted), fill them from the bin and store the ultracompost with him, until the bin is empty. Drop the rest of the ash and the empty potion vial: the bird runs pass the volcano whenever you need more ash, and you make the next compost potion when the next batch starts.

            Done when the ultracompost is with the leprechaun.

            Clockworks, bird houses and seeds

            Walk to the Woodcutting Guild, unnote your steel bars at the bank chest (you get one per free slot, so five with room to spare) and drop one: four clockworks are all the bird runs need, because dismantling a full bird house gives its clockwork back. Chop four logs from the guild's normal trees. Teleport to your house and make the four clockworks at the crafting table (8 Crafting, one steel bar each). You are about 9 Hunter from the museum quiz, so your first houses are regular ones (5 Hunter, 5 Crafting); oak needs 14 Hunter. Use a clockwork on each log with the hammer and chisel in your inventory to make four bird houses.

            Then pickpocket the two master farmers in Hosidius (38 Thieving) until you have about 50 low-level seeds and four barley seeds. A failed pickpocket stuns you for five seconds and hits for 3, so eat now and then on a long run. Keep the two or three commonest types (potato, onion and cabbage seeds come most often) and drop the rest as they appear, because each seed type is a slot of its own. A bird house takes ten low-level seeds of any mix, and the barley seeds are for Forgettable Tale of a Drunken Dwarf's brew. Last, steal one golovanova fruit top from a Hosidius fruit stall (25 Thieving): it is the only source, and it becomes the botanical pie for Eadgar's Ruse. It comes about twice in a hundred steals, so expect to stand at the stall for a while.

            Done when you hold four bird houses, the seeds and the golovanova top.

            The first bird run

            Break a Chronicle card, walk east through Varrock and take the barge north of the Digsite to Fossil Island. Set the four houses at the four sites: two in the Verdant Valley, one in the Mushroom Forest (west of the ancient shroom, just north of the Mushroom Meadow mushtree) and one at the Tar Swamp entrance (don't go into the swamp). Each takes ten low-level seeds or five high-level ones, and seed types can be mixed; drop any low-level seeds left over (keep the barley), because the next runs' seeds come from the Draynor seed market. A house fills in about 50 minutes (10 birds at most); dismantling it returns the clockwork, and emptying it early loses the house, the seeds and the loot. Birds arrive about one every five minutes on average, so a house you check early is only part full.

            Leave by the barge. Your fire runes from Aubury are Superheat Item for the iron bars in the next two blocks, with the nature runes in your pouch, and your Varrock teleports from now on. Teleport to Falador for the next block.

            From here on, do a bird run whenever you pass Varrock and the houses are ready: empty each, rebuild it with four fresh logs and seeds, and keep the clockworks. Regular logs to 14 Hunter, oak to 24, then willow. Each tier also needs a Crafting level one above its Hunter level (15 for oak, 25 for willow), and the wiki advises ironmen to keep Crafting ahead of Hunter for that reason; at about 33 Crafting the willow houses this guide stops at are no problem. Keep doing them through 1.4 until you reach 42 Hunter, the goal from bird runs. The Museum Camp, whose bank chest unnotes your log stacks on the island, is built on the second run at the end of the Lumbridge block. The chest needs 21 Construction, two oak planks, an iron bar and five nails; the iron bar is one of the two you superheat in the next block.

            Save the first two teak seeds you get from nests, or later from farming contracts, for Woodcutting. Looking further ahead: from 46 Hunter, chase earth implings for an earth talisman; from 65, chase magpie implings, which with the Taverley crystal chest are the best source of a runite bar for Song of the Elves.

            Done when the first four bird houses are set.

            Coins: about 430,000 gp after the rune axe · Slots: about 21/28 at the end; about 26/28 at the peak, with the clockworks and logs · Requirements: 22 Mining for volcanic ash; 25 Construction for the crafting table 2; 8 Crafting for clockworks; 22 Herblore for compost potions; 25 and 38 Thieving for the fruit stall and master farmers

            Wiki references Museum Camp Mycelium transportation system Volcanic ash Crafting table 2 Clockwork Reward cart Compost potion Ultracompost Golovanova fruit top Master Farmer Bird house trapping

            Burthorpe: Death Plateau and Troll Stronghold

            This block does both troll quests in one go, frees Eadgar and opens the Troll Stronghold's secret south entrance for Eadgar's Ruse later. On the way north it finishes Sir Percival's part of Holy Grail, and on the way out of Port Sarim it collects what the next two blocks need: the iron bars, the clay for Prince Ali's key and a redberry for the skin paste. The two quests go in this order because Troll Stronghold can't start until Death Plateau is complete, and Death Plateau's reward is the right to buy climbing boots from Tenzing: Troll Stronghold's route climbs rocks that need those boots and 15 Agility.

            Gate in 43 Magic for Superheat Item; Death Plateau and 15 Agility for Troll Stronghold; a Falador Castle deathpile for the Death Plateau hand-in.
            Carry in about 21/28.
            Exit Death Plateau and Troll Stronghold done; Eadgar freed; the secret south entrance open; Holy Grail past Sir Percival; about 425,000 gp; about 22/28 with the iron bar, clay and redberry.
            Carries forward an iron bar (the Museum Camp), a clay and a redberry (Prince Ali Rescue).

            Port Sarim, Rimmington and the Goblin Village

            From Falador, walk south to Port Sarim. At Gerrant's Fishy Business buy a fly fishing rod and about 50 feathers (Death Plateau's ten trout), and at Wydin's food store one redberry for Prince Ali's skin paste. In the Rimmington mine, mine two iron ores and one clay, and cast Superheat Item on both ores (43 Magic, four fire runes and one nature rune each): one bar is for Dunstan's spiked boots, the other for the Museum Camp's bank chest. Superheat never fails, where a normal furnace smelts iron ore into a bar only half the time. The spell works only on unnoted ore in your inventory, and each cast gives 53 Magic XP on top of the Smithing XP a furnace would give for the bar. Keep the clay unworked; it becomes soft clay in Draynor.

            Walk north past Falador to the Goblin Village with the magic gold feather. For Holy Grail, choose Open (not Prod) on the sacks in the house on the east side and tell Sir Percival his father wants him; he takes your magic whistle. The sacks only give up Percival while the feather is in your bag, so drop nothing until he has talked to you. The whistle leaves your inventory on its own when the conversation ends, and from that point fairy ring BJR takes you into the Fisher Realm, which is why you no longer need a whistle. Drop the feather. Holy Grail's last step is the Fisher Realm by fairy ring later in this section, and it needs no more items.

            Done when you hold two iron bars, the clay and the redberry, and Sir Percival has the whistle.

            Death Plateau

            Walk on to Burthorpe and talk to Denulth in the Imperial Guard camp to start Death Plateau, and follow it: win the gamble with Harold in the Toad and Chicken (bets must be over 60 gp, and a 101 gp bet bankrupts him at once), find the combination for the stone balls, and get the secret way from Tenzing the Sherpa. Tenzing wants ten unnoted bread, ten unnoted cooked trout and the spiked boots Dunstan makes from a pair of climbing boots, Denulth's certificate and an iron bar (Tenzing sells climbing boots for 12 gp: buy one pair for Dunstan and one to wear), and the wiki is explicit that all 21 items go in one go: a UIM needs 21 free slots for the hand-in.

            Denulth's tent is south of the Games Room. The trail to Harold starts with Eohric on the top floor of Burthorpe Castle, and Harold himself sits upstairs in the pub, on its west side. He won't talk about the combination until you give him an Asgarnian ale, which the pub's bar sells, or take the one on the table downstairs. When he is bankrupt he hands you an IOU: read it, and the combination on its back replaces it. Read it before you touch the stone balls, because the storage room door in the castle stays shut if the balls go down first; if that happens, pick one up, read the combination and put it back. Keep the combination to the end, since Denulth wants it when you report. If you lose it, Harold gives you another. For the secret way, talk to Saba in the cave north-west of Burthorpe, then take the south branch at the fork to Tenzing's house. The north branch is the main path, and that is where the trolls attack. Dunstan works at the anvil in the north-east of the village.

            Your inventory is about 26 here, so use the Falador Castle deathpile from 1.3, and get the food after the death, because cooked food and bread disappear from a deathpile. Die in the castle (the death destroys the Chronicle; Diango sells it back in Draynor in the next block), walk back from Lumbridge and pick up only the coins, the spiked boots and quest items, the fly fishing rod and the feathers. Cast Lumbridge Home Teleport, fly fish ten trout in the river east of Lumbridge Castle and cook them on the castle range, drop the rod and feathers, buy the ten bread from the Culinaromancer's Chest, take the Burthorpe Games Room minigame teleport to Tenzing, then walk back to the pile within the hour and pick everything up. Tenzing refuses noted food and won't take a partial hand-in, so check that all 21 items are in your bag before you talk to him.

            Once he gives you the secret way map, walk it before you report: leave his house by the side door into his backyard, cross the stile, and head north and then east past the second mountain goat until the game says you have gone far enough. A warning about trolls appears on the way; ignore it, because they only attack on the main path. Then talk to Denulth with the combination in your bag to complete the quest. The reward includes a pair of steel claws, which nothing in this guide uses, so drop them at once. If you lose the map, Tenzing gives you another.

            Wear the climbing boots for both troll quests, with the graceful boots in your inventory, and drop them when you are back in Burthorpe: Tenzing sells another pair for 12 gp each time a later block climbs Trollheim, and a pair kept in the bag would cost a slot all the way there.

            Done when Death Plateau is complete and your pile is collected.

            Troll Stronghold

            Talk to Denulth again to start Troll Stronghold. Beat Dad (level 101) in the arena with your rune sword and best food, stopping when he yields (magic from the gate is a safespot), and leave by the north gate; run past the thrower trolls. Inside the Stronghold, kill a Troll General (max hit 38; safespot behind the counter with magic or ranged) for the prison key. In the prison, the two sleeping guards Berry and Twig (level 71) hold the cell keys: pickpocket them with 30 Thieving, or kill them through the cell doors. Free both Eadgar and Godric, leave by the secret exit and climb the south-east rocks, and report to Dunstan in Burthorpe to complete the quest (1 quest point, 2,000 Agility XP and a law talisman). Before you leave, unlock the secret south entrance to the Stronghold.

            The route starts behind Tenzing's house: cross the stile, follow the path to the rocks to the north and climb them in the climbing boots, then head east to the arena, climbing rocks twice more. Dad hits up to 27 with his tree and his hits can stun you, so eat early. The safespot is beside the arena's western gate. When he asks you to spare him, agree; you can then walk past him to the north gate, but he can still hit you while you stand in his reach. North of the arena a cave leads into Trollheim past level 69 and 71 mountain trolls, and level 68 thrower trolls attack from range along the north of the mountain. The rock-climbing shortcuts from the east side to the west (41 to 47 Agility) get you past most of them, and the path to the north-west goes to the God Wars Dungeon, not the Stronghold.

            Inside, go south from the entrance to a door in the west wall and north from there to the generals; the southernmost one can be safespotted from either side of the desk in his room. The prison is at the far north end of the Stronghold, down the stairs into Burntmeat's kitchen: use the prison key on the locked door and climb down, then go west and south to Berry and Twig. A failed pickpocket wakes that troll and starts a fight, and an empty cell is the safespot if you have to kill them. The secret south entrance comes out on this prison level, in the mountain passage between Burthorpe and the Fremennik Province, and it needs climbing boots to reach. Eadgar's Ruse comes back to the Stronghold twice, for Burntmeat at the end of the Keldagrim block and for the parrot in its own block.

            Sleeping Giants isn't in this block any more. Kovac's crate needs 20 free slots at once, which your kit never has outside a deathpile, and this block's pile is already busy with the Death Plateau hand-in: it gets a planned death of its own in the Al Kharid block, four blocks from now.

            Done when Troll Stronghold is complete.

            Coins: about 425,000 gp · Slots: about 22/28 at the end; about 27/28 at the peak, in the Rimmington mine · Requirements: 43 Magic for Superheat Item; none for Death Plateau; Death Plateau and 15 Agility for Troll Stronghold

            Wiki references Holy Grail Superheat Item Death Plateau Troll Stronghold Climbing boots

            Lumbridge, Draynor and Varrock: Prince Ali Rescue, The Lost Tribe and Fairytale I

            This block builds the Museum Camp, completes Prince Ali Rescue and Romeo & Juliet, takes The Lost Tribe as far as the goblin emotes, starts Fairytale I with two of its five farmers and plants Forgettable Tale's barley. Prince Ali's rescue takes eight slots at the end, so the camp comes first, while its planks and bar are still in your bag, and the rescue items are collected in the order below and used the same hour.

            Gate in 29 Construction for the camp's loom; Goblin Diplomacy, Rune Mysteries, 13 Agility, 13 Thieving and 17 Mining for The Lost Tribe; Lost City, Nature Spirit, Priest in Peril and The Restless Ghost for Fairytale I.
            Carry in about 22/28.
            Exit the Museum Camp built; Prince Ali Rescue and Romeo & Juliet done; The Lost Tribe waiting for the goblin emotes; Fairytale I started; the barley planted; the second bird run done; about 412,000 gp; about 20/28.
            Carries forward the brooch and the candle (The Lost Tribe), the botanical pie (Eadgar's Ruse), potato seeds.

            The Museum Camp

            Teleport to Varrock. At the Lumber Yard east of the city, buy 25 steel nails from the sawmill operator, and at Varrock East Bank unnote oak planks, which comes out as many as you have free slots, so take the two you need first and let the rest stay noted. Take the barge north of the Digsite to Fossil Island, and build the camp's structures with your hammer, in this order, so the oak planks never pile up in your bag:

            1. The bank chest (21 Construction): the two oak planks, the iron bar and five nails. For a UIM it is the island's unnoting point, and from now on it unnotes the log stacks for your bird houses.
            2. The well (22): unnote two oak planks at the new chest (it gives as many as fit, so drop any surplus), and buy a bucket and two ropes at the camp's general store; it takes the two planks, the bucket, a rope and five nails.
            3. The spinning wheel (28): unnote four oak planks the same way; four planks and five nails.
            4. The loom (29): unnote the last two oak planks the same way; two planks, the second rope and five nails.
            5. The fossil cleaning station (5): five planks, which the wiki says are found in the camp itself, and the last five nails.

            The barge lands you just west of the camp, and every structure is built with the hammer in your bag, so keep it until the last one is up. The sixth structure, the fire pit, takes another iron bar, three soft clay and two logs, and Peter by the rowboat only hands out his five fossils once all six are built. Neither is needed in this guide, so leave the fire pit unless you want the fossils for kudos. Drop the four oak planks you have left: nothing else in this chapter uses them. Ride the barge back.

            Done when the bank chest, well, spinning wheel, loom and cleaning station are built.

            Prince Ali's rescue items

            In Varrock, buy a pink skirt from Thessalia's Fine Clothes. Walk to Lumbridge, buy a bucket at the general store, take a pot of flour from the Culinaromancer's Chest in the castle basement, chop a log and burn it for ashes, and mine one copper and one tin ore in the Lumbridge Swamp's eastern mine; smelt them into a bronze bar at the Lumbridge furnace.

            Walk to Draynor. Fill the bucket at a well and use it on your clay for soft clay, fill it again, and in Aggie's house use the redberry, ashes, pot of flour and bucket of water on her for skin paste; drop the bucket. In the jailhouse east of the bank, talk to Lady Keli until she lets you take a key print with the soft clay. Walk back to the Lumbridge furnace and smelt the key print with the bronze bar into the bronze key: since January 2026 the key is made at any furnace, not by Osman.

            The jail guards outside are level 26 and attack anyone under combat level 53, which you are well past by now. Lady Keli only lets you near the key if you work through her boasts in order: say you have heard of her because she is famous, ask about her latest plan, ask how she knows nobody will free the prince, ask to see the key, and then ask to touch it. The key print appears when she lets you touch it, as long as the soft clay is in your bag.

            Talk to Fred the Farmer north of the castle for a pair of shears, shear three sheep in his pen and pick two onions from the patch by his farm. Spin the wool into three balls on the castle's first-floor spinning wheel for the wig, and drop the shears. In Draynor, give Ned the three balls of wool for a wig and buy a rope from him (15 gp in his dialogue now that Dragon Slayer I is started), give Aggie the two onions for yellow dye, and dye the wig. Walk south to Port Sarim and buy three beers in the Rusty Anchor.

            In Draynor, Ned's house is west of where Leela walks and Aggie's is west of Ned's, so the wig, the rope and the dye are three doors in a row.

            Your bag is fullest at the jailhouse, at about 26 items with the wig, paste, key, skirt, rope and beers.

            Done when you hold the yellow wig, skin paste, bronze key, pink skirt, rope and three beers.

            Prince Ali Rescue

            In Draynor, talk to Leela south of the wheat field. In the jailhouse, give Joe the three beers, use the rope on Lady Keli, open the cell door with the bronze key, and give Prince Ali the wig, skin paste and pink skirt. Walk east through Lumbridge, go on to Al Kharid (the toll gate is free from now on). Report to Chancellor Hassan in the palace to complete Prince Ali Rescue (3 quest points and 700 gp).

            If Leela doesn't tell you to deal with the guard, the wig is still white: dye it before you go in. Offer Joe the beers until he is drunk, and only then tie up Keli. Hassan is in the palace's southern chamber, and the reward also gives 500 Smithing and 500 Crafting XP.

            Don't craft the diary coif yet: it needs 38 Crafting and you are about 35 here. The coif is made in 1.4's Al Kharid block, after Elemental Workshop II and the Fremennik Trials have lifted your Crafting.

            Done when Prince Ali Rescue is complete.

            The Lost Tribe and the botanical pie

            Walk back to Lumbridge. Talk to Sigmund on the castle's upper floor to start The Lost Tribe, ask the Duke, the cook, Hans, Father Aereck and Bob about the cellar until one of them mentions the goblin (the cook does), and tell the Duke. At the swamp east of the castle, buy a candle from the candle seller (1,000 gp: the nearest shop candle is in Catherby, and a candle isn't worth a separate trip). Go down the kitchen trapdoor, mine through the rubble (17 Mining) and squeeze through (13 Agility), light the candle with your tinderbox, and take the brooch about ten steps in; come back the same way, because straying off the route can make the gas explode a naked flame. Show the brooch to the Duke. Keep the candle and the brooch: Mistag's cave at the end of the quest is next block.

            Sigmund stands on the first floor near the Duke. Hans walks round the outside of the castle, Father Aereck is in the chapel and Bob runs the axe shop, but the cook in the kitchen is first on the wiki's list, so start with him. The rubble is on the east side of the cellar. The candle seller stands by the dark hole into the Lumbridge Swamp Caves. If the gas does catch, it deals 12 damage and puts the candle out; relight it with your tinderbox and go back to the path. Reldo needs the brooch in the next step, so it stays in your bag from here.

            In the castle kitchen, bake the botanical pie: take a pie dish and a pot of flour from the Culinaromancer's Chest, fill a jug at the sink (the chest sells jugs too), make pastry dough and a pie shell, add the golovanova fruit top, and bake it on the range (52 Cooking). A pie has two halves: half for Sanfew at the start of Eadgar's Ruse, half for the troll potion later. The golovanova top came from the Hosidius fruit stall, its only source, so bake it on this range on purpose: with Cook's Assistant done, the Lumbridge range never burns a botanical pie from 52 Cooking. Each half boosts Herblore by 4 and heals 7.

            Done when you hold the brooch, the candle and the botanical pie.

            Fairytale I, the barley and the seeds

            In Draynor, talk to Martin the Master Gardener by the pig pen to start Fairytale I - Growing Pains (never pickpocket him: it blocks him for 10 minutes). At Olivia's seed stall in the market, buy about 200 potato seeds for your next bird runs; they stack in one slot. Buy the Chronicle back from Diango in the market too (300 gp, its charges come back with it): the Death Plateau death destroyed it, and Fairytale I's grotto exit below uses it.

            Walk to the hops patch north of Lumbridge. Talk to Vasquen, the first of the five farmers Fairytale I asks you to consult, then take the rake, seed dibber and compost from the tool leprechaun, rake the patch, add the compost and plant the four barley seeds. They are Forgettable Tale's barley malt later in this section.

            Done when Fairytale I is started, Vasquen is consulted and the barley is planted.

            Varrock: Romeo & Juliet, Reldo and Dreven

            Pick two cadava berries from the bushes south-east of Varrock. Give them to the Apothecary in south-west Varrock for a cadava potion, give it to Juliet in her house west of the square, and tell Romeo in the square to complete Romeo & Juliet (5 quest points).

            The bushes are just west of the south-east Varrock mine, and the Apothecary's shop, with the purple potion icon, is west of the sword shop. Choose to talk about something else when he greets you. Don't drink the cadava potion yourself: it knocks you out for a moment. The reward also includes an antique lamp worth 500 XP in a skill of your choice; rub it straight away so it doesn't take a slot.

            In the palace library, show Reldo the brooch for The Lost Tribe, then search the northern bookcase on the west wall for the goblin symbol book and read it. At the bush patch by the Champions' Guild, talk to Dreven, your second Fairytale I farmer. Reldo sits in the northern wing of the library, and the book is the one that tells you the symbol on the brooch belongs to the Dorgeshuun.

            Chop four logs on the way east and do your second bird run from the Digsite barge, rebuilding each house as you empty it with a fresh log and your potato seeds. Your Hunter should now be 12 or more, enough for The Ascent of Arceuus later; from 14 Hunter use oak logs.

            Done when Romeo & Juliet is complete, Reldo has seen the brooch, Dreven is consulted and the second bird run is set.

            Coins: about 412,000 gp · Slots: about 20/28 at the end; about 26/28 at the peak, at the jailhouse · Requirements: 21 to 29 Construction for the Museum Camp; 13 Agility, 13 Thieving and 17 Mining for The Lost Tribe; 52 Cooking for the botanical pie

            Wiki references Museum Camp Prince Ali Rescue The Lost Tribe Candle Botanical pie Fairytale I - Growing Pains Romeo & Juliet

            Falador, the Goblin Village and the Lost Tribe

            Cast Falador Teleport. This block consults Fairytale I's last three farmers, starts Eadgar's Ruse, takes Fairytale I into Zanaris and finishes The Lost Tribe. It also buys the pestle and mortar and the vial that The Shadow of the Storm needs two blocks from now, because Taverley is the last place on the route that sells them.

            Gate in 31 Herblore (boostable), Druidic Ritual and Troll Stronghold for Eadgar's Ruse; the goblin emotes for The Lost Tribe's end.
            Carry in about 20/28.
            Exit Eadgar's Ruse started; The Lost Tribe done and Death to the Dorgeshuun started; Fairytale I at Zandar Horfyre; the ghostspeak amulet worn; about 411,000 gp; about 21/28.
            Carries forward the symptoms list (Zandar Horfyre), half a botanical pie and the ranarr (the troll potion), a pestle and mortar and a vial (The Shadow of the Storm).

            Falador and Taverley: three farmers and Eadgar's Ruse

            Talk to Elstan at the allotment south of Falador and to Heskel at the tree patch in Falador Park: your third and fourth Fairytale I farmers. Walk to Taverley and talk to Alain at the tree patch, your fifth. At Jatix's Herblore Shop buy a pestle and mortar and one vial. Elstan's allotment is through the cabbage field on the farm south of the city.

            Eat half of the botanical pie for the Herblore boost (27 + 4 is 31) and talk to Sanfew upstairs in Taverley to start Eadgar's Ruse. Keep the other half for the troll potion. Walk to the Goblin Village and talk to the two generals for The Lost Tribe, mediating between them until you learn the goblin emotes.

            Sanfew is on the first floor above Jatix's Herblore Shop; choose the general questions and then ask him for more work. The boost only needs to hold while you talk to him, which is why the second half is saved: the wiki warns that a boosted start means boosting again for the troll potion, and that is what the other half is for in the Eadgar's Ruse block. With Generals Bentnoze and Wartface, ask whether they have heard of the Dorgeshuun, then steer the story to the tribe refusing to fight, then to the brooch and the emotes. Open your emote tab before you leave and check that the Goblin Bow and Goblin Salute are there; the bow is the one Mistag needs to see.

            Done when the three farmers are consulted, Eadgar's Ruse is started and you know the goblin emotes.

            Draynor and Zanaris

            Cast Falador Teleport and walk south-east to Draynor. Tell Martin the Master Gardener what the five farmers said. Walk on to the shed in the Lumbridge Swamp, wield your dramen staff and enter Zanaris. Talk to the Fairy Godfather in the throne room, then to Fairy Nuff north of the bank for the symptoms list. Wield your staff of air again and leave. Fairytale I's next step is Zandar Horfyre in the Dark Wizards' Tower, at the start of the next block.

            Ask the Godfather where the queen is, and he names Fairy Nuff, whose room is in the north-west of the city, north of the bank.

            While you're in the swamp, walk to Father Urhney's hut and ask him for a ghostspeak amulet, and wear it: you lost yours with the rest of 1.3's quest items, and the nature grotto and Creature of Fenkenstrain both need it. Urhney replaces a lost amulet as long as you don't already hold one, so the same hut is your answer if you lose this one too.

            Done when you hold the symptoms list and wear the ghostspeak amulet.

            The end of The Lost Tribe

            Go down the Lumbridge Castle kitchen trapdoor, light your candle and follow the cave route the wiki maps to Mistag, staying on it (clicking the minimap auto-paths) because the swamp gas can explode a naked flame. Perform the Goblin Bow in front of Mistag and ask him to show you out. Then follow the wiki's last steps: tell the Duke, who won't believe you; pickpocket Sigmund for his key and open his chest in the next room; pick the lock on the H.A.M. hideout's trapdoor north-west of the castle and take the silverware from the crate south of the ladder; give it to the Duke for the peace treaty; and follow Kazgar from the cellar to Mistag with the treaty to complete The Lost Tribe (1 quest point, 3,000 Mining XP and a ring of life). Give Mistag the brooch for a mining helmet, and start Death to the Dorgeshuun with him, doing only its very first dialogue.

            The carved symbols in the tunnels tell you which way to go: Rekeshuun means north, Idithuun south, Ekeleshuun east and Narogoshuun west, and you follow each one until you reach the next. A wrong turn can drop you into the Lumbridge Swamp Caves, where the gas is. Sigmund's chest is in the spinning wheel room. The hideout's lock can take a few tries, but you don't need a lockpick. Kazgar waits by the tunnel entrance in the cellar and takes you through to the mines when you talk to him; there, ask Mistag to fetch Ur-tag, and the treaty is signed in a cutscene in the castle's dining room.

            Drop the candle, the mining helmet and the ring of life. You keep wearing the ring of dueling, and a ring of life would teleport you out of every planned death in this chapter. Chapter 2's Death to the Dorgeshuun collects its own H.A.M. robes in the hideout, so leave any here.

            The holy symbol this block used to make now waits for Chapter 2: nothing in Chapter 1 uses it, Chapter 2 casts and blesses its own before Animal Magnetism, and that keeps a slot free for the rest of this section.

            Done when The Lost Tribe is complete and Death to the Dorgeshuun is started.

            Coins: about 411,000 gp · Slots: about 21/28 · Requirements: 31 Herblore (boostable), Druidic Ritual and Troll Stronghold for Eadgar's Ruse

            Wiki references Eadgar's Ruse The Lost Tribe Fairytale I - Growing Pains Jatix's Herblore Shop Ring of life

            Fairytale I's magic secateurs

            This block takes Fairytale I from the symptoms list to the magic secateurs. It is kept short on purpose: the three items the quest asks for are random, and you carry them into Morytania with nothing else on top of your kit.

            Gate in Fairytale I at Zandar Horfyre; the ghostspeak amulet.
            Carry in about 21/28.
            Exit the magic secateurs; the Falador diary's haircut and pump tasks; about 410,000 gp; about 21/28.
            Carries forward the magic secateurs (Tanglefoot), a spade stored with the tool leprechaun, the pestle and mortar and vial (The Shadow of the Storm).

            Falador, Zandar and the Draynor skull

            Teleport to Falador. Get a haircut from the hairdresser, and buy a bucket at the general store, fill it at the pump north of the west bank and drop it, both for the diary.

            Climb over the western Falador wall and take the symptoms list to Zandar Horfyre at the top of the Dark Wizards' Tower. Walk back round to Sarah's Farming Shop at the farm south of Falador and buy a spade. Walk east to Draynor Manor and dig on the grave north of the building, inside the fence, for the Draynor skull. Hand the spade to the tool leprechaun at the manor's belladonna patch: he keeps one for you, and any leprechaun gives it back, which is how a UIM owns a spade without carrying it.

            Zandar reads the list and sends you to Malignius Mortifer, who can tell you how to deal with the Tanglefoot. The skull can only be dug up once: if you drop or lose it, digging again gives nothing, so keep it until Malignius has it.

            Give the skull to Malignius Mortifer, north-west of the Port Sarim rune shop. Your quest journal then names the three random items the magic secateurs need. He stands just north-west of Port Sarim with four apprentice wizards around him, which makes him easy to pick out.

            Done when Malignius has the skull and your journal lists the three items.

            The three items

            The items are random. Notes for the hard ones:

            • A clean avantoe or irit: the Reward Cart's remaining rolls drop both grimy, and Zahur in Nardah cleans a grimy herb for a fee; the Sorceress's Garden is the other source.
            • A clean snapdragon: buy one for 10 vouchers at the Brimhaven Agility Arena.
            • A nature talisman: the wiki's UIM advice of 50 abyssal pearls at Guardians of the Rift needs Temple of the Eye first, and the Abyss needs Enter the Abyss, both of which come later in this section. If your list has the talisman, check the wiki's talisman page for the monsters that drop it near you before anything else: this is the one item that can hold the block up.
            • White berries: the Lava Isle spawn is in deep Wilderness, so take them from cave crawlers in the Fremennik Slayer Dungeon or from chaos druid warriors instead.

            If your list has jangerberries or edible seaweed, bring a spare: the ghasts in Mort Myre can rot food in your bag on the walk to the grotto, and those count as food.

            Melzar's Maze and the Crafting Guild are not in this block any more. Melzar's Maze moves to the Port Sarim block near the end of the section, so its map piece is not carried through twenty blocks, and the Crafting Guild's gold ore, a Falador diary task that needs a brown apron and 40 Crafting, waits for Chapter 2.

            Done when you hold the three items.

            The nature grotto

            Use the Shades of Mort'ton minigame teleport and walk north-west through Mort Myre to the nature grotto, killing a snail on the way for thin snail meat and its shell; step back out of the swamp and craft the shell into a snelm with your chisel there for the diary, because the task only counts a snelm made in Morytania outside Mort Myre; drop both. With the ghostspeak amulet on, give the Nature Spirit the three items for the magic secateurs.

            The grotto is deep in the swamp, north-west of Mort'ton, and the last stretch is a jump across a broken bridge to the cave entrance. The spirit enchants your secateurs into the magic secateurs, and only they can hurt the Tanglefoot.

            Break a Chronicle card to leave; it puts you outside the Champions' Guild for the next block.

            Done when you hold the magic secateurs.

            Coins: about 410,000 gp · Slots: about 21/28 at the end; about 24/28 with the three items · Requirements: Fairytale I's own; the ghostspeak amulet for the grotto

            Wiki references Fairytale I - Growing Pains Tool Leprechaun Nature talisman Ultimate Ironman Guide/Quests

            Al Kharid: Sleeping Giants and The Shadow of the Storm

            This block does two desert quests from one base. Sleeping Giants needs 20 empty slots when you search Kovac's crate, so it gets a planned death of its own right next to the Giants' Plateau; The Shadow of the Storm follows from the same spot. Sleeping Giants' 6,000 Smithing XP is also the first of the three quest rewards that carry you to the 40 Smithing the gold bars need later.

            Gate in 15 Smithing for Sleeping Giants; The Golem, Demon Slayer and 30 Crafting for The Shadow of the Storm; 33 quest points for the Champions' Guild; a planned death in the Al Kharid mine.
            Carry in about 21/28.
            Exit Sleeping Giants and The Shadow of the Storm done; about 404,000 gp; about 19/28.
            Carries forward nothing new. The Chronicle is destroyed by the death and bought back two blocks from now.

            Varrock: the black items, nails and oak logs

            Evil Dave only lets you into the demon's lair in at least three accepted black items, worn at once, and two of them are sold where the Chronicle put you. Upstairs in the Champions' Guild, buy a black full helm (1,372 gp) and black platelegs (2,496 gp) at Valaine's Shop of Champions. Walk into Varrock and buy a priest gown top at Thessalia's Fine Clothes. Do a bird run from the Digsite barge if the houses are ready.

            Then walk to the Lumber Yard north-east of the city, buy ten steel nails from the sawmill operator and chop three oak logs from the oaks around the yard. Your bag is now full, at 28, and that is fine for the next two minutes: use your ring of dueling's Emir's Arena teleport and walk south-east to the Al Kharid mine.

            Done when you hold the three black items, ten nails and three oak logs.

            Planned death: the Al Kharid mine

            Take off everything you wear and let the scorpions in the mine kill you (level 14; it takes a few minutes with no armour on). The death destroys the Chronicle: you buy it back at Diango's for 300 gp when you reach Draynor two blocks from now, and its charges come back with it. Nothing until then needs its teleport.

            You respawn in Lumbridge with nothing. Walk to Fred the Farmer's house north of the castle, take the shears from the table inside and shear one sheep: Kovac wants one piece of wool, and a ball of wool does not work. Drop the shears. Walk east through the Al Kharid gate and north to your pile, and pick up only the hammer, the chisel, the ten nails and the three oak logs. The hammer has to be an ordinary one (an Imcando hammer also works), which yours is.

            Done when you stand at your pile with the wool, the hammer, the chisel, the nails and the oak logs.

            Sleeping Giants

            Talk to Kovac on the Giants' Plateau, just east of the mine, to start Sleeping Giants. Inside the foundry, repair the polishing wheel, the grindstone and the trip hammer with the logs, the wool and the nails, talk to Kovac twice, then search the crate: this is the step that needs 20 empty slots, and you now have 26. Put each crate item into the crucible separately, set the moulds Kovac names at the mould jig, pour the metal, and cool the preform with a bucket of water from the pile by the waterfall (your hands must be empty to pick it up). Then work it as Kovac describes: heat it in the lava until it is red and use the trip hammer until the progress bar is yellow; cool it to almost green and use the grindstone until the bar is green; cool it again and finish on the polishing wheel. Talk to Kovac to complete the quest: 1 quest point and 6,000 Smithing XP.

            Kovac is the one hill giant beside the giant anvil with a Strike option rather than Attack; strike him and he asks you to stop, and the quest starts. The repairs split as follows: the trip hammer takes one oak log and five nails, the polishing wheel two oak logs, five nails and the wool, and the grindstone only needs your chisel. The wiki says the 20 slots are only needed for about 20 seconds, so put the crate's items into the crucible straight away. Dunk heats the preform faster than Heat, and Quench cools it faster than Cool. The trip hammer cools the metal, the grindstone heats it and the polishing wheel cools it, so watch the temperature bar as you work. A sword made outside the right temperatures loses quality, but Kovac accepts it for the quest whatever its quality.

            UIM risk The foundry's first room is an instance: anything you drop there is gone once you talk to Kovac, so pick it up first.

            Walk back to the mine and pick up the rest of your pile; you have an hour from the death, and this takes about twenty minutes.

            Done when Sleeping Giants is complete and your pile is collected.

            The evil disguise

            Talk to Father Reen, south of the Al Kharid bank, to start The Shadow of the Storm. The wiki's quest page says he hands you a Silverlight if you don't have one, and you dropped yours after Delrith; if he doesn't, Sir Prysin in Varrock Palace sells a replacement for 500 gp. Mine one silver ore in the Al Kharid mine and smelt it at the furnace: it becomes the demonic sigil.

            Take only one Silverlight into the ruins. Evil Dave won't let you join the ritual while you carry a dyed and an undyed Silverlight together, and dyeing one deletes any other Silverlight in your bag.

            Cast Teleport to House and put all six graceful pieces in the magic wardrobe, the gloves, boots and cape included: a UIM can only take graceful back out when the full set is stored, so storing just the three pieces the black items replace would lock them in. Then put on the black full helm, the black platelegs and the priest gown top. Then use the ring of dueling to the Emir's Arena again and walk south to the Shantay Pass: buy a Shantay pass and two waterskins, and take the magic carpet to Uzer.

            Done when you are at Uzer in the three black items, with Silverlight, the silver bar, the pestle and mortar and the vial.

            The Shadow of the Storm

            Follow the wiki's steps:

            1. Talk to Father Badden at the ruins, pick a black mushroom by the dungeon entrance, grind it into your vial with the pestle and mortar and use the black dye on Silverlight. Drop the pestle and mortar and the empty vial.
            2. Go down into the ruins, tell Evil Dave you want to join, and enter the portal. Denath gives you an incantation that is different for every player: write it down. Take the strange implement in the lair, ask Jennifer for the demonic sigil mould, and talk to Matthew about Josef's book.
            3. Outside, ask the clay golem what happened (before you search anything), then search the four kilns around Uzer until one gives you the demonic tome.
            4. Ride the carpet back to the Shantay Pass, walk to the Al Kharid furnace and cast the demonic sigil from the mould and the silver bar. Check that the sigil is in your bag before you drop the mould: the wiki says a lost mould can't be had again. Drop the mould, buy another Shantay pass and return to Uzer.
            5. Give Matthew the tome and lead the incantation in the order Denath gave you. Pick up the sigil Denath drops, follow the cultists out for Tanya's, persuade Evil Dave to go back for Eric's, and give a sigil each to Father Badden and Father Reen when they agree to help. Use the strange implement on the clay golem and talk to him again.
            6. Back in the lair, talk to Matthew to form the circle and chant in the book's order. Agrith-Naar (level 100) attacks in an instance. Flinch him with the rune sword from behind the north-western torch, which avoids all his damage, and wield Silverlight for the last hit: without it his health goes back to 12. Don't step behind a pillar or torch between a hit's XP drop and its damage.

            Agrith-Naar fights with melee up close and switches to Fire Blast if you step out of reach or turn on Protect from Melee, so the torch is your defence, not your prayers. If you stand out of reach with Protect from Magic on, he pulls you back next to him. What matters for the kill is that Silverlight is equipped when the final blow lands.

            UIM risk The fight is an instance, so anything you drop on its floor is lost.

            Silverlight becomes Darklight; taking it off triggers the last dialogue and completes the quest. Put the 10,000 XP on Ranged (any combat skill except Prayer), for the 43 Ranged you want later in this section. That XP shortens the Loar shade session in the Imp Catcher block, where you throw knives to 43.

            Cast Teleport to House, take the full graceful set back out and put it on, and drop the black items, Darklight, the waterskins and anything else the quest left you.

            Done when The Shadow of the Storm is complete and you are back in graceful at your house.

            Coins: about 404,000 gp · Slots: about 19/28 at the end; 28/28 for the two minutes before the planned death, about 27/28 holding the cultists' sigils · Requirements: 15 Smithing for Sleeping Giants; The Golem, Demon Slayer and 30 Crafting for The Shadow of the Storm

            Wiki references Sleeping Giants The Shadow of the Storm Valaine's Shop of Champions Silverlight Ultimate Ironman Guide/Item Management

            Creature of Fenkenstrain

            This block does the whole of Creature of Fenkenstrain, which Garden of Tranquillity needs later. The quest fills your bag with body parts in the Experiment Cave, so for this one trip you shed four cheap things and buy three of them back afterwards. Its reward, the ring of charos, is the ring the first Garden of Tranquillity block turns into the ring of charos (a) at the Wise Old Man's house.

            Gate in Priest in Peril and The Restless Ghost; 20 Crafting and 25 Thieving; 43 Magic for Superheat Item; the ghostspeak amulet.
            Carry in about 19/28.
            Exit Creature of Fenkenstrain done, with the ring of charos; about 385,000 gp; about 20/28.
            Carries forward the ring of charos (Garden of Tranquillity), chaos runes (your Fire Bolts for the rest of the section), three redberries (Thurgo's pie).

            Varrock: the wires, the silver bar and the runes

            At the Hosidius allotment north of your house, hand the magic secateurs to the tool leprechaun and take the spade from him. Cast Varrock Teleport. Do a bird run if the houses are ready and drop what is left of the potato seeds afterwards; Draynor's seed market is in the next block.

            Buy about 200 chaos runes from Aubury: with your fire runes and the staff of air they are Fire Bolt, your safespot spell from here to the end of the section. Mine one silver ore in the mine south-west of Varrock and cast Superheat Item on it for a silver bar. In the mine south-east of Varrock, mine three copper and three tin ores and superheat each pair into a bronze bar, then smith three bronze wires at the anvils in Varrock (4 Smithing, one bar each). The wires have to be unnoted, which they are when you smith them yourself.

            Now drop the hammer, the chisel and the tinderbox: the Varrock general store sells all three for 1 gp each on your way back. Unnote one swordfish at the east bank and cook it.

            Done when you hold three bronze wires, a silver bar, the spade, the chaos runes and one cooked swordfish, and the hammer, chisel, tinderbox and seeds are dropped.

            Canifis and the castle

            Walk east through Paterdomus to Canifis. Read the signpost in the centre of the village to start Creature of Fenkenstrain. At the Canifis general store buy a needle and five thread (a costume needle would not have worked), and buy the pickled brain from the table in the Hair of the Dog for 50 gp. If the signpost says you don't meet the requirements, go to Dr Fenkenstrain anyway: he still offers you the job.

            In Fenkenstrain's Castle, north-east of the village, talk to Dr Fenkenstrain and choose the braindead and grave-digging answers. Upstairs, read The Joy of Grave Digging in the west bookcase and Handy Maggot Avoidance Techniques in the east one: they hold the marble and obsidian amulets, which you combine into the star amulet at once.

            Done when you hold the star amulet and the pickled brain.

            The graves and the Experiment Cave

            Dig at the cross-shaped grave marker in the Haunted Woods, south-east of the castle, for the decapitated head, and use the pickled brain on it; the vampyres here are level 61 to 72, so don't stand around. Head north out of the woods, use the star amulet on the easternmost memorial and push it to enter the Experiment Cave; drop the amulet once you're in. The grave is Ed Lestwit's, south-east of fairy ring ALQ, and the right memorial is the one with an indent in it.

            Kill a level 51 Experiment for the cavern key, with Fire Bolt and your swordfish. Follow the north-western path to the end, search the chest and climb the ladder, then dig up the three graves for the torso, arms and legs. Drop the spade and the key. This is the block's peak, at about 27 items for a moment. The other experiments in the cave don't attack unless you do, and the crevice to the south-west is a safespot for Fire Bolt against the level 51 one. The three graves are to the north once you climb the ladder.

            Give Dr Fenkenstrain the body parts, then talk to him again to hand over the needle and thread.

            Done when the doctor has the body parts, the needle and the thread.

            The lightning conductor

            With the ghostspeak amulet on, talk to the Gardener Ghost for the shed key. Search the cupboard in the shed for the garden brush, take three canes from the pile beside the shed, and attach them to the brush with the three bronze wires. Upstairs, use the extended brush on the western fireplace for the conductor mould. The ghost is behind the castle, the canes are east of the shed, and attaching them needs 20 Crafting. Use the brush on the middle of the fireplace, not on its surround, or nothing happens.

            The conductor is cast from the mould and your silver bar at a furnace, and the nearest you can use is Lumbridge's: cast Lumbridge Home Teleport, cast the conductor at the furnace north of the castle, cast Varrock Teleport and walk back to the castle. Climb the southern ladder upstairs and repair the lightning conductor, talk to the doctor, then go through the metal door upstairs, climb the northern ladder and talk to the monster. Back downstairs, pickpocket Dr Fenkenstrain (25 Thieving) for the ring of charos, which completes the quest: 2 quest points and 1,000 Thieving XP.

            If the furnace doesn't offer you the lightning conductor, log out and back in; if it still won't, talk to the Gardener Ghost again, which the wiki says can unlock the option.
