The doctor gives you the tower key for the metal door, and you talk to the monster twice: the second conversation is the one that tells you about the ring.

            Done when Creature of Fenkenstrain is complete and you hold the ring of charos.

            Back through Varrock

            Walk back to Varrock. Buy a hammer, a chisel and a tinderbox at the general store, and pick three redberries from the bushes by the mine south-east of the city: they are the pie Thurgo wants in The Giant Dwarf. Walk south to Lumbridge for the next block.

            Done when you hold the tools and the redberries.

            Coins: about 385,000 gp after the chaos runes · Slots: about 20/28 at the end; about 27/28 at the peak, at the three graves · Requirements: 20 Crafting and 25 Thieving for Creature of Fenkenstrain; 43 Magic for Superheat Item; 4 Smithing for bronze wire

            Wiki references Creature of Fenkenstrain Bronze wire Needle Ring of charos Superheat Item

            Zanaris and Draynor: Fairytale I, the fairy rings and A Porcine of Interest

            This block completes Fairytale I and A Porcine of Interest and opens the fairy rings with the first steps of Fairytale II. It also bakes the redberry pie Thurgo wants in The Giant Dwarf, because Lumbridge is the last range and pie dish on the way there.

            Gate in the magic secateurs; Lost City and Nature Spirit for Fairytale II.
            Carry in about 20/28.
            Exit Fairytale I and A Porcine of Interest done; the fairy rings open; the Chronicle bought back; about 388,000 gp; about 23/28.
            Carries forward the redberry pie (Thurgo), the magic secateurs and three jute seeds (the next block's first stops), potato seeds.

            Lumbridge: the secateurs and the redberry pie

            At the hops patch north of Lumbridge, where your barley is growing, take the magic secateurs back from the tool leprechaun. Leave the barley in the ground: grown hops don't die, and Forgettable Tale's brew is made much later in this section.

            In the castle basement, take a pie dish and a pot of flour from the Culinaromancer's Chest. In the kitchen, fill a jug at the sink (the chest sells jugs), make pastry dough and a pie shell, add the redberries and bake the redberry pie on the range (10 Cooking). Drop the jug and the empty pot.

            Done when you hold the magic secateurs and the redberry pie.

            Fairytale I

            Walk to the shed in the Lumbridge Swamp, wield the dramen staff and enter Zanaris. Wield the magic secateurs, squeeze through the gap towards the cosmic altar (using the low-level Agility shortcut once is a diary task) and kill Tanglefoot, which only the secateurs can hurt. Pick up the queen's secateurs and give them to the Fairy Godfather to complete Fairytale I - Growing Pains: 2 quest points, 3,500 Farming, 2,000 Attack and 1,000 Magic XP, and the magic secateurs are yours to keep.

            Head south-west from the entrance, past the two Agility shortcuts, to the ruins around the cosmic altar. The lower shortcut, the 46 Agility jutting wall, is the Lumbridge & Draynor hard diary task, and failing it costs 4 Hitpoints. The Tanglefoot's corridor is through the gap on the west side of the altar room, past the Gatekeeper, and a few level 45 baby tanglefoots wait in the tunnel before the level 111 Tanglefoot at the far end. It hits up to 11 with melee, and your secateurs hit harder the higher your Farming and Strength are. To flinch it, stand one tile east of the southern end of the corridor, attack, and step back to the east tile until its health bar disappears; repeat.

            UIM risk The fight is an instance, so anything dropped there is lost, and you need the dramen staff to get back into Zanaris at all, so don't take risks with low Hitpoints.
            Done when Fairytale I is complete.

            Draynor: the Chronicle, the seeds and A Porcine of Interest

            Walk to Draynor. Buy the Chronicle back from Diango for 300 gp and wear it; it keeps the charges it had. At Olivia's seed stall buy about 200 potato seeds for the bird runs and three jute seeds for a diary task in the next block, and buy a rope from Ned.

            Talk to Martin the Master Gardener by the pig pen to start Fairytale II - Cure a Queen, and again in the market. He needs five minutes before the next step, which is the time A Porcine of Interest takes. The wait counts logged-in time only, so stay logged in while you do the sourhog.

            For A Porcine of Interest, walk to the crossroads north of the village, follow the cabbages and carrots north-east to the strange hole, use the rope on it and climb down. Investigate the skeleton at the end of the cave, take the reinforced goggles from Spria and wear them, then kill Sourhog (level 37). The wiki notes he resists slash and magic, so use the rune sword on its stab style. Cut off his foot, show it to Sarah at the farm south of Falador, and report to Spria in Draynor to finish: 1 quest point, 5,000 gp and 1,000 Slayer XP. Drop the goggles.

            The quest starts at the notice board behind Fortunato's Wine Shop in Draynor, and the wiki's order has you talk to Sarah at the Falador farm about the bounty before you go to the crossroads. The hole is beside the River Lum. At the skeleton the pig's acid knocks you out and Spria brings you to her house in Draynor; during that cutscene, don't click the ground or follow the kitten, or you start again at the cave entrance. The sourhog is immune to poison. You need a slash weapon or a knife to cut off the foot, and your rune sword or knife does it; a bronze scimitar spawns by the skeleton if you have neither. Besides the coins and XP, the quest gives 30 Slayer reward points.

            Don't buy a Slayer shopping list from Spria or anyone else here: mirror shield, rock hammer, facemasks, earmuffs, nose peg, spiny helmet, bug lantern, insulated boots, boots of stone, bag of salt, slayer gloves, ice coolers and enchanted gems. Every one of them is for Chapter 2's Slayer and is sold by the same masters then.

            Done when A Porcine of Interest is complete and Martin's five minutes are up.

            Fairytale II until the rings open

            Talk to Martin again. In Zanaris, pick up Nuff's certificate from the floor of Fairy Nuff's grotto north of the bank and study it, read the Rune Temple sign just south of the cosmic altar, talk to the Fairy Godfather in the throne room, then to the Co-ordinator just north of it, which unlocks the fairy rings, and to Fairy Fixit by the ring. Drop the certificate. The rest of the quest needs 57 Herblore and waits for a later chapter.

            Both the certificate and the sign matter: the wiki says that without the sign read, the later ring sequence to the queen only tells you to investigate further. Ask the Godfather both of his questions and agree to come back once you've found the queen. From the Co-ordinator, go west to the fountains and south to the fairy ring, where Fairy Fixit stands. From now on any fairy ring works for you with the dramen staff wielded.

            Done when you can use the fairy rings.

            Coins: about 388,000 gp · Slots: about 23/28 · Requirements: Lost City, Nature Spirit and Fairytale I for Fairytale II's start

            Wiki references Fairytale I - Growing Pains Fairytale II - Cure a Queen A Porcine of Interest Chronicle Fairy rings

            The fairy ring tour: Holy Grail, Jungle Potion and Tai Bwo Wannai

            With the dramen staff wielded, this block uses the rings for a string of diary tasks, finishes Holy Grail, does Jungle Potion and the Tai Bwo Wannai cleanup, and starts three quests. The Karamja stop used to come much later; it is here because its fairy ring is the cheapest way in, and because Jiminua's store sells the antipoison the Kourend blocks need.

            Gate in the fairy rings; 13 Farming for jute; 3 Herblore for Jungle Potion; 20 Farming for The Garden of Death.
            Carry in about 23/28.
            Exit Holy Grail and Jungle Potion done; Tai Bwo Wannai Trio, The Garden of Death and Olaf's Quest started; 100% Tai Bwo Wannai favour; about 388,000 gp; about 22/28.
            Carries forward the redberry pie (Thurgo), one antipoison (Kourend).

            Holy Grail and the Kandarin rings

            Visit in order:

            • CIR, for the diary. It lands you in the Kebos Lowlands south of Mount Karuulm, and using it is a Kourend & Kebos medium diary task.
            • BJR, the Fisher Realm: cross the bridge, go up through the castle and take the Holy Grail. Sir Percival took your whistle in Burthorpe's block, so the ring is your way in. Percival is the king now and you don't fight the Black Knight Titan again; the grail is reached by the staircase at the castle's east end and the ladder above it.
            • ALS, McGrubor's Wood, for the diary. Walk out to the hops patch north of the wood, take the rake and seed dibber from the tool leprechaun, plant the three jute seeds for the diary, and hand him the rake, the dibber and the magic secateurs: he stores them until the white tree needs the secateurs. Then walk east to Camelot and give the Holy Grail to King Arthur to complete Holy Grail: 2 quest points, 11,000 Prayer XP and 15,300 Defence XP. Walk back to the ring. The ALS task is on the Kandarin medium diary, and the white tree is the Ice Mountain stop in Garden of Tranquillity II.
            • BIS, the Ardougne zoo, and AIR, both for the diary. Both rings are tasks on the Ardougne medium diary; BIS opens in the zoo's unicorn pen, which has no other access, and AIR is an empty island south-east of Ardougne, so you only touch each one and dial on.
            • DIS, for the diary. It is south of the Wizards' Tower, and travelling there by fairy ring is a Lumbridge & Draynor medium task that needs Fairytale II started, as it now is.
            • AJP, optionally, to do Twilight's Promise; it needs some combat. Otherwise it waits for the end of the section.
            Done when Holy Grail is complete and the jute is planted.

            Karamja: Jungle Potion and Tai Bwo Wannai

            Take ring CKR and walk north to Tai Bwo Wannai. At Jiminua's Jungle Store, north-west of the village, buy two antipoisons, a machete and a spade.

            Talk to Trufitus in his house to start Jungle Potion. He wants five herbs one at a time, each cleaned and shown to him before he names the next:

            1. Snake weed, from the marshy jungle vines south-west of the village. A search can take one to five minutes, depending on your Herblore, so keep searching.
            2. Ardrigal, from the palm trees north-east of Trufitus, past the harpie bug swarms. The palms are on a peninsula just south of the Agility shortcut, and the swarms there are level 46.
            3. Sito foil, from the scorched earth directly south of the village. The tribesmen around it attack on sight, and their poison starts at 11 damage, so keep an antipoison sip ready here.
            4. Volencia moss, from the rocks at the mine south-east of Trufitus. Search the empty rocks, not the ore.
            5. Rogue's purse, from the fungus-covered walls of the cave north-west past the swarms; search the rocks to enter and run past the level 53 jogres. A rogue's purse dropped by a jogre doesn't count. The rocks are on the coast west of the ardrigal peninsula, and the fungus wall is south of the cave entrance; the search can take several minutes.

            Return to Trufitus to complete it: 1 quest point and 775 Herblore XP. Then talk to Timfraku upstairs in the village to start Tai Bwo Wannai Trio; the rest of that quest is Chapter 2's.

            Now do the Tai Bwo Wannai cleanup with the machete until your favour is 100%, which is a Karamja medium diary task, and don't cash it in: My Arm's Big Adventure in Chapter 2 needs 60% of it. Kill jungle spiders north of the calquat patch if you need the last few percent, and make and cook a spider on a stick for the diary. If a gout tuber appears while you cut, dig it up with the spade at once (it disappears after 10 minutes) and keep it only if you want Chapter 2's opal machete trade to be a little cheaper; finding it is not a diary task in itself. Use one antipoison whenever the jungle poisons you.

            Favour drops when you claim trading sticks, so taking any now would eat into the 60% My Arm uses later, and that quest takes the favour away when you hand it over. Dense jungle gives the most favour per cut and jungle spiders give one to two percent each, though the wiki says they only count once you have claimed trading sticks from Murcaily at least once. For the spider on a stick, use the machete on a thatching spar for a skewer stick, add the spider carcass a jungle spider drops, and cook it (16 Cooking). The gout tuber matters because Safta Doc's opal machete in Chapter 2 needs one; keeping it saves the 240 trading sticks Gabooty charges for a tuber then.

            Drop the machete, the spade and whatever is left of the first antipoison. Keep the second antipoison for Kourend.

            Done when Jungle Potion is complete, Tai Bwo Wannai Trio is started and your favour is 100%.

            The Garden of Death and Olaf's Quest

            Take ring BLS. Start The Garden of Death (20 Farming, not boostable) by searching the tent at the campsite south of the lizardman settlement, and explore the cave below until you have enough words to be sent to Lake Molch. The lizardmen on the way poison, which is the first use of your antipoison; sip it, don't finish it.

            The tent gives you Kasonde's journal, and the cave is down the hole beside the camp. Search the numbered stone table for the first tablet, then the other tables, the vase and the eastern chest for the carvings and phrases that translate its words; once the tablet points north to an island, the only one it can mean is on Lake Molch, which the Kourend blocks visit. You can't teleport out of the cave, so climb back up before you walk to the ring. The lizardmen on this route are up to level 75 and attack with poisoned ranged hits.

            Take ring DKS. Talk to Olaf Hradson to start Olaf's Quest, and kill five rock crabs for the diary. Walk south-east past Rellekka to the Keldagrim entrance for the next block. Olaf is beside the ring, north-east of Rellekka, and the rest of his quest, which needs 40 Firemaking and 50 Woodcutting, is done in Chapter 2.

            Done when The Garden of Death and Olaf's Quest are started.

            Coins: about 388,000 gp · Slots: about 22/28 at the end; about 26/28 on Karamja · Requirements: 13 Farming for jute; 3 Herblore for Jungle Potion; 20 Farming for The Garden of Death

            Wiki references Fairy rings Holy Grail Jungle Potion Jiminua's Jungle Store Tai Bwo Wannai Cleanup The Garden of Death

            Keldagrim and the mountains: The Giant Dwarf, Mountain Daughter and Eadgar's Ruse

            This block completes The Giant Dwarf and Mountain Daughter and takes Eadgar's Ruse to the parrot. The Giant Dwarf's 2,500 Smithing XP is the second of the three quest rewards on the way to 40 Smithing, and Forgettable Tale of a Drunken Dwarf, which it unlocks, is done whole in the Blast Furnace block later. The Giant Dwarf also opens the Keldagrim minecarts, and Mountain Daughter uses them for its trip to White Wolf Mountain, which is why the two quests share a block in that order.

            Gate in 12 Crafting, 16 Firemaking, 14 Thieving and 33 Magic for The Giant Dwarf; 20 Agility for Mountain Daughter; 43 Magic for Superheat Item; an Eadgar's Ruse already started.
            Carry in about 22/28.
            Exit The Giant Dwarf and Mountain Daughter done; Eadgar's Ruse waiting for the parrot; about 380,000 gp; about 18/28.
            Carries forward nothing new. The hammer, chisel and tinderbox are dropped at the end and bought back in Varrock in the next block.

            The Giant Dwarf: the axe and Thurgo

            Enter the cave east of Rellekka and talk to the Dwarven Boatman to start The Giant Dwarf; finish the conversation with Commander Veldaban when you arrive, and talk to Blasidar the sculptor on the east side of the city, south of the kebab shop. He needs clothes, boots and an axe for his statue, and the order below keeps your bag under 27.

            Inside the entrance, carry on east past the statues; the passage south leads to the foot of Trollweiss Mountain instead. Talk to the Dwarven Boatman, not the Ferryman: his ride is free, and it ends with the boat hitting the king's statue and you under arrest at the Black Guard headquarters, which is how you meet Veldaban. Blasidar's house is across the bridge from the western half of the city.

            Start with the axe. Take the dwarven battleaxe from Santiri at the weapon shop in the north-west of West Keldagrim, buy three cut sapphires from Hervi's gem stall in the eastern market, use them on the axe and talk to Santiri again. Buy one iron ore from Ordan at the Blast Furnace (25 gp) and cast Superheat Item on it for an iron bar.

            Cast Falador Teleport and walk south through Port Sarim to Thurgo at Mudskipper Point. Give him the iron bar and the redberry pie and he repairs the axe. Chop one log from a tree by his hut, then choose his option to return to Keldagrim immediately. That option puts you down at Blasidar's house. If you lose the axe later, Thurgo gives you a new one, but you have to set three sapphires in it again.

            Done when you are back in Keldagrim with the repaired axe and one log.

            The Giant Dwarf: the clothes, the boots and the Consortium

            Ask Vermundi in the eastern market for special clothes. In the library in the north-east of West Keldagrim, ask the librarian about King Alvis's clothes and climb a bookcase for the book on costumes (the ladder needs you under 30 kg, which graceful takes care of). Buy one coal from Ordan (67 gp), put the coal and the log into Vermundi's spinning machine, light it with your tinderbox and pay her 200 gp for the exquisite clothes. Give Vermundi the book first: she can't make the clothes without a picture of them. If lighting the machine fails, light it again.

            Talk to Saro at the anvil south-west of the bank. In Dromund's house north of the bank, take the left boot while he isn't looking, then cast Telekinetic Grab on the right boot through the window from behind the house (33 Magic, one law rune). The left boot is on the table by the door. The right one is on another table that Dromund's cat watches, which is why it has to come through the window.

            Give the clothes, the axe and the boots to Riki, the sculptor's model in Blasidar's house, and talk to Blasidar. Then join a Consortium company: talk to the secretary of any company except the Red Axe upstairs in the market and do her tasks. Each asks for three to five of an ore or bar; buy the ores from Ordan and superheat what needs smelting. If a task asks for something you can't get, ask again before you close the dialogue, and never hop worlds mid-task. At 100 points the director lets you join; tell him Blasidar sent you, and report to Commander Veldaban west of the bank to complete the quest: 2 quest points, 2,500 XP each in Mining, Smithing and Crafting, and 1,500 each in Magic, Thieving and Firemaking.

            Talk to Blasidar before you take any task: the wiki warns that if you skip him, the director's tasks can go on without end. Points don't carry from one company to another, so stay with the one you pick. A secretary's task is three to five unnoted ores or coal, with ten minutes plus one minute per item to deliver; clay is the one item Keldagrim neither sells nor lets you mine, so ask for another task if you get it. Each delivery is worth 20 points, and a failed or cancelled task costs 2. At 75 points the director calls you in and his tasks switch to two to four bars, worth 12 points each, which is where Superheat earns its place. If a task gets stuck, logging out and back in clears it. When you join, say Blasidar sent you and that you will vouch for him at the meeting. Veldaban's meeting is a cutscene of about four minutes; you can decline it and read the notes afterwards.

            Done when The Giant Dwarf is complete.

            Mountain Daughter

            At Gunslik's Assorted Items in the West Consortium building, buy a rope (23 gp) and a pair of leather gloves (7 gp), and unnote one plank at the Keldagrim bank. Leave the city and walk to the Mountain Camp east of Rellekka: take the path north past the guard and use the rope on the boulder to get round the rockslide.

            Talk to Hamal the Chieftain to start Mountain Daughter, then follow the wiki's parts:

            1. Dig mud from the pond south of Hamal's tent and take a pole from north of it (the dramen staff doesn't work as one). Go north along the lake, use the mud on the tall tree and climb it, pole-vault over the rock clump, lay the plank on the flat stones and listen to the shining pool. Use the plank on the flat stone again to get back. Keep the pole and the plank: you cross to the pool this way again for the Kendal and for the burial, and the mud on the tree only has to be put there once.
            2. Talk to Hamal about the people of Rellekka, and to Jokul north of his tent. Find Svidi between Rellekka and the Keldagrim entrance, talk to Brundt in Rellekka's longhall, mine the Ancient Rock in the camp's southern tent with your pickaxe for half a rock, take it to Brundt for the safety guarantee, give that to Svidi and return to Hamal. Talk to Jokul before you go looking for Svidi, because the search doesn't work until you have. Svidi stands near the black unicorns outside the Keldagrim entrance, and with Brundt you ask about the Mountain Camp.
            3. Talk to Hamal about the food supplies. Ride the Keldagrim minecart to White Wolf Mountain, put the leather gloves on and pick the thorny bushes by the gnome glider for a white pearl; eat it for the seed and give the seed to Hamal. Drop the leather gloves.
            4. Speak to the shining pool again, cross back, follow the northern path east and chop through the trees to the cave. The Kendal (level 70, melee only) can be safespotted behind the skeletons in his room: use Fire Bolt. Take the corpse back to Hamal. Face east when you chop the dead trees. In the cave, tell the Kendal you are no one special, ask about the sacrifice, call out the bear suit and challenge him, and the fight starts. He hits up to 9, and the skeletons that block him are the ones around the pillar in the middle of the room. Take the woman's corpse from the room, and tell Hamal the Kendal was no god before you agree to help.

            Before the last step, drop the hammer, the chisel and the tinderbox: it needs nine slots at once, and Varrock sells all three again in the next block. Gather five muddy rocks around the camp, get Asleif's necklace from Ragnar by the tall tree, cross to the island in the shining pool, bury the corpse and use the muddy rocks on the mound. That completes the quest: 2 quest points, 2,000 Prayer XP and 1,000 Attack XP. Drop the bearhead, the pole, the plank and the rope. The Kendal always drops the bearhead, and nothing in this guide uses it.

            Done when Mountain Daughter is complete.

            Eadgar and Burntmeat

            Use the Burthorpe Games Room minigame teleport and buy a pair of climbing boots from Tenzing (12 gp); wear them with the graceful boots in your bag. Take Death Plateau's secret way up to Trollheim and talk to Eadgar in his cave at the top about goutweed. Go down into the Troll Stronghold, talk to Burntmeat in the kitchen at the south end of the middle floor, and return to Eadgar, who now wants a parrot. That is the Ardougne zoo's job in the farming tour later.

            Eadgar's cave is the unmarked one on the south side of Trollheim's summit; he is only there because you freed him in Troll Stronghold. Burntmeat is one of the troll cooks, and he trades what he knows about goutweed for a human to put in his stew, which is the fake man the rest of Eadgar's Ruse builds. The cooks don't attack, but many of the trolls on the way do.

            Break a Chronicle card to leave, put the graceful boots back on and drop the climbing boots.

            Done when Eadgar has asked for the parrot.

            Coins: about 380,000 gp · Slots: about 18/28 at the end; about 27/28 at the peak, at Mountain Daughter's burial · Requirements: 12 Crafting, 16 Firemaking, 14 Thieving and 33 Magic for The Giant Dwarf; 20 Agility for Mountain Daughter

            Wiki references The Giant Dwarf Ordan Gunslik's Assorted Items Mountain Daughter Eadgar's Ruse

            Kourend I: Port Piscarilius, Shayzien, Lovakengj and Arceuus

            Kourend is split into three blocks. This one buys back the tools Mountain Daughter cost you, sails to Port Piscarilius and starts four of the five Kourend quests. Whenever you need the minecart network, the Wintertodt camp's station is the hub.

            Gate in 16 Strength and 10 Mining for Tale of the Righteous; 20 Thieving and 25 Cooking for The Queen of Thieves; 18 Agility for The Depths of Despair; 12 Hunter for The Ascent of Arceuus.
            Carry in about 18/28.
            Exit The Queen of Thieves, Tale of the Righteous, The Forsaken Tower and The Ascent of Arceuus started; the Arceuus spellbook; about 378,000 gp; about 25/28.
            Carries forward Kharedst's memoirs, the letter (The Queen of Thieves), earth runes (the Battlefront teleport), the fly fishing rod and feathers (the River Molch trout), the antipoison.

            Varrock and Port Sarim

            The Chronicle put you outside the Champions' Guild. In Varrock, buy a hammer, a chisel and a tinderbox at the general store, and a shortbow and a few bronze arrows at Lowe's Archery Emporium: one puzzle in Tale of the Righteous has to be hit with ranged, and you own no ranged weapon yet. Do a bird run from the Digsite barge. Use up your potato seeds on it and drop the rest: there is no bird run from Kourend, and the master farmers in Hosidius refill the stack when you are back there.

            The bow is for one moment only. Pagida's puzzle wants a magic hit, a melee hit and a ranged hit in the same visit, and fists don't count as the melee hit, so the shortbow joins your Fire Bolt runes and the rune sword rather than replacing anything.

            Cast Falador Teleport and walk to Port Sarim. At Gerrant's Fishy Business buy a fly fishing rod and ten feathers for the Kourend diary's trout. The rod is 5 gp and feathers 2 gp each. The River Molch's spots give salmon as well as trout, which is why you buy more than one feather. Talk to Veos on the docks and sail to Port Piscarilius.

            Done when you are in Port Piscarilius with your tools, the bow and arrows, the rod and the feathers.

            Port Piscarilius, Shayzien and Lovakengj

            Start The Queen of Thieves with Tomas Lawry outside the bank (20 Thieving, not boostable). Buy a piece of meat and a raw potato at Kenelme's Wares and a bowl at the general store, fill the bowl at the sink in Robert O'Reilly's house, cook the stew (25 Cooking) and give it to him. He sends you through the manhole into the Warrens: kill Conrad King on the dock for Devan Rutter, then speak to the Queen of Thieves, who sends you to Kingstown.

            Everything for the stew is a few steps from Tomas. Kenelme's Wares is the house directly west of him, Leenz's General Supplies is the general store, and Robert lives in the building directly south of him. In Robert's house, fill the bowl at the sink and add the raw potato, cook the meat on his range and add it, and then cook the bowl into stew. The manhole is a short walk east of Tomas, marked by a dungeon icon on the minimap. Below, go a little south and then west over two small bridges to the large island where Devan Rutter stands; the bridges don't show on the minimap, so don't give up when the map looks like a dead end. Conrad King is back up top, on the dock south of the Piscarilius foodhall, and the kill happens whichever way you answer when asked how to deal with him. The Queen of Thieves, Lady Shauna Piscarilius, is in the tent just north of Devan Rutter.

            Before you leave the port, ask Veos on the docks for a new copy of Kharedst's memoirs: you destroyed yours after Client of Kourend in 1.2 to free a slot, and each Kourend quest you finish now adds a teleport page to it. Each quest's reward includes a torn page; use it on the book and that city becomes a teleport destination, and the page adds 20 charges. Don't recharge it at the Old Memorial: each charge there costs a law, body, mind and soul rune, and the pages' own charges are far more than the five teleports the diary needs.

            UIM risk Veos replaces the book whenever it is lost, and the lord of each house replaces a lost page.

            Take the minecart to Shayzien. Don't buy 45 lizardkickers in the Cloak and Stagger here for later. Talk to Phileas Rimor, in the house just west of the Cloak and Stagger, to start Tale of the Righteous. On the way to Lovakengj, tag a lizardman that someone else is already fighting and get the kill for the diary; your antipoison covers the poison. Talk to Lady Vulcana Lovakengj to start The Forsaken Tower. Leave the diary's volcanic sulphur for Chapter 2: it needs 42 Mining and a facemask or gas mask.

            Lizardmen are level 53 or 62, aggressive and poisonous, which is why one antipoison came with you from Karamja. Lizardman Canyon, between Shayzien and Lovakengj, is the place: Shayzien soldiers fight them in its eastern half, most of which is multicombat, so only tag one that is already busy, and leave if it turns on you. Lady Vulcana is in the Lovakengj Assembly, west of the town's southern bank. Each minecart ride costs 20 gp until The Forsaken Tower is finished, after which the whole network is free, so the rides in these three blocks are a small cost that ends with that quest.

            Done when The Queen of Thieves, Tale of the Righteous and The Forsaken Tower are started and the lizardman is tagged.

            Arceuus and Kingstown

            In Arceuus, talk to Galana in the library for The Depths of Despair, which you started in Hosidius at the top of this section, and find and read The Varlamore Envoy for it. Hand a book in at the library for the diary, putting the XP on Magic. Talk to Mori to start The Ascent of Arceuus.

            Galana is on the library's ground floor, in its north-eastern section. The Varlamore Envoy sits on a different shelf for each player, and every square of bookcase is its own shelf, so search them one at a time in the area she names. If you log out before you find it, ask her again, because the book can move. Reading it is not optional: until you have, the Crabclaw Caves refuse you entry, and they are where Kourend III finishes the quest. For the diary book, one of Sam, Professor Gracklebone or Villia asks for a title, and Biblia on the top floor narrows it to a floor and section. Magic is the better pick because a book pays 15 XP per Magic level against 5 per Runecraft level. Mori stands just north of the Arceuus pub.

            Walk south to Kingstown, pick the lock on the chest in Councillor Hughes' house for The Queen of Thieves' letter, and at the castle talk to Councillor Andrews for The Ascent of Arceuus and pray at the altar for the diary.

            Hughes is not at home, and the chest with the letter is upstairs in her house. In Kourend Castle, Councillor Andrews is on the first floor and the diary's altar is on the top floor. Andrews refuses to help, which is what the quest expects: talk to Mori again on your way back through Arceuus and she points you to Lord Arceuus and the Tower of Magic.

            Back in Arceuus, kill the five Tormented Souls (level 16) at the Tower of Magic for The Ascent of Arceuus. For Tale of the Righteous, have Archeio in the library teleport you to the Library Historical Archive and solve Pagida's puzzle: push the strange device fully west and hit it with magic from the north and melee from the south, then fully east and hit it with the shortbow from the south (NPC attack options must not be hidden). Drop the bow and arrows afterwards.

            The Tower of Magic is just west of the library, and the Tower Mage lets you in. One soul attacks you as you enter and the other four go for the mages, so deal with yours first and then help them. Archeio is on the library's ground floor. In the prison, use Fire Bolt rather than an ice spell: ice freezes the device and you can't push it until the freeze wears off. A cast can miss, so bring runes for a few. All three hits have to land in one visit; if you leave before the last one, that part starts over. When every crystal is cleansed, the north doors open: investigate the skeleton in the northern cell before you leave, because that is what you report to Phileas.

            Last, buy about 20 earth runes at Regath's Wares in Arceuus (5 gp each) and talk to Tyss at the Dark Altar to switch to the Arceuus spellbook: its Battlefront Teleport (a law, a fire and an earth rune) is how the next block reaches Mount Karuulm.

            Tyss stands by the Dark Altar just north of Arceuus. The switch costs nothing, and he lifts the spellbook again whenever you ask, which is what the end of the next block does. Battlefront Teleport needs 23 Magic and lands at the Ancient Grave north-west of the Kebos Battlefront, south-east of Mount Karuulm.

            Done when you hold the letter and the earth runes and are on the Arceuus spellbook.

            Coins: about 378,000 gp · Slots: about 25/28 at the end; about 27/28 with the stew ingredients · Requirements: 16 Strength and 10 Mining for Tale of the Righteous, 20 Thieving and 25 Cooking for The Queen of Thieves, 18 Agility for The Depths of Despair, 12 Hunter for The Ascent of Arceuus

            Wiki references Tale of the Righteous The Queen of Thieves The Forsaken Tower The Ascent of Arceuus Regath's Wares Kharedst's memoirs

            Kourend II: the Forsaken Tower, Mount Karuulm and Getting Ahead

            Gate in 30 Crafting and 26 Construction for Getting Ahead (neither boostable); 20 Fishing for the trout; 49 Agility for the boulder leap.
            Carry in about 25/28.
            Exit Getting Ahead done; The Forsaken Tower and The Ascent of Arceuus advanced; back on the standard spellbook; about 381,000 gp; about 23/28.
            Carries forward Kharedst's memoirs, the letter, the antipoison.

            The Forsaken Tower and Mount Karuulm

            Take the minecart to the Wintertodt camp and progress The Forsaken Tower. Its puzzles: start the steam generator and align the power grid as the wiki's image shows; for the furnace, end with 4 gallons of coolant in the 5-gallon jug and pour it into the coolant west of the furnace before you light it; for the potion, read the old notes to find the cleansing fluid (its position differs per player, and a wrong one hits for 25 or more and poisons, so log out before advancing the dialogue if unsure); for the altar, move the discs W to E, W to C, E to C, W to E, C to W, C to E, W to E, W to C, E to C, E to W, C to W, E to C, W to E, W to C, E to C, then take Dinh's hammer to Undor.

            At the camp, tell Undor you have been sent to help him. If he says he has a job to finish first, talk to Ignisia, east of him, and go back to him. The tower itself is not at the camp: it stands on the peninsula due west of Lovakengj, so ride the minecart to Lovakengj and walk west. Inside, inspect the display case and take the ladder north of it down to the basement; the generator crank is in the crates of the room just east of that ladder. The tinderbox and both jugs are in the cupboards of the back room, through either of its doors, and the notes are in a cupboard in the potion room on the first floor. Do the jugs before you touch the furnace, because lighting it with no coolant in place hits hard. When every lock on the display case turns green, go down and take the hammer. Undor is the one who takes it, so you hand it over the next time you pass the camp.

            Cast Battlefront Teleport and progress The Ascent of Arceuus past its Mount Karuulm step. While you're up there, mine an iron ore in the Mount Karuulm mine for the diary and drop it, and fly fish a trout in the River Molch for the diary; drop the rod and the feathers afterwards.

            The Mount Karuulm step is the elevator into the Karuulm Slayer Dungeon. The Tasakaal there ignore you until you say "Arceuus", and this part needs no special boots. The iron needs 15 Mining and any pickaxe. The River Molch runs past the Farming Guild, between its farmland and the Kebos Swamp.

            Done when The Forsaken Tower waits for its last report, The Ascent of Arceuus is past Mount Karuulm and the trout is caught.

            Getting Ahead

            Do Getting Ahead at the farm south of the Farming Guild. Everything it needs is on the farm, so collect each thing only when its step comes; the last step is the fullest your bag gets in Kourend, at 28. Gordon is outside and Mary is inside the house; Gordon pays nothing until you prove the beast is dead, and no other trophy convinces him.

            1. Talk to Gordon, then to Mary in the main building. Take the pot of flour from upstairs in the house and use it on the southern gate beside the cow pen.
            2. Follow the flour trail south over the bridge and west to the cave at the Kebos Lowlands mine, and kill the Headless Beast (level 82) with Fire Bolt and cooked swordfish. Mine one clay at the mine and pick up the two planks by the cave entrance; soften the clay with water back at the farm. The beast fights in melee and now and then slams the ground to drop a rock: step off your tile when you see it coming. The wiki's safespot is the pond in the lair; trap the beast on the other side of it and it never uses the slam. Both planks lie by the broken rowboat just west of the cave entrance. The bucket spawn and the sink are both in Mary's room.
            3. Back at the farm, talk to Gordon and Mary and use your knife on the soft clay for a clay head.
            4. Kill a bear west of the farm for its fur. Take the needle and thread from upstairs in the house and sew the fur onto the head; drop the needle and thread. A grizzly bear and a bear cub spawn west of the farm, and two black bears to the east.
            5. Take the red dye from the shelves in the house and use it on the head. The shelves are next to the stairs, and Gordon accepts only this last, bloody version of the head.
            6. Take the saw from the shed north of the house and the nails from the workbench beside it, and mount the head on the wall south of the stairs with the two planks and six nails. Talk to Gordon to finish: 1 quest point, 3,000 gp, 4,000 Crafting XP and 3,200 Construction XP. Mounting needs your hammer as well. A nail can bend while you hammer, so if you run short, go back to the workbench for more. If your bag is too full for the coins, Gordon hands them over when you talk to him again later.

            If you are a slot short at the last step, drop the tinderbox; the general store in Port Piscarilius sells another for 1 gp in the next block.

            Return to the Wintertodt camp and progress The Forsaken Tower again. This is the hammer hand-in to Undor; the minecart from the Farming Guild station takes you to the camp.

            Done when Getting Ahead is complete.

            Back to the standard spellbook

            Cast the Arceuus home teleport to the Dark Altar and progress The Ascent of Arceuus there, then talk to Tyss next to the altar to switch back to the standard spellbook. Take the boulder leap shortcut in the Arceuus essence mine (49 Agility) for the diary.

            The Arceuus home teleport needs no runes but can only be cast once every 30 minutes, and combat interrupts it. Switching back costs nothing. You go back because your Falador, Camelot and Varrock teleports for the rest of the chapter are standard spells.

            Done when you are on the standard spellbook.

            Coins: about 381,000 gp · Slots: about 23/28 at the end; 28/28 at the peak, mounting the head · Requirements: 30 Crafting and 26 Construction for Getting Ahead, 20 Fishing for the trout, 49 Agility for the boulder leap

            Wiki references Getting Ahead The Forsaken Tower Battlefront Teleport Kourend & Kebos Diary

            Kourend III: the five Kourend quests completed

            Gate in each quest's requirements; 30 Crafting and 30 Construction for the crane.
            Carry in about 23/28.
            Exit The Depths of Despair, The Queen of Thieves, The Forsaken Tower, Tale of the Righteous, The Garden of Death and The Ascent of Arceuus done; about 400,000 gp; about 21/28.
            Carries forward a fresh stack of bird house seeds, a raw shark (Olaf's lyre).

            Hosidius and Port Piscarilius

            Teleport to your house in Hosidius and refill the compost bin to the north with 15 compostable items. At the Woodcutting Guild's sawmill operator buy about 50 steel nails for the crane. In the Crabclaw Caves south of Hosidius, kill a sand crab with magic for the diary, kill the Sand Snake that guards the Royal Accord, take the accord to Lord Kandur Hosidius and complete The Depths of Despair.

            The Crabclaw Caves entrance is south of the Woodcutting Guild; don't confuse it with Crabclaw Isle. Inside, go east, south through a crevice and over the stepping stones and rocks, which is the part the 18 Agility is for. The Sand Snake is level 36, hits no more than 3 and is weak to magic, so Fire Bolt ends it quickly. Search the chest it guards for the Royal Accord of Twill. Lord Kandur is in the Hosidius family house north-west of the vinery, and his reward includes the Lunch by the Lancalliums page for the memoirs.

            In Port Piscarilius, unnote three planks at the bank and repair a crane for the diary (30 Crafting, 30 Construction, your hammer, the nails and the planks); drop the leftover nails. Complete The Queen of Thieves: give Tomas Lawry the letter, then report to Shauna in the Warrens. At Frankie's Fishing Emporium in the south-east corner of the port, buy one raw shark (170 gp): it enchants Olaf's lyre two blocks from now, and you are two Fishing levels short of catching one. Buy a tinderbox at the general store if Getting Ahead cost you yours. Unnote and cook two swordfish for the fights ahead.

            Nails of any metal work on the crane, and the three planks only have to be of one type. When you hand Tomas the letter, don't expect a reward from him: he has none for you. The reward comes from Shauna, whom you reach through the same manhole into the Warrens as before; it includes 2,000 gp, 2,000 Thieving XP and The Fisher's Flute page.

            Done when The Depths of Despair and The Queen of Thieves are complete and the crane is repaired.

            The Forsaken Tower and Tale of the Righteous

            In Arceuus, buy a rope at Regath's Wares (25 gp). Take the minecart to Lovakengj and complete The Forsaken Tower.

            Tell Phileas Rimor in Shayzien what you found in the archive, then in the Shayzien encampment talk to Lord Shayzien until he gives permission for the Mount Quidamortem expedition. At Mount Quidamortem, continue Tale of the Righteous until you've spoken to Duffy and Gnosi at the unstable altar, and talk to the Mountain Guide to unlock the quick route. You fight the Corrupt Lizardman in the red cave, and the lizardmen on the way poison: this is what the rope, the pickaxe, the swordfish and the rest of the antipoison are for. Leave the cave, take the minecart back to Shayzien, and report to Lord Shayzien, then Phileas, then Lord Shayzien again to complete Tale of the Righteous.

            The Forsaken Tower ends with Lady Vulcana in the Lovakengj Assembly, and from then on every minecart ride is free. Lord Shiro Shayzien is in the War Tent of the Shayzien Encampment, up the ladder on the first floor. At the peak of Mount Quidamortem, Historian Duffy is in the tent to the east. Use the rope on the crevice on the mountain's west side to enter the cave, mine the first boulder and push the second. The Corrupt Lizardman (level 46) appears when you walk up to the magic gate; past it, inspect the unstable altar, and Gnosi is close by. The Mountain Guide gives quick travel between the peak and Shayziens' Wall. When you go back to Phileas, his house is wrecked and he is gone; that is part of the story, so go straight back to Lord Shayzien. The quest pays 8,000 gp and 1,500 Mining XP, plus the History and Hearsay page.

            Done when The Forsaken Tower and Tale of the Righteous are complete.

            The Garden of Death and The Ascent of Arceuus

            Walk west to Lake Molch and take the boat to the island; the diary's bluegill needs 43 Fishing and 35 Hunter, so it waits until your bird runs are further on. Continue The Garden of Death on the island and at Xeric's Shrine, then use Kharedst's memoirs to teleport to Shayzien, run south and complete The Garden of Death at the Ruins of Morra.

            From Shayzien, the lake's east dock is to the north-west, near the Giant Pit. The quest's dungeons block teleports, so climb out before you use the memoirs. Lizardmen on this route have a poisonous ranged attack, and lizardman brutes and mountain trolls are about too, so keep the antipoison handy. At the Ruins of Morra, go down the hole in front of the large step pyramid; reading the last tablet and taking the warning note from the floor ends the quest, for 10,000 Farming XP.

            Complete The Ascent of Arceuus. After the Tower of Magic, talk to the Tower Mage and go upstairs to Asteros; take the Mount Karuulm elevator and talk to Kaal-Xil-Dar; find the Ancient Grave south-east of the mountain and follow the tracks, searching bushes and plants, to the Trapped Soul (level 30); talk to Kaal-Xil-Dar again, use the device at the rocks by the Dark Altar and finish with Lord Trobin upstairs in the Tower. It rewards 1 quest point, 2,000 gp, 1,500 Hunter and 500 Runecraft XP, and the page A Dark Disposition; use each quest's page on the memoirs as you get it.

            On the tracks, inspect every plant along them: miss one and the Trapped Soul never appears. It pops out of a bush, slips back in if you run out of its sight, and vanishes if the fight drags on, so stay where it can see you and keep hitting. The wiki notes you can safespot it from the far side of its bush.

            With all five city quests done, use the memoirs to teleport to each of the five cities once for the Kourend & Kebos medium diary. Each teleport uses one charge and lands you near a patch of lancalliums in that city.

            Done when every Kourend quest in this block is complete and the five teleports are used.

            Seeds for the bird runs

            Drop Kharedst's memoirs and what is left of the antipoison: the book's teleports have done their diary work, Veos hands out another copy whenever you want one, and it would cost a slot for the rest of the chapter.

            Teleport to your house and pickpocket the two Hosidius master farmers until you have about 150 low-level seeds of one or two types, dropping the other types as they come. The next block's bird runs use them.

            Done when you hold the seeds.

            Coins: about 400,000 gp with the quest rewards · Slots: about 21/28 at the end; about 27/28 at the crane · Requirements: as each quest lists; 30 Crafting and 30 Construction for the crane

            Wiki references The Depths of Despair The Queen of Thieves Tale of the Righteous The Garden of Death The Ascent of Arceuus

            70 Woodcutting and the log basket

            Gate in 35 Woodcutting for teaks; the Soul Wars minigame teleport.
            Carry in about 21/28.
            Exit 70 Woodcutting; the log basket; the beaten book for Elemental Workshop II; about 400,000 gp; about 23/28.
            Carries forward the log basket, the beaten book, the raw shark (Olaf's lyre).

            Woodcutting to 70

            Train Woodcutting to 70 on the teaks north of Soul Wars, which the Soul Wars minigame teleport puts you next to (2-tick teaks are faster, but that is tick manipulation, so it is optional), or on a Forestry world if you want more Forestry rewards. Drop the logs as you go. Wear your graceful and keep a little cooked swordfish for the events. There are three teaks on the island, each log worth 85 XP. The wiki lists the Forestry worlds as 388, 398, 434, 444, 487 and 510, and they are where you work on the bark below.

            The UIM part of this block is the log basket. It costs 5,000 anima-infused bark from Forestry events plus 300 oak and 300 willow logs: deposit the logs into the Friendly Forester's log storage as you chop them (they count in place of noted logs), then buy the basket in the Forestry shop. The bark is hours of events, so work through it on Forestry worlds as part of the Woodcutting. The basket holds 28 logs in one slot, and it is what lets a bird run use oak, willow and teak logs that don't grow next to the Digsite.

            The Forestry shop is run by the Friendly Forester, and you can buy the basket at its Draynor Village or Seers' Village branches. Bark comes from taking part in the events: chopping the roots in Rising Roots, feeding the sapling, pruning entlings and the like, each with a bonus at the end. Once you own the basket, open it and the logs you chop go into it on their own. Never use Destroy on it while it holds logs, because the contents go with it. Chapter 3 chops mahogany with the basket open for Mahogany Homes, so it stays with you from here on.

            Sulliusceps and mushroom pies are no longer part of this chapter. The pies were there to boost Crafting to 40, and the quest XP of this section (The Giant Dwarf, Getting Ahead and Elemental Workshop II) now carries you past 40 on its own.

            Done when you are 70 Woodcutting and hold the log basket.

            Bird runs and the beaten book

            Keep doing bird runs through this block, with the best logs your Hunter allows in the basket. On the last one, buy a tinderbox at the Varrock general store if Getting Ahead cost you yours, and walk from the barge to the Exam Centre south of the Digsite: search the bookcases in its library (the wiki puts the beaten book in the most north-eastern of the south-eastern bookcases), read the beaten book and the scroll inside it, drop the scroll and keep the book. It starts Elemental Workshop II in the next block.

            Reading the scroll is what later lets you search the workshop's machinery for the key, which is why you read it before dropping it. The book itself has to be in your bag at the very end of the quest, when you smith the mind helmet.

            Done when you hold the beaten book.

            Coins: about 400,000 gp · Slots: about 23/28 · Requirements: 35 Woodcutting for teaks

            Wiki references Log basket Forestry Bird house trapping Elemental Workshop II

            Rellekka and Seers' Village: The Fremennik Trials, Dwarf Cannon and Elemental Workshop II

            Section 1.3 left The Fremennik Trials with Olaf's lyre and Manni's keg. This block finishes them, takes Dwarf Cannon to its last errand and does Elemental Workshop II, whose 7,500 Smithing XP is the third quest reward on the way to 40 Smithing. Elemental Workshop II comes before the lyre on purpose: its 7,500 Crafting XP is what lifts your Crafting to the 40 the lyre needs.

            Gate in 40 Crafting (reached through Elemental Workshop II's 7,500 Crafting XP, so do that step first), 25 Fletching and 40 Woodcutting for the lyre; 20 Magic, 30 Smithing and Elemental Workshop I for Elemental Workshop II; 20 Agility for the Coal Trucks log.
            Carry in about 23/28.
            Exit The Fremennik Trials and Elemental Workshop II done; the Yrsa, fish stall and Rellekka coal Fremennik diary tasks done; Dwarf Cannon waiting for Nulodion; about 399,000 gp; about 19/28.
            Carries forward nothing new. The chisel is dropped for the workshop and the tinderbox after the Trials; both are bought back in Varrock in the next block.

            Dwarf Cannon at the Coal Trucks

            Cast Camelot Teleport and cross the log west of Seers' Village to the Coal Trucks. For Dwarf Cannon, which Captain Lawgof started for you in 1.3: repair the six bent railings on the west and south sides of his fence with your hammer and talk to him; take the dwarf remains from the top of the tower south of the gate and talk to him again; find Lollk in the cave at the south-east corner of the Fishing Guild (the north-westernmost room, the northernmost crate by the east wall) and report back; and repair the cannon with the toolkit he gives you: the pliers on the safety switch, the hook tool on the spring and the tooth tool on the gear. Finish the conversation. He now sends you to Nulodion, which is two blocks from now.

            The hammer has to be in your bag, but using it on a railing does nothing: inspect each bent railing instead. An attempt can fail and cost a hitpoint or two or briefly lower your Strength or Crafting, so keep inspecting until the chat says the railing is fixed. The dwarf remains are on the top floor of the watchtower, reached by its ladder. In the goblin cave, take the centre of the three passages north-west to the goblin outpost and keep going north-west to the room of crates. Use the three tools in the order given above, which the wiki marks as required.

            Done when the cannon is repaired and Lawgof has sent you to Nulodion.

            Elemental Workshop II

            Drop the chisel: the workshop fills your bag with parts, and Varrock sells it again in the next block. Keep the tinderbox for the Trials' strange object later in this block, and drop it there.

            The quest needs two elemental bars, and each takes one elemental ore and four coal at the workshop's furnace. So make them one at a time: mine four coal at the Coal Trucks, walk to the anvil building in Seers' Village, go through the odd-looking wall and down the stairs (the battered key from Elemental Workshop I opens the way; if you no longer have it, search the bookcase in the house south-west of the bank for the battered book again and cut the key out with your knife), mine the Elemental Rock in the earth room to the west and kill the level 35 earth elemental that appears for its ore, and smelt the bar at the furnace south of the centre room. Then fetch four more coal and make the second. With both bars, search the machinery on the south-west side of the water room, the elemental room to the north, for the key and take it down the hatch in the centre room. Then follow the wiki's steps:

            1. Take both schematics from the crate to the south, and upstairs make one crane claw at the workbench (one only). Downstairs, lower the crane with the western lever and fit the claw.
            2. On the south-west catwalk, open the junction box and connect the pipe ends.
            3. Search the crates for a pipe and the small, medium and large cogs, fitting each as you find it: the pipe on the north catwalk's broken piping; the small cog on the upper-left pin, the medium on the lower-left and the large on the right, on the east side of the ground floor. Pull the old lever.
            4. Put the second elemental bar on the jig cart under the claw. Raise the crane, rotate it, lower it into the lava twice until the bar is orange, rotate back, lower and release, and send the cart on with the lever north of the claw controls.
            5. At the press, pull the western-most lever, then the yellow lever. At the water tank: northern lever, corkscrew twice, northern lever, west valve twice, east valve twice, northern lever (the bar is blue), corkscrew twice, northern lever, yellow lever. In the wind tunnel, pull the east lever until the bar is white, pull it again to stop the fan, then the yellow lever.
            6. Take the primed bar, go down the north-west stairs and through the east door, put the bar in the extractor gun and operate the extractor hat (it drains 20 Magic). Take the primed mind bar up both spiral stairs and smith a mind helmet at the workbench with the beaten book in your inventory.

            A few things stall the machine, and each has a plain fix. At the junction box, connect the top-middle pipe to the top-right, then bottom-right to top-left and bottom-middle to bottom-left. If the bar stays on the claw after you lower it onto the cart, raise the crane once more and it drops onto the jig. If the eastern valve reports it is sucking air, the pipe on the north catwalk is missing; if the fan reports it, a cog is on the wrong pin. A cog or the pipe you lose can be taken again from the crate it came from. Without the beaten book in your bag, the last step makes a shield rather than the helmet.

            That completes Elemental Workshop II: 1 quest point, 7,500 Smithing XP and 7,500 Crafting XP. Drop the helmet, the book, the keys and the schematics.

            Done when Elemental Workshop II is complete.

            The branch and the golden fleece

            From Seers' Village, walk to the fairy ring in McGrubor's Wood (ALS), take it to DKS and walk to Rellekka. Chop a branch from the Swaying tree east of the village entrance. Talk to Lalli, south-east of the tree, and ask about the other human; get the pet rock from Askeladden outside the longhall; pick a cabbage, a potato and an onion from the patch in the village's south-east corner; give Lalli the pet rock, put the three vegetables and the rock into his stew, and talk to him again for the golden fleece. With the four stew items you are at 28 for a minute.

            The branch needs 40 Woodcutting and an ordinary axe; a blessed axe won't cut it. Lalli only mentions the other human once you have spoken to Olaf about his trial; if he doesn't, talk to Olaf first. Look after the fleece: once this trial is done, Lalli gives out no more until the whole quest is finished, and then only for 1,000 gp each.

            Cast Camelot Teleport. In Seers' Village, spin the fleece into golden wool on the spinning wheel upstairs in the house south-west of the bank, use your knife on the branch for an unstrung lyre (40 Crafting) and string it with the wool (25 Fletching). In the pub, ask the Poison Salesman about the Fremennik Trials and buy a low alcohol keg for 250 gp. The Seers' wheel is used because the one in Rellekka stays closed to you until the Trials are complete.

            Done when you hold the lyre and the low alcohol keg.

            Olaf's concert and Manni's keg

            Walk to the fairy ring in McGrubor's Wood (ALS) and take it to DKS. Walk to the bridge on the road south of the village, where the council workman stands; from its northern end, walk a little west to the narrow path that heads south into the water by the large rib cage, and use the raw shark on the strange altar south-west of Rellekka with the lyre in your inventory to enchant it. The spirit there, Fossegrimen, takes one raw shark, manta ray or sea turtle per lyre, which is why the shark from Port Piscarilius was carried this far.

            In the longhall, buy two beers. Challenge Manni the Reveller to a drinking contest with one of them and lose. Give the other to the council workman at the bridge south of the village for a strange object, light it with your tinderbox and put it in the pipe on the longhall's east outer wall. Go in by the north-eastern back door, walk onto the stage and play the enchanted lyre for Olaf's vote. Back in the main hall, take a keg of beer from the table, use the low alcohol keg on it and challenge Manni again. Then talk to Brundt the Chieftain to complete The Fremennik Trials: 3 quest points and 2,812.4 XP in each of Agility, Attack, Crafting, Defence, Fishing, Fletching, Hitpoints, Strength, Thieving and Woodcutting. Drop the lyre and the tinderbox.

            Using the low alcohol keg on it swaps their contents, and the strange object you lit goes off and distracts the hall while you do it. The Trials also open Rellekka's facilities and free trips to Waterbirth Island with Jarvald.

            That opening is what three Fremennik diary tasks from 1.3 were waiting for, so do them before you leave. Browse Yrsa's Accoutrements, west of the marketplace: her shoe store is gone, and opening her clothes shop counts. Steal once from one of the three fish stalls in the marketplace (medium, 42 Thieving, which you passed long ago) and drop the fish. Mine one coal in the mine north-east of the longhall (medium, 30 Mining) with your pickaxe and drop it.

            Done when The Fremennik Trials is complete.

            Coins: about 399,000 gp · Slots: about 19/28 at the end; about 28/28 with the second load of coal and the tinderbox, 27/28 for a minute at Lalli's stew · Requirements: 40 Crafting, 25 Fletching and 40 Woodcutting for the lyre; 20 Magic and 30 Smithing for Elemental Workshop II

            Wiki references The Fremennik Trials Poison Salesman Dwarf Cannon Elemental Workshop II

            Eadgar's Ruse

            This block finishes Eadgar's Ruse in one visit to the trolls. Its last hand-in is seventeen items (ten grain, five raw chickens, a log and a robe), so it uses a planned death on the Death Plateau path. Finishing it here also frees the two slots the ranarr and the pie half have held since the start of the section.

            Gate in Eadgar's Ruse at the parrot; 27 Herblore and the botanical pie half; a planned death on the Death Plateau path.
            Carry in about 19/28.
            Exit Eadgar's Ruse done; about 34 Herblore; about 398,000 gp; about 19/28.
            Carries forward nothing new. The Chronicle is destroyed by the death and bought back in Draynor at the start of the next block.

            Varrock, the Stronghold and the parrot

            Break a Chronicle card. In Varrock, buy a chisel and a tinderbox at the general store, and about 100 more chaos runes from Aubury if your stack is under 150. Do a bird run. Take the spirit tree at the Grand Exchange to the Tree Gnome Stronghold and buy a vodka and a pineapple from Heckel Funch on the Grand Tree's first floor. Take the spirit tree on to the Battlefield of Khazard and walk north to the Ardougne zoo.

            Talk to Parroty Pete and ask him both questions. Slice the pineapple into chunks with your knife, use the vodka on the chunks for alco-chunks, and use them on the aviary hatch on the west side of the parrot cage. Never drop the parrot: it flies away.

            Parroty Pete is by the northern wolf enclosure. The wiki's safety net only works after your first visit to Eadgar with the parrot: from then on, he can give it back to you if it gets away. Until then, keep it in your bag and go straight to Trollheim.

            Done when you hold the drunk parrot.

            Taverley and the climb

            Cast Falador Teleport and walk to Taverley. At Jatix's buy a pestle and mortar and a vial, and fill the vial at the fountain. Chop two logs. Walk to Burthorpe, buy climbing boots from Tenzing (12 gp) and wear them, and take the secret way up to Trollheim. You are at about 27 here.

            Talk to Eadgar about the fake man. Run north down both flights of stairs into the Troll Stronghold and use the parrot on the rack north of the cells, then return to Eadgar. Find the troll thistle in the grass patches by his cave entrance (it is small and grass-coloured, and only one patch has it at a time), light one log and dry the thistle on the fire, and grind it with the pestle and mortar. If you can't spot the thistle, check the grass north and west of the cave, or hop worlds and look again.

            Now make the troll potion in one go, because the boost only lasts a minute at the level you need: eat the pie half (27 + 4 is 31), use the grimy ranarr's clean leaf on the vial of water for a ranarr potion (unf), which needs 30 (clean the ranarr first; it needs 25), and add the ground thistle, which needs 31. Give Eadgar the potion and the second log; the wiki says the chicken, grain, log and robe can each be handed over separately. Drop the pestle and mortar.

            Done when Eadgar has the troll potion and a log, and the parrot is on the rack.

            Planned death: the Death Plateau path

            Go back down the secret way to Tenzing's house and walk round to the main path that the Imperial Guard watches, where the trolls on the plateau throw rocks at anyone who walks up. Take off everything except the climbing boots and let them kill you; the pile forms on the path, which you can reach again without boots. The death destroys the Chronicle.

            In Lumbridge, use the Burthorpe Games Room minigame teleport. In Taverley, get the dirty robe from Tegid, south-east of the herblore shop, and pick ten grain in the field south-west of him. Walk to your pile and take only the climbing boots, your coins and your rune sword. Kill five chickens at Tenzing's house for five raw chickens.

            Climb to Eadgar and give him the robe, the grain and the chickens. Fetch the parrot from the rack in the Stronghold, talk to Eadgar again for the fake man, and give it to Burntmeat in the kitchen. Search the kitchen drawers by the south wall for the storeroom key, go down the stairs to the storeroom, sneak past the guards to the back and take one goutweed, clicking the tile beside it, not the plant. One is all this chapter needs.

            The guards walk in a cycle of four. Run for the goutweed crate as the two nearest guards pass each other. If one spots you, it throws a rock for up to 6 damage and puts you back at the entrance; protection prayers don't stop it, but the cycle carries on where it was, so try again. You are sent out after one goutweed either way.

            Leave by the secret way, pick up your pile (you have an hour from the death; the whole loop takes about forty minutes, so don't stop on the way), put the graceful boots back on and drop the climbing boots. Give the goutweed to Sanfew in Taverley to complete Eadgar's Ruse: 1 quest point, 11,000 Herblore XP and the Trollheim Teleport spell (61 Magic). In his dialogue, choose to ask another question, not to talk about sanfew serum, to hand it in. The spell is how Chapter 2 gets back to Trollheim for Troll Romance without the climbing boots.

            If something goes wrong on the way back and you die a second time while the pile is still on the path, the wiki says the time left from the first death is what counts, not a fresh hour. So a second death doesn't buy you time; get to the pile first.

            Done when Eadgar's Ruse is complete and your pile is collected.

            Coins: about 398,000 gp · Slots: about 19/28 at the end; about 27/28 on the first climb · Requirements: 31 Herblore (the pie half boosts 27), Troll Stronghold and Druidic Ritual

            Wiki references Eadgar's Ruse Botanical pie Ultimate Ironman Guide/Item Management

            Garden of Tranquillity I: the farming tour

            Garden of Tranquillity sends you round the farmers of Gielinor with three crops that each take 20 to 40 minutes to grow, so this block plants all three first and fills the waiting time with Plague City. Everything the quest plants or digs with lives with the tool leprechaun, not in your bag.

            Gate in 25 Farming (not boostable), Creature of Fenkenstrain, Priest in Peril and The Restless Ghost for Garden of Tranquillity; the ring of charos; the fairy rings.
            Carry in about 20/28.
            Exit the delphinium, snowdrop and orchid seeds; Plague City done; about 395,000 gp; about 27/28.
            Carries forward the three seed types, two plant cures, a pure essence, a filled plant pot, the watering can, a fishing rod and the magic secateurs, all for the next block.

            Varrock and Draynor

            Cast Varrock Teleport and talk to Queen Ellamaria in the palace garden to start Garden of Tranquillity. Do a bird run. Cast Lumbridge Home Teleport and walk to Draynor: buy the Chronicle back from Diango (300 gp) and wear it, and at Olivia's seed stall buy two marigold seeds, six cabbage seeds and six onion seeds, which the wiki's ironman note confirms she sells.

            With the ring of charos on, talk to the Wise Old Man in his house. He quizzes you: answer "Show them a range of colours", "Take his generous gift", "It's absolutely…", "Put on the silly helmet and jump", "You of course", "Ask me nicely" and "No, especially", and he turns the ring into a ring of charos (a). From now on, use the Charm answers with each farmer until they make their request, then accept with the plain answer, and plant only after the request: a crop planted earlier doesn't count.

            Six of the seven questions need these answers, and the seventh accepts anything. The plain answer at the end matters: pick a Charm option when you agree to grow a crop and the farmer refuses, and you have to agree again anyway. If you lose the delphinium or vine seeds, Elstan or Bernald hands out more when you talk to them again. The crops take 20 to 40 minutes, which is why this block plants and then leaves.

            Done when you wear the ring of charos (a) and hold the three seed types.

            Falador and Port Sarim

            Walk to the farm south of Falador. At Sarah's Farming Shop buy a watering can, a gardening trowel, a spade and two plant cures. Talk to Elstan at the allotment, take a bucket of ultracompost and the rake and seed dibber from the tool leprechaun, compost the flower patch and plant a marigold seed. Hand the leprechaun the spade and the tools you aren't using; from here on he carries the rake, dibber, spade, trowel and watering can for you, and every patch on this tour has one.

            Walk south to Port Sarim and buy a chocolate bar at Wydin's food store, then west past the Crafting Guild to the snape grass spawn on the coast and pick one. They are two of the three ingredients of Plague City's hangover cure.

            Done when the marigold is planted and you hold the chocolate bar and the snape grass.

            Catherby

            Cast Camelot Teleport and walk to Catherby. Talk to Dantaera at the allotment: she sends you for a white tree shoot, which is the next block. At Vanessa's farming shop buy a plant pot, take the trowel from the leprechaun and fill the pot with soil at the allotment; it is for the white tree. Take the watering can too and keep it for the same job. Buy a fishing rod at Harry's Fishing Shop (5 gp); it fishes your ring out of a well in the next block.

            Done when you hold a filled plant pot, the watering can and the fishing rod.

            Ardougne: the cabbages and Plague City

            Walk west to the fairy ring in McGrubor's Wood, picking one sprig of dwellberries from the spawn in the wood on the way, and take ring ALS to BIS, the Ardougne zoo. Stamp the barcrawl card at the Flying Horse Inn.

            North of the city, talk to Kragen at the allotment, compost both allotment patches and plant three cabbage seeds in each. Use an empty bucket from the leprechaun on the dairy cow beside the farm, grind the chocolate bar with your knife, and add the chocolate dust and then the snape grass to the bucket of milk: that is the hangover cure. Take the magic secateurs back from the leprechaun here. Add the dust and the grass by using one item on the other: a left-click on the chocolate bar or the chocolatey milk eats or drinks it.

            Do Plague City while the cabbages grow:

            1. Talk to Edmond in the north-westernmost house of East Ardougne, then to Alrena, who takes the dwellberries and gives you the gas mask; take the picture of Elena from the table and talk to Edmond again.
            2. Use four buckets of water on the mud patch behind the house (a bucket spawns there and the water is beside it), dig with the spade that spawns near Edmond, go south to the farthest pipe, try the grill, and tie a rope to it; the general store by the market sells one. Talk to Edmond.
            3. Wear the gas mask and climb the pipe. Talk to Jethick, east, for a book; take it to the Rehnisons' house in the north and talk to Milli upstairs; try the door of the big house with the X south of the square; get a warrant by talking to the Clerk and then Bravek in the civic building north of the square, and use the hangover cure on Bravek.
            4. Back at the X house, search the barrel for the key, go downstairs and free Elena. Return through the sewer to Edmond to finish: 1 quest point, 2,425 Mining XP and a scroll that unlocks Ardougne Teleport when you read it. Read it.

            If you lose the gas mask before you climb into the pipe, Alrena's cupboard holds spares. Bravek gives you the warrant once he has the cure and you tell him the mourners won't listen to you. The barrel with the key is west of the staircase in the X house.

            Drop the gas mask: Desert Treasure's smoke dungeon takes a 200 gp facemask from a Slayer master just as well. Have Wizard Cromperty, north of the market, teleport you to the essence mine for the diary, and mine one pure essence there for the next block's rune dust. Pickpocket a master farmer north of the city for the diary.

            When the cabbages are grown, harvest them, talk to Kragen for the snowdrop seeds and drop the cabbages.

            Done when Plague City is complete and you hold the snowdrop seeds and one essence.

            Lyra's onions, the marigolds and the willows

            Take ring BIS to ALQ and walk to the allotment west of Port Phasmatys. Talk to Lyra, compost both patches and plant three onion seeds in each.

            While they grow, cast Falador Teleport. At the farm south of the city, pick your marigolds and give them to Elstan for the delphinium seeds.

            Cast Camelot Teleport, walk to ring ALS and return to ALQ. Harvest the onions, talk to Lyra for the orchid seeds and drop the onions. Hand the leprechaun everything he stores.

            Done when you hold the delphinium, snowdrop and orchid seeds.

            Coins: about 395,000 gp · Slots: about 27/28 at the end and in Ardougne · Requirements: 25 Farming for Garden of Tranquillity; none for Plague City

            Wiki references Garden of Tranquillity Ring of charos (a) Plague City Tool Leprechaun

            Garden of Tranquillity II: the vines, the roses and the white tree

            This block collects the last three plants and finishes the quest. It runs west to east, Burthorpe to Edgeville to Ice Mountain to Varrock, and your bag is at its fullest of the whole section just before the planting, so nothing else is added to it.

            Gate in the three seed types, two plant cures, an essence, a filled plant pot, the watering can, the fishing rod and the magic secateurs.
            Carry in about 27/28.
            Exit Garden of Tranquillity done; about 395,000 gp; about 20/28.
            Carries forward the ring of charos (a), worn in place of the ring of dueling whenever a charter or a fee is involved.

            Burthorpe: the vines

            Use the Burthorpe Games Room minigame teleport. Talk to Bernald in the garden behind the Toad and Chicken and use one plant cure on his vines; it doesn't work. Walk to Taverley and ask Alain at the tree patch about a stronger cure, and buy a pestle and mortar at Jatix's. Use your hammer on the essence for rune shards and the pestle and mortar on the shards for rune dust, add the dust to the second plant cure, and use the strong plant cure on the vines. Bernald gives you the vine seeds. Drop the pestle and mortar. With Alain, use the plain answers: a Charm option makes him refuse to help. The essence you mined in Ardougne is what becomes the rune dust here.

            Done when you hold the vine seeds.

            Edgeville: the roses

            Cast Camelot Teleport, walk to ring ALS and take it to DKR, east of Edgeville; walk west to the Edgeville Monastery. Talk to Brother Althric in the garden. Throw the ring of charos (a) down the well in Edgeville as he asks, take four seeds each from the red, pink and white rose bushes in the monastery garden, and fish the ring back out of the well with the fishing rod. Drop the rod. Throw the ring only when Althric asks for it. Once it is in the well, nothing but your own fishing rod gets it back; an oily or barbarian rod won't do. If the ring is destroyed some other way, Dr Fenkenstrain can be pickpocketed for a replacement.

            Done when you hold the three kinds of rose seed and wear the ring again.

            Ice Mountain: the white tree

            Walk west past Barbarian Village to Ice Mountain. Use the magic secateurs on the white tree in the north of the mountain for a shoot, plant it in your filled plant pot and water it with the watering can you kept from the last leprechaun. Drop the can.

            Done when you hold the white tree sapling.

            Varrock: the statues and the garden

            Cast Varrock Teleport. Ask Queen Ellamaria about the statues for a trolley. The wiki's steps: at Lumbridge Castle use the trolley on the king's statue, push it east over the bridge and place it on the garden's eastern plinth; at Falador square use the trolley on the Saradomin statue, push it north through the gate and place it on the southern plinth. Do each without teleporting once the statue is on the trolley and without stopping for more than five minutes, or the statue resets. Walk to Lumbridge and Falador for them: this is twenty minutes of pushing. If the five minutes run out, the statue is taken back to where it stood and you need a new trolley from Ellamaria. A random event that teleports you away counts as teleporting too. If the trolley gets stuck, logging out and back in frees it.

            Back in the garden, take two buckets of ultracompost and the tools from the leprechaun at the Varrock Castle tree patch, fill the two orchid pots with the compost (a bottomless bucket doesn't count), and plant everything: the roses, the delphiniums, the snowdrops, the orchids, the vines and the white tree. The crops take 10 to 15 minutes and can't die. Talk to Ellamaria, then to King Roald with the ring of charos (a) on, and watch the cutscene to complete Garden of Tranquillity. After the cutscene, stay by the queen and don't open any interface, or you have to fetch the king again. The quest pays 2 quest points, 5,000 Farming XP and the ring of charos (a) for good. Pick a white tree fruit in the garden for the diary. Drop the apple tree seed, the acorn, the guam seeds and the compost potion it rewards, or plant what your patches can take.

            Hand the magic secateurs to the leprechaun at the tree patch.

            Done when Garden of Tranquillity is complete.

            Coins: about 395,000 gp · Slots: about 20/28 at the end; 28/28 with all seven plants, just before the garden · Requirements: 25 Farming
