            Wiki references Garden of Tranquillity Strong plant cure White tree

            Edgeville and Ice Mountain: Enter the Abyss, the Wilderness tasks and Dwarf Cannon

            This block does A Soul's Bane, finishes Enter the Abyss, does three Wilderness diary tasks from one planned deathpile, clears the Stronghold of Security and completes Dwarf Cannon. Below Ice Mountain, Black Knights' Fortress and Dragon Slayer I's Oracle used to be here: the first two are Chapter 2's now, and the Oracle is in the chapter's last block, so that no map piece is carried.

            Gate in Enter the Abyss started; 5 Runecraft for water runes, 9 for earth runes; 31 Prayer for the monastery's upper floor; a planned death at the Edgeville Monastery.
            Carry in about 20/28.
            Exit A Soul's Bane, Enter the Abyss and Dwarf Cannon done; the Abyss and team cape diary tasks done; the Stronghold of Security cleared; about 405,000 gp; about 20/28.
            Carries forward nothing new. The Chronicle is destroyed by the death and bought back in Draynor later.

            A Soul's Bane and Enter the Abyss

            In Varrock, do a bird run. A Soul's Bane starts with a rope used on the rift, and you hold none now, so back at the Digsite pickpocket the workmen (25 Thieving) until one gives you a rope, about one try in eleven, and drop the brushes, buckets, gloves and spades that come with it. Then walk to the rift south-east of the Digsite for A Soul's Bane: in Anger use the rack's sword on unicorns, spear on bears, mace on giant rats and battleaxe on goblins; in Fear search the holes clockwise, never the same one twice; in Confusion find the beast that takes damage; in Hopelessness kill each creature three times, then finish the dialogue before you attack Tolna's three heads. Re-enter the rift afterwards for the Varrock diary.

            Launa waits by the rift east of Varrock, just south of the Earth Altar. Once the rope is set up on the rift, you climb down it from then on, so the diary's re-entry needs no second rope. Logging out throws you out of the rift, but rooms you have finished stay finished. In Hopelessness, don't eat during the fight, because eating heals the creatures. Tolna's room is multicombat and the three heads attack together; after each head dies he turns human for a moment, and talking to him then takes you back up.

            UIM risk The quest is instanced and not safe: anything you drop inside is gone for good, so drop nothing.

            Back in the city, talk to the Mage of Zamorak in the Chaos Temple for the scrying orb. It needs three teleports to the essence mine from three different wizards, with the orb in your inventory, and you leave by the portal each time: Aubury in Varrock; Brimstail in his cave, by the spirit tree from the Grand Exchange; and Wizard Cromperty in Ardougne, with the Ardougne Teleport you unlocked in Plague City. Mine two pure essence on the last visit. Cast Varrock Teleport and give the Mage of Zamorak the orb to complete Enter the Abyss: 1,000 Runecraft XP, the small pouch and the Abyss. Drop the pouch; if you want one again when Runecraft training resumes in Chapter 2, the Dark Mage in the Abyss replaces a lost small pouch.

            The Chaos Temple is south-east of Aubury's shop. The mage won't talk while you wear a Saradomin or Guthix item, so take any off first; keeping them in the bag is fine. The orb can't be dropped, only destroyed, so it can't slip out of your bag by accident. The Abyss access is used again in Chapter 2: Temple of the Eye needs Enter the Abyss and brings you back to this mage in the chapel.

            Done when A Soul's Bane and Enter the Abyss are complete and you hold two pure essence.

            The Stronghold of Security

            Walk west towards Barbarian Village. In the Edgeville Dungeon on the way, take a Slayer task from Vannaka and squeeze through the obstacle pipe into the sewers (51 Agility), both for the diary; at the canoe station by the river, make a waka canoe for the diary (57 Woodcutting) without riding it. Enter the Stronghold of Security and clear all four floors, then perform the four emotes for the diary. The reward chests that teach the emotes only open with two-factor authentication on your account. Keep the 10,000 gp and drop the boots.

            The entrance is in the middle of the Barbarian Village mine. Each room has two sets of doors with a safe space between them, and the second door can ask an account-security question that you must answer correctly to pass. From the second floor down the monsters are aggressive, and the giant spiders on the third floor attack at any combat level, so keep food to hand. The chests on the first three floors pay 2,000, 3,000 and 5,000 gp, which is the 10,000 you keep, and the third floor's chest also restores your stats when you open it.

            Done when the four floors are cleared.

            Planned death: the Edgeville Monastery

            Walk north to the Edgeville Monastery. The wiki lists its Kharid scorpion as a UIM deathpile spot: it sits on the upper floor, which Abbot Langley opens at 31 Prayer, and Holy Grail and Mountain Daughter put you well past that. Take off everything and keep trying to pick the scorpion up: each attempt stings you for 3. The death destroys the Chronicle. The sting isn't poisonous and protection prayers don't reduce it, so it is a steady, predictable way down that doesn't need your gloves off.

            You respawn in Lumbridge with nothing. Use the Soul Wars minigame teleport and the portal back to Edgeville, and pick up from the pile only your pickaxe, one pure essence and 50 gp.

            Done when your pile is at the monastery and you hold the pickaxe, an essence and 50 gp.

            Three diary tasks in the Wilderness

            Walk north into the Wilderness. Buy a team cape from Richard and put it on once you are inside the Wilderness, which is what the diary counts. Mine an iron ore near the Mage of Zamorak, then have him teleport you into the Abyss. Leave through the water rift and craft the essence into water runes for the diary. Leave through the altar's portal, walk back to the pile for the second essence, and repeat through the earth rift for the earth runes task. If anything goes wrong past the ditch, all you lose is a pickaxe.

            The Mage of Zamorak stands by the South Wilderness mine. His teleport drains your Prayer to 0 and skulls you, and the Abyss's outer ring is a multicombat area full of aggressive creatures, so don't linger. The nearest way through to the inner ring is always the rock or the gap; the rock takes your pickaxe, and the chance to pass rises with your Mining level, so a failure just means another try. The team cape only counts if you put it on once you are past the ditch: walking in already wearing it does nothing. If a player hits you at any point, log out before you go back to the pile; the wiki warns that their kill credit can stick to a later death even when something else finishes you.

            Walk back to the pile and pick everything up.

            Done when the three Wilderness tasks are done and your pile is collected.

            Dwarf Cannon

            Walk west to Ice Mountain and talk to Nulodion at the Dwarven Black Guard camp on its south side for the ammo mould and the instruction notes. He is in the camp's western building. Cast Camelot Teleport, cross the log to the Coal Trucks and give both to Captain Lawgof to complete Dwarf Cannon: 1 quest point and 750 Crafting XP.

            Done when Dwarf Cannon is complete.

            Coins: about 405,000 gp with the Stronghold's reward · Slots: about 20/28 · Requirements: 5 and 9 Runecraft for the two Abyss tasks; 31 Prayer for the monastery's upper floor

            Wiki references Enter the Abyss Stronghold of Security Abyss Wilderness Diary Dwarf Cannon Ultimate Ironman Guide/Item Management

            Keldagrim: Forgettable Tale and 40 Smithing

            Forgettable Tale of a Drunken Dwarf is two waits of fifteen to twenty minutes with a Blast Furnace next door, so this block does the whole quest and uses the waits to smelt the last stretch to 40 Smithing, the level your gold bars need in the Al Kharid block.

            Gate in The Giant Dwarf, Fishing Contest, 22 Cooking and 17 Farming for Forgettable Tale; 30 Smithing for steel bars.
            Carry in about 22/28 with the barley malt.
            Exit Forgettable Tale done; 40 Smithing; about 335,000 gp; about 20/28.
            Carries forward nothing new.

            Lumbridge: the barley malt

            Cast Lumbridge Home Teleport. At the hops patch north of the castle, pick two barley from the plants you left in the Lumbridge block, take the seed dibber from the leprechaun and keep it, and cook the two barley into barley malt on the castle range. Keldagrim has no range, which is why the malt is made here. Keldagrim has no tool leprechaun either, which is why the dibber comes with you.

            Done when you hold two barley malt and the seed dibber.

            The four kelda seeds

            Use the Blast Furnace minigame teleport. Talk to Commander Veldaban at the Black Guard headquarters west of the bank to start Forgettable Tale of a Drunken Dwarf. He takes you to the Drunken Dwarf's house in East Keldagrim: buy two beers at the Laughing Miner just south of it and give him one. He wants kelda stout instead, and gives you the first kelda seed. The other three:

            • The Rowdy Dwarf, who circles the Laughing Miner, wants a random item. Ask again after a few minutes until it is one you can make on the spot: ashes (burn a log) or the broken glass on the floor of the Drunken Dwarf's house.
            • Gauss, in the King's Axe Inn south of the west bank: toast with him using your second beer, which isn't used up.
            • Khorvak, in the pub under White Wolf Mountain: ride the minecart there, and either promise to bring the seed back or give him a dwarven stout from a table.

            The Rowdy Dwarf turns down a few odd items even when they seem to match, such as the Draynor Manor cabbage or certain flyers, so stick to the ashes or the broken glass. Gauss's toast leaves your beer in your bag, which is why one beer covers both him and the stout's last step.

            Done when you hold four kelda seeds.

            The hops, the furnace and the brew

            At the hops patch in front of the palace in West Keldagrim, ask Rind the gardener for permission, weed the patch with the rake he gives you and plant the seeds with your dibber. They take 15 to 20 minutes, and logging out resets the timer.

            Use that wait at the Blast Furnace. You should be about 39 Smithing after Sleeping Giants, The Giant Dwarf, The Forsaken Tower and Elemental Workshop II. Pay the foreman (2,500 gp per 10 minutes below 60 Smithing; the ring of charos (a) halves it) and put about 50,000 gp in the coffer, which pays the dwarves while you are in the room; you take back what is left. Buy iron ore and coal from Ordan in equal numbers, as many pairs as your free slots allow, put them on the conveyor, and collect the steel bars after cooling the dispenser with a bucket of water from the sink beside it. Each bar is 17.5 Smithing XP. Drop the bars as you go: nothing in this chapter uses them, and Desert Treasure's six are made in Al Kharid on the day they are handed in. Use a Blast Furnace world, where the dwarves run the machine.

            Pick your world before you pay the foreman and stay on it: the fee doesn't carry over if you hop. Put the coal on the belt before the iron ore, or the furnace makes iron bars instead of steel. The coffer is only drawn on while you are in the basement on a Blast Furnace world, at 1,200 gp a minute. The two waits keep you there for 30 to 40 minutes, which is 36,000 to 48,000 gp, plus a 1,250 gp foreman fee for each 10 minutes with the ring on; check the coffer each time you come back from the hops and the vat.

            When the hops are ready, pick them, buy two buckets at Gunslik's and fill them at the Blast Furnace's sink, and climb to the fermenting vat in the Laughing Miner. Add the two buckets of water, the two barley malt and the kelda hops, in that order, then take the pot from the table beside the vat and have Blandebir fill it with ale yeast for 25 gp, and add that. The brew takes another 15 to 20 minutes: go back to the furnace and stop when you reach 40 Smithing, at most about 205 bars from a level 39 that has just started.

            Done when the stout is brewed and you are 40 Smithing.

            The Red Axe tunnel

            Drink your beer for an empty beer glass, turn the valve behind the vat's barrel and fill the glass. Give the kelda stout to the Drunken Dwarf and ask about the Red Axe. Talk to the southernmost cart conductor about the closed tunnel, then to the director of the company you joined in The Giant Dwarf, upstairs in the market, and ride the southernmost cart with both hands empty and two free slots. The director has to have the boards taken down first, which is why you visit him. If you log out in the tunnel you are put back in the city, but your progress is kept.

            Rooms 1, 3 and 5 are cart-track puzzles: take square stones from the box and set the junctions so the cart follows the path the room shows (one click sends the cart along the top path, two along the bottom, three removes the stone). In room 2 listen to Hreidmar and crawl through the far hole; in room 4 search the crates and the bookcase until you have read the letters; room 6 is a cutscene that sends you back to the city. Don't chase the chests: the top one in room 1 can't be reached and the top one in room 3 is empty. Room 4 won't let you on until you have searched a bookcase.

            Buy a kebab at Kjut's Kebabs for 1 gp and a beer at the pub, talk to Commander Veldaban, and then eat the kebab or drink the beer in the Laughing Miner to finish (in that order: eating or drinking before you speak to Veldaban doesn't start the last cutscene): 2 quest points, 5,000 Cooking XP and 5,000 Farming XP. Drop the two dwarven stouts and the seed dibber, or hand the dibber to the next leprechaun you pass.

            Done when Forgettable Tale of a Drunken Dwarf is complete.

            Coins: about 335,000 gp after the ore (about 18,000), the foreman (about 5,000) and about 48,000 of coffer time · Slots: about 20/28 at the end · Requirements: The Giant Dwarf, Fishing Contest, 22 Cooking and 17 Farming for Forgettable Tale; 30 Smithing for steel

            Wiki references Forgettable Tale of a Drunken Dwarf Blast Furnace Ordan Gunslik's Assorted Items

            The Tithe Farm to 62 Farming and Reward Cart batch 3

            Do the Tithe Farm until 62 Farming. Its points buy the seed box and the auto-weed unlock (the herb sack needs 58 Herblore, which you don't have yet, so keep the last 250 points unspent; Chapter 2 buys the sack once your Herblore reaches 58). Watch the inventory here: carrying 15 watering cans would eat it. Buy Gricoller's can with your first 200 points (it holds 1,000 charges against a watering can's 8), use the 20x5 layout (no stamina potions needed), and keep only the seed dibber, spade, the can and the seeds while you farm; the leprechaun by the farm hands you the dibber and spade. Points come at 1 per 3 fruit plus 2 per full sack of 100 (100 fruit is 35 points), so the can, auto-weed, seed box and the herb-sack reserve (750 points) take about 2,150 fruit. Water each plant before its first, second and third minute; the 75th fruit in a sack gives a large XP bonus; leaving by the door removes unsacked fruit and seeds, and logging out loses the plants.

            The farm is directly north of the Hosidius South minecart station; once you have been inside, the Tithe Farm minigame teleport takes you straight there. The seed table gives golovanova seeds from 34 Farming and bologano from 54. Switch to bologano as soon as you reach 54, because its 75th-fruit bonus is 3,500 XP against golovanova's 1,500; you take one kind per visit, so leave and come back in to change. A plant is fully grown three minutes after planting, and a grown plant can't die, so only the three waterings need watching. Gricoller's can refills at any water source, and Farmer Gricoller's shop also refills it for free. The leprechaun inside the farm keeps it in his watering can slot when you leave.

            Gate in 34 Farming for the Tithe Farm; 45 for the Farming Guild.
            Carry in about 20/28.
            Exit 62 Farming; the seed box and auto-weed; 250 points kept for the herb sack; the magic and mahogany log stacks; limpwurt growing; about 325,000 gp; about 24/28.
            Carries forward 250 Tithe Farm points (the herb sack in 2.2), the seed box, the noted magic logs (Desert Treasure), the noted mahogany logs (Chapter 2), noted guam (Watchtower), noted limpwurt roots as they come (Temple of Ikov). Gricoller's can stays with the leprechaun.

            When you have the seed box, put your bird house seeds in it, walk to the Wintertodt camp and search the Reward Cart for its last rolls, until you hold 22 mahogany logs and 16 magic logs (how many searches that takes is an estimate that varies per account). Herbs and gems come noted, one slot per type, and can't go in a herb sack or a gem bag; herb seeds go in the seed box, and ores and essence stay noted. Keep the noted magic logs (Desert Treasure wants twelve, and the other four are for later quests), the noted mahogany logs (Chapter 2 wants 22: 20 for the Varrock diary planks and 2 for the lectern), the herb seeds, and the noted guam for Watchtower's potion; drop the rest, however much it is worth. Herbs you harvest yourself go in your inventory: clean them and use them for potions as you go, because the herb sack that would hold them is not yours until Chapter 2.

            The Reward Cart stands just outside the Wintertodt camp bank. What each search gives is decided when you search, using your skill levels at that moment, so the higher your levels when you search, the better the tiers: mahogany logs need 25 Woodcutting and magic logs 50, and both come noted, 10 to 20 at a time. Big-search takes five rewards a click. The seed box holds six seed types, so choose which ones earn a place; with the box open, seeds you pick up or pickpocket from master farmers go straight into it. The wiki notes that the box's own death protection doesn't apply to Ultimate Ironmen, so it goes into a deathpile like everything else.

            Then start farming contracts at the Farming Guild (the Wintertodt minecart reaches it). On your first visit, chop mahogany north of the guild for the diary. The standard long farming notes apply; the UIM points are that the seed box carries your farming seeds, and that the tool leprechaun holds your tools and compost between runs.

            Guildmaster Jane hands out the contracts: easy ones from 45 Farming, medium from 65 and hard from 85. A crop you planted before she assigned it still counts as long as you haven't checked its health yet, so a patch already growing the right thing can finish the contract early. If she gives you something you can't grow yet, ask for something easier; easy contracts themselves can't be cancelled. The mahogany trees you chop for the diary are the same ones Chapter 3 uses for Mahogany Homes planks.

            One UIM milestone sits just past this section's 62 Farming: Hespori. At 65 Farming, and once a farming contract seed pack gives you a Hespori seed, grow it in the Farming Guild. So it is storage for safe skilling; quests and fights keep using deathpiles. The patch is in a cave in the guild's west wing, the seed takes 22 to 32 hours to grow, and Hespori is a level 284 boss. Arno is the one who runs the retrieval service.

            UIM risk Its item retrieval service is the wiki's top pick for UIMs: die in Hespori's fight and everything you carry is stored until you collect it (25,000 gp a collection), but any unsafe death while items are stored deletes them.

            Start one more crop now: Temple of Ikov wants 20 limpwurt roots. Pickpocket the Hosidius master farmers for seven or so limpwurt seeds, grow them in the flower patches your contracts and runs pass (26 Farming, about three roots a seed), and use each harvest on the tool leprechaun, who turns produce into banknotes: twenty noted roots are one slot.

            Done when you are 62 Farming with 250 points kept.

            Coins: about 325,000 gp · Slots: about 24/28 with the seed box and the noted stacks · Requirements: 34 Farming for the Tithe Farm; 45 for the Farming Guild

            Wiki references Tithe Farm Herb sack Seed box Farming contract

            Knives, Ranged and Imp Catcher

            Gate in 50 Thieving and 50 Agility for the Rogues' Den; none for Imp Catcher.
            Carry in about 24/28.
            Exit Imp Catcher done; 43 Ranged; a stack of throwing knives; about 323,000 gp; about 25/28.
            Carries forward the knives (Temple of Ikov and Watchtower).

            Use the Burthorpe Games Room minigame teleport, go down into the Rogues' Den under the Toad and Chicken and buy knives from Martin Thwait, hopping worlds for more (15 bronze, 10 iron and 5 steel a world, a few hundred in total). Knives stack, so this is one of the few big buys that suits a UIM. Two later quests depend on them: the Fire Warrior in Temple of Ikov and Gorad in Watchtower are both fights this guide does with thrown knives.

            Wield the knives. Take ring ALS from Camelot to BIS and kill imps around Ardougne and the Tower of Life south of it for all four Imp Catcher beads (black, yellow, red and white, each 5/128 per imp). The wiki's densest group is east of the Ardougne Monastery, with twelve spawns. Imps are level 2 and never attack you, but every so often one vanishes in a puff of smoke and reappears a short way off, which is why the knives beat walking up and hitting them. Ring DIS takes you to the Wizards' Tower: complete Imp Catcher by handing Wizard Mizgog all four beads at once, for 1 quest point, 875 Magic XP and an amulet of accuracy; wear it, and drop the ghostspeak amulet it replaces: nothing else in this chapter needs it, and Chapter 2 collects a new one. Mizgog is on the top floor of the tower, and he won't take the beads one at a time, so keep any spare beads until you hold all four colours. If you lose it later, Mizgog makes another from a fresh set of the four beads.

            Use the Shades of Mort'ton minigame teleport and throw knives at Loar shades until 43 Ranged, safespotting them from the stone stands by the funeral pyres (behind the west pyre in the south-west, or behind the buildings in the centre; shades float over water and climb fenced bridges, so check each spot) and picking up your knives as you go; steel first, then iron, then bronze. Loar shades are level 40, aggressive and notice you from further away than most monsters, even around corners, so pick your spot before you start throwing. That also covers the 40 Ranged the Ranging Guild wants in Temple of Ikov's block, and the quest's own 10,500 Ranged XP takes you further.

            Done when Imp Catcher is complete and you are 43 Ranged.

            Coins: about 323,000 gp · Slots: about 25/28 · Requirements: 50 Thieving and 50 Agility for the Rogues' Den

            Wiki references Martin Thwait's Lost and Found Imp Catcher Loar Shade

            Al Kharid: jewellery and blackjacking to 85 Thieving

            This block makes the few pieces of jewellery a UIM keeps, and then earns Chapter 2's money.

            Gate in 40 Smithing for gold bars; 38 Crafting for the diary coif; 27 Crafting and 27 Magic for the rings of dueling; 65 Thieving and the full rogue outfit for blackjacking.
            Carry in about 25/28.
            Exit two fresh rings of dueling and a games necklace; 85 Thieving; about 1,420,000 gp; about 27/28, which the end of the next block brings down.
            Carries forward the Chapter 2 money.

            Jewellery

            Jewellery doesn't stack, so make what you will use and no more. Start with the diary coif, which needs 38 Crafting (Elemental Workshop II and the Trials have given you that): kill a cow in the pen east of the river and have Ellis the tanner turn the cowhide into soft leather (1 gp), buy a needle and a thread at Dommik's Crafting Store, and craft a coif back inside the Lumbridge cow pen from the leather menu; the diary wants a coif, not a cowl, and this is a Lumbridge & Draynor medium task. Drop the coif, the needle and the thread before you buy any gems. Use the last charge of your ring of dueling to the Emir's Arena. In the Al Kharid mine, mine three gold ores and smelt them at the furnace. Watchtower's gold bar is made later, in the Rimmington mine after Biohazard, so it doesn't take a slot through the two blocks before it. At the gem trader buy two cut emeralds and one cut sapphire, and at Dommik's a ring mould and a necklace mould. The gem trader, in the north of Al Kharid, holds only one cut emerald per world and takes hours to restock it, so hop once for the second. Make two emerald rings (27 Crafting) and a sapphire necklace (22), drop the moulds, and buy cosmic runes at Ali Morrisane's rune shop: Lvl-2 Enchant on the rings makes two rings of dueling (27 Magic), and Lvl-1 Enchant on the necklace makes a games necklace. Wear one ring and carry the other and the necklace. The games necklace's Burthorpe and Wintertodt camp teleports replace two minigame teleports you have been waiting on.

            The ring's Ferox Enclave charge is the one the Wilderness Prayer method below leans on, and Chapter 2 uses both items again: the ring for Emir's Arena on almost every Al Kharid visit, and the necklace for Burthorpe and the Corporeal Beast.

            The digsite pendants, the amulet of magic and the binding necklace this block used to make are gone: nothing in the chapter needs them. The amulet of strength waits for Chapter 2, as before.

            Done when you hold two rings of dueling and a games necklace.

            Blackjacking

            Earth orbs charged at the Wilderness obelisk would pay for Chapter 2, but a UIM drops the orbs, and on the wiki's numbers stealing artefacts in Port Piscarilius can't replace them as money: each delivery pays only 500 to 1,000 coins. Blackjacking Menaphite thugs is better on both counts: about 233,000 XP an hour at 80 Thieving with wines, and the wiki puts blackjacking from 45 to 91 at about 5,000,000 coins with the full rogue outfit, roughly 0.86 gp per XP.

            So this step has a level target, not a coin target: blackjack Menaphite thugs from about 80 to 85 Thieving. That is about 1,270,000 XP (3,258,594 for 85 against 1,986,068 for 80), about five and a half hours, and at the wiki's rate it brings in roughly 1.1 million coins. Stop at 85 even if the coins land a little short or long: 85 is what Chapter 2 builds on (elf pickpocketing opens there once you reach Lletya).

            Take the magic carpet from the Shantay Pass to Pollnivneach. Buy a willow blackjack from the blackjack seller in Pollnivneach, as you did in 1.3. He stands just south of the kebab shop. Put all six graceful pieces in the magic wardrobe, take the full rogue outfit out of your costume room's armour case and wear it, so every successful pickpocket pays double. Buy jugs of wine from Faisal the Barman in the Asp & Snake Bar as you need them and drop each empty jug at once. Lure a Menaphite thug into a quiet corner, knock it out, pickpocket it twice while it's unconscious, and repeat; if a knock-out fails, knock it out again at once. The thugs live in the south of the town, and the wiki's spot is the southern part of the house next to the bar. A thug that has just shrugged off a knock-out turns on you, and while it is fighting you it can't be knocked out or pickpocketed, so the quick second swing matters. Put the outfit back in the armour case and the graceful back on when you reach 85, and drop the blackjack.

            While you're next in Port Piscarilius, steal and deliver a single artefact to Captain Khaled with a lockpick (the wiki says one is required; Martin Thwait's Lost and Found in the Rogues' Den sells them for 20 gp, so take the games necklace to Burthorpe for one, and drop it after the delivery): that is a Kourend & Kebos hard diary task, and it's the only artefact this chapter needs. Khaled is in the foodhall on the east side of the port, and he names one house in the south-western district built over the water. The drawer is upstairs in most of those houses and on the ground floor in the northern one. Watch the patrolling guards on the way out: if one catches you, the artefact is taken and you need a new task. Walk back to Khaled, because a teleport or a logout removes the artefact too.

            Done when you are 85 Thieving with about 1,420,000 gp.

            Coins: about 1,420,000 gp (roughly 1.1 million from the five levels) · Slots: about 27/28 with the jewellery · Requirements: 38 Crafting for the diary coif; 40 Smithing for gold bars; 65 Thieving and the full rogue outfit for Menaphite thugs

            Wiki references Ring of dueling Games necklace Blackjacking Stealing artefacts Elf (Thieving)

            43 Prayer

            The usual way to 43 Prayer is to kill about 70 green dragons in the Wilderness with Water Bolt and offer the bones at the Chaos Temple (church) altar, which saves half of them. For a UIM there are two ways to do it.

            Gate in 23 Magic for Water Bolt (Wilderness method); none for the Ectofuntus route.
            Carry in about 27/28; a deathpile at the Edgeville Monastery for the first method, which is also what makes the full bag harmless.
            Exit 43 Prayer; the anti-dragon shield (worn) and one set of dragon bones; the hammer, chisel, knife and fire runes left behind; about 1,415,000 gp; about 24/28.
            Carries forward the anti-dragon shield (Watchtower, then Elvarg in Chapter 2), one set of dragon bones (Watchtower).

            The Wilderness, with deathpiles (faster)

            First cast Lumbridge Home Teleport and ask Duke Horacio in the castle for an anti-dragon shield, then use the Soul Wars minigame teleport and its portal to Edgeville. The shield can only be worn once Dragon Slayer I is started, which you did at the Champions' Guild in 1.3. It cuts a green dragon's breath by roughly four-fifths, so wear it on every trip. Each trip starts with a planned death at the Edgeville Monastery's scorpion, as in the Abyss block. From the pile, take only: the anti-dragon shield, your staff of air, the rune pouch (it holds the water runes), the chaos runes, your noted raw swordfish and the ring of dueling. Cooked food doesn't survive a deathpile, so unnote a few fish at the Edgeville bank booth and cook them on the stove west of the bank before each trip. Kill green dragons at one of the Wilderness spots the wiki lists, for example north of the Graveyard of Shadows, with Water Bolt until your free slots are full of bones. Offer them at the Chaos Temple (church) altar at Wilderness level 38, west of the Lava Maze, then walk south below level 20 before you teleport out to the Ferox Enclave with the ring of dueling, which, like most teleports, fails above level 20. The temple is multi-combat and player killers watch it, so offer your bones without stopping and leave. Walk back to the pile inside the hour. If a player kills you, you lose that trip's cheap kit and bones and nothing else. If your chaos runes run low, Aubury in Varrock is a short walk from Edgeville. Plan on 70 to 80 dragons, three or four trips.

            Drop the green dragonhides. Chapter 2 plans its own crafting money. Keep one set of dragon bones for Watchtower, and keep the anti-dragon shield when you are done: Watchtower's Ogre Enclave and Chapter 2's first block, Elvarg, both need it. The bones are for Toban, who won't accept baby dragon bones, so check that the set you keep is the adult kind. If you lose the shield, Duke Horacio gives you another for free.

            When you collect the pile for the last time, put the shield on and leave it on. Your shield slot is empty anyway: the Chronicle was destroyed at the Abyss block's planned death and isn't bought back until after Temple of Ikov. Leave the hammer, the chisel, the knife and the fire runes in the pile, with any cooked swordfish. Biohazard, the next quest, needs five free slots, and each of these comes back for a few coins before its next job: the fire runes from Aubury after Guidor, the hammer and chisel in Varrock at the start of the Hunter block, and the knife in Catherby before Temple of Ikov.

            Done when you have killed the green dragons you planned, 70 to 80, over three or four trips.

            The Isle of Souls and the Ectofuntus (no Wilderness)

            The second way is to safespot the blue dragons in the Isle of Souls Dungeon, which is slower because they're tankier, and offer the bones at the Ectofuntus for four times their burying XP. The dungeon's entrance is on the east side of the island; the Soul Wars minigame teleport and a run north-east gets you there. The blue dragons are in the third of its four rooms, counting anti-clockwise from the entrance, and the wiki's safespot is the cove beside the adamantite rocks. The Ectofuntus needs no quest, and Ghosts Ahoy (for the ectophial) can wait for Chapter 2 if you arrive in Port Phasmatys by charter ship, which the wiki says skips the two ecto-token fee at the town's barrier. The Ectofuntus needs a pot and a bucket per bone, so work in loads of a few bones at a time. This route ends the same way as the other: get the anti-dragon shield from Duke Horacio and wear it, and drop the hammer, the chisel, the knife and the fire runes.

            Done when you are 43 Prayer.

            Coins: about 1,415,000 gp · Slots: about 24/28 at the end, with the dragon bones in the bag and the anti-dragon shield worn · Requirements: 23 Magic for Water Bolt; no quest for the Ectofuntus route

            Wiki references Green dragon Chaos Temple (church) Ectofuntus Ghosts Ahoy

            Ardougne and Yanille: Biohazard, Watchtower and the Observatory

            My Arm's Big Adventure used to be in this block. It needs seven unnoted buckets of supercompost carried to a troll roof on top of your kit, so it moves to Chapter 2, next to Making Friends with My Arm, which needs it.

            Gate in Plague City for Biohazard; 14 Magic, 15 Thieving, 25 Agility, 14 Herblore and 40 Mining for Watchtower; 40 Smithing and 43 Magic to superheat Watchtower's gold bar; the dragon bones and the anti-dragon shield.
            Carry in about 24/28; about 23/28 once the log basket is in the magic wardrobe with the graceful outfit.
            Exit Biohazard, Watchtower and Observatory Quest done; Underground Pass started; the bird snare; about 1,420,000 gp; about 23/28.
            Carries forward the bird snare from Yanille (the next block), the anti-dragon shield (worn). The log basket stays in the magic wardrobe until the next block.

            Biohazard

            Biohazard has you wear a gas mask, a medical gown and a priest gown top and bottom, and with the graceful outfit on, each would push a graceful piece into your bag. So cast Teleport to House first and put all six graceful pieces in the magic wardrobe, together with the log basket, which the wiki lists as magic wardrobe storage and which nothing needs before the next block. That leaves the five free slots this quest fills twice, at the Chemist and at the Dancing Donkey Inn. Then use the ring of dueling to Castle Wars, walk east to Yanille, unnote three planks at the bank and walk back to the professor in the reception room north of Castle Wars. Talk to him to start Observatory Quest, and again to give him the planks. He takes the planks first and only then asks for the bronze bar and the molten glass, and after Biohazard your bag has no room to unnote the planks beside the bars and the glass.

            Cast Ardougne Teleport. Talk to Elena in her house in East Ardougne to start Biohazard, and follow the wiki's steps: Jerico's bird feed and pigeon cage distract the watchtower, Omart's rope ladder gets you over the wall, the rotten apple goes into the mourners' cauldron, and the medical gown from Nurse Sarah's house gets you into the mourner headquarters, where the mourner upstairs drops the key when you kill him and the distillator is in the crate. Elena gives you three vials and a plague sample for the Varrock chemist Guidor.

            The wiki lists a gas mask for this quest: you climb Omart's ladder with it on. You dropped yours after Plague City, and a free one is in the cupboard in Edmond and Alrena's house, so take it before you go to Jerico. Jerico lives just south of the northern bank and north of the chapel: the bird feed is in the cupboard on his north wall and the pigeon cage is behind the house. Omart waits by the wall north of the Battlefield spirit tree. Stand where the two spiked fences meet at the small watchtower north of him, put the bird feed on a fence, then use the pigeon cage. In West Ardougne, the mourner headquarters is in the north-east corner: squeeze through the fence gap on its west side, take the rotten apple just west of the gap and use it on the stew in the cauldron. Nurse Sarah's house is the large one south-west of the West Ardougne church, and the gown is in its northern part. Wear the gown every time you go back to the headquarters. The mourner upstairs is level 13 and the key drops into your bag when he dies, so have a free slot; the distillator is in the third crate from the left on the north wall. You leave West Ardougne by the manhole in the middle of the city and the mud pile, or by talking to Kilron in the south-east corner. Once Elena has the distillator, drop the key and the medical gown, and the pigeon cage if it is still in your bag: her four items and the Chemist's touch paper take all five free slots.

            The Varrock gate guard confiscates any vial you carry in yourself. Teleporting no longer breaks the vials or the plague sample. Ask Captain Barnaby on the Ardougne dock to take you to Rimmington, talk to the Chemist there, and give the vials to his three errand boys outside: the liquid honey to Chancy, the ethenea to Da Vinci and the sulphuric broline to Hops. The Chemist only talks about the quest while the plague sample is in your inventory, and he gives you the touch paper that Guidor needs. If you hand a boy the wrong vial, give the rest out anyway, speak to all three in Varrock, and go back to Elena for a new set.

            The Observatory's bronze bar and molten glass wait until Guidor has the vials, because in Varrock the vials and the sample take every free slot you have. From Rimmington, cast Falador Teleport.

            From Falador, walk on to Varrock. Buy a priest gown top and bottom at Thessalia's, collect the vials from the errand boys in the Dancing Donkey Inn in the south-east of the city, wear the gown and go through the gate to Guidor's house. The Dancing Donkey Inn is inside the fenced area: its guard searches you at the gate and finds nothing, because the boys hold the vials, and you need the plague sample and three free slots to take them back. Don't step out of the fenced area again with the vials, or the guard takes them on the way back in. Guidor's house is in the south-east corner of the same area, and his wife Julie only lets a priest in, hence the gown. If you forget to buy one, Asyff in the Fancy Clothes Store inside the area gives one free. Take the gown off and drop it, and buy back about 200 fire runes (4 gp each) at Aubury's rune shop, south of Varrock East Bank. Cast Teleport to House, drop the gas mask and put the graceful outfit back on; the log basket stays in the wardrobe. Now make the Observatory's two items and Watchtower's gold bar, the glass first so that the bag never overfills. Cast Falador Teleport, buy one bucket of sand and one soda ash from the charter ship's trader on the Port Sarim docks, and smelt them into molten glass at the Falador furnace. In the Rimmington mine, mine one copper and one tin ore and cast Superheat Item on them for a bronze bar, and mine one gold ore and superheat it for the gold bar (40 Smithing). Then cast Ardougne Teleport, tell Elena, and talk to King Lathas in the castle to complete the quest: 3 quest points and 1,250 Thieving XP. Talk to him again to start Underground Pass, steal from the chest upstairs in the castle for the hard diary, and enter the Combat Training Camp north of the city for the diary. Lathas's throne room is on the first floor of East Ardougne Castle.

            Done when Biohazard is complete, Underground Pass is started and you hold a bronze bar and a molten glass.

            The Observatory

            Walk south to the professor in the reception room north of Castle Wars. He already has the planks: give him the bronze bar and then the molten glass, one conversation each. Check that he has taken all three items before you go underground, or you repeat the dungeon. Go down into the dungeon on the hill to the east and search the chests for the goblin kitchen key; the antipoison that respawns in the chest north-east of the stairs covers the spiders. The key is often in the south-easternmost chest but moves around, a level 31 poisonous spider can jump out of a chest, and you must read the dialogue to the end after the right chest or the key isn't given. Prod the sleeping guard at the kitchen gate and trap him behind a column or kill him, and take the lens mould from the stove. A trapped guard attacks again when you come back past. The professor gives the molten glass back: use it on the mould, hand him the lens and the mould, walk through the dungeon to the Observatory without clicking anything during the cutscene, look through the telescope and name your constellation. If the cutscene doesn't start on the Observatory stairs, the professor doesn't have the finished lens yet. It gives 2 quest points and 2,250 Crafting XP. Drop the reward unless it is XP.

            Done when Observatory Quest is complete.

            Watchtower

            In Yanille's Dragon Inn, stamp the barcrawl card. At Aleck's Hunter Emporium, which also counts for the diary, buy a bird snare (6 gp) for the next block. Spotted kebbits are caught by falconry, so you need no noose wand. The shop is just east of the inn, and viewing its stock is the easy Ardougne Diary task.

            Do the whole of Watchtower, starting with the Watchtower Wizard on the tower's top floor (the trellis outside needs 18 Agility). The wiki's list is 20 gp, a gold bar, a tinderbox, a pickaxe, dragon bones (baby dragon bones don't work), two ropes, a guam leaf and a vial of water, a pestle and mortar, bat bones, jangerberries, a light source, a death rune (it turns up during the quest) and two cave nightshades, plus the anti-dragon shield for the Ogre Enclave. You arrive with the gold bar, the dragon bones, the shield and the noted guam; collect the rest where the quest uses it, not all at once: the ropes, the vial and the pestle and mortar from Yanille's shops, the jangerberries on the island west of the city, the cave nightshades in the Skavid caves (take a third for the diary), and the bat bones from the wiki's nearest bats. Use your knives on Gorad, a level 68 chieftain you can safespot in the middle of his island. Make the magic ogre potion in this order: guam potion (unf), jangerberries (use them, because left-click eats them), then ground bat bones; never add ground bones first, or it explodes. The reward is 4 quest points, 15,250 Magic XP, 5,000 gp and the Watchtower Teleport spell.

            UIM risk Don't drop anything in the instanced Watchtower before the final lever, because dropped items are lost.

            Have a free slot before you kill Gorad, because Grew wants his tooth and a full bag means killing him again. Toban takes the dragon bones; don't bury them by mistake. The 20 gp pays the bridge guards in Gu'Tanoth if you are below 25 Agility, the city guards' riddle is answered with the death rune, and the gold bar, used on a guard, opens the way to the Mad Skavid. In the city, steal the rock cake from the southern market stall and use it on the guard instead of eating it. Don't eat the cave nightshades either: they hurt. In the Ogre Enclave, the use and eat options on the cave nightshade are swapped, so read the menu before you click, and never talk to or attack an ogre shaman, which costs 20 or 30 Hitpoints; the finished potion is what kills them. If the last shaman's cyan crystal goes missing, search the shaman robe in the south-east corner. Mine the Rock of Dalgroth in the centre of the enclave (mine it, don't prospect it) before you leave, or you need nightshades to get back in, then run past the blue dragons to the north-east exit with the shield on.

            UIM risk One rope is lost swinging to Grew's island from the long branch, not the trunk, and the second gets you back; a rope spawns near Toban if you need another.

            Leave The Hand in the Sand for Chapter 2: it needs 49 Crafting, which can't be boosted.

            Done when Watchtower is complete.

            Coins: about 1,420,000 gp · Slots: about 23/28 at the end; 28/28 for a moment at the Chemist and at the Dancing Donkey Inn, about 27/28 with the bars and the glass · Requirements: as each quest lists

            Wiki references Biohazard Observatory Quest Watchtower Charter ship The Hand in the Sand

            Hunter to 46

            Bird runs should have taken you to 42 Hunter.

            Gate in 42 Hunter; the bird snare from Aleck's; 500 coins for Matthias's falcon.
            Carry in about 23/28; about 25/28 once the log basket, hammer and chisel are back and the bird snare is gone.
            Exit 46 Hunter; the cerulean twitch and essence impling diary tasks; about 1,420,000 gp; about 25/28.
            Carries forward nothing new.

            Start by taking back what Biohazard made you leave. Cast Teleport to House and take the log basket out of the magic wardrobe. Then walk to the fairy ring you prefer (Camelot Teleport and ALS is the standing route) and take ring DKS first: catch a cerulean twitch with the bird snare for the Fremennik diary, then drop the snare and whatever the catch gave you. It is an easy diary task and needs only 11 Hunter, and doing it first frees the snare's slot for later. Cast Varrock Teleport and buy a hammer and a chisel at the general store (1 gp each); bird houses are made with both. Spotted kebbits need 43 Hunter, so make one more bird house run from the Digsite barge first if you are still on 42.

            Return to a fairy ring the same way, take ring AKQ to the Piscatoris Hunter area and catch spotted kebbits by falconry to 46 Hunter. Matthias, at the falconry area, rents you a gyr falcon and a falconer's glove for 500 coins, and nothing may be worn in your weapon, shield or hands slots while you hold the glove. He moves those items into your bag if there is room, so you need three free slots, one each for your staff, the anti-dragon shield and your gloves, which is what dropping the snare left you. Drop the fur and bones as they come. When you reach 46, talk to Matthias to hand the falcon back, and put the three items on again. Take the ring to Zanaris and enter Puro-Puro through the wheat field: Elnock Inquisitor gives you a net and jars if you have none, and you catch an essence impling for the diary (eclectic implings need 50 Hunter). The task is the medium Lumbridge & Draynor Diary one, and it needs 42 Hunter, which you already have. Drop the Hunter tools.

            From 46 Hunter, earth implings can give an earth talisman; from 65, magpie implings and the Taverley crystal chest are the best sources of a runite bar for Song of the Elves. Neither is needed in this chapter.

            Done when you are 46 Hunter.

            Coins: about 1,420,000 gp, less 500 for the falcon · Slots: about 25/28 at the end; 28/28 at Matthias, with your staff, shield and gloves in the bag · Requirements: 43 Hunter for spotted kebbits

            Wiki references Spotted kebbit Cerulean twitch Puro-Puro

            Temple of Ikov

            Winelda, deep in the temple, takes 20 unnoted limpwurt roots, which means 20 free slots at the moment you unnote them. That is a deathpile quest for a UIM, and it uses the Falador Castle pile you know from 1.3.

            Gate in 42 Thieving; 20 noted limpwurt roots; a thrown weapon; 51 Magic for Ardougne Teleport; a Falador Castle deathpile.
            Carry in from the pile only: the roots, the knives, the rune pouch, coins, a candle, the tinderbox, a knife and food.
            Exit Temple of Ikov done; the Kandarin diary steps; about 1,420,000 gp; about 24/28.
            Carries forward nothing new.

            Cast Camelot Teleport and buy a candle at the candle shop in Catherby, and a knife (15 gp) from the trader on the charter ship at the Catherby dock to replace the one you left at Edgeville; enter the Ranging Guild south-west of Seers' Village for the diary (40 Ranged) and steal from the chest in Hemenster for the diary. Unnote and cook one swordfish, which is all the free slots allow once the candle and the knife are in the bag. Cast Falador Teleport.

            Die in Falador Castle as before. Walk back from Lumbridge and take from the pile only the noted roots, the knives, the rune pouch, your coins, the candle, the tinderbox, the knife and the cooked swordfish. Unnote the 20 roots at the Falador west bank, and cast Ardougne Teleport. Winelda wants all twenty in one hand-over, which is why they ride unnoted and why everything else stays in the pile. The light load also helps on the lava bridge, which only lets you over at -1 kg or less.

            Do Temple of Ikov, starting with Lucien in the Flying Horse Inn. In the temple north of the city, light the candle for the dark stairs at the start, cut through the web for the boots of lightness and wear them: the lava bridge needs your weight at -1 kg or less. Lucien gives you his pendant at the start; wear it in your amulet slot (your amulet of accuracy is waiting in the pile), because the dungeon's north door, and a later one, only open for someone wearing it. The temple's ladder is directly south of the Ranging Guild and west of the Sorcerer's Tower, and the dark stairs are west of the entrance. If the bridge drops you, you take 20 damage; walk south and take the ladder, which puts you outside south of Hemenster. Use "Search for traps" on the trapped lever before you pull it. Pulling it unsearched drops you onto spikes for 20 damage. The Fire Warrior only takes damage while at least one ice arrow is equipped (they come from the chests in the ice chamber and aren't used up), so equip one arrow and throw knives at him. Only one of the six chests holds arrows at a time and the chamber has level 61 ice spiders, so search until a chest gives you arrows and move on. The Fire Warrior is level 84 and attacks only with magic, so put Protect from Magic on. Don't drift away or take too long: he vanishes and the fight starts again. Give Winelda the roots, and choose your ending as the wiki describes; siding with the Guardians of Armadyl means killing Lucien at his house between Edgeville and the Grand Exchange, while wearing the Armadyl pendant. To side with the Guardians, take the Pendant of Lucien off before you talk to them and pick the wiki's dialogue options; wearing it, or a wrong answer, makes them attack. The quest gives 1 quest point, 10,500 Ranged XP and 8,000 Fletching XP.

            Cast Falador Teleport and pick up your pile; you have an hour from the death. Buy the Chronicle back in Draynor when you next pass.

            Done when Temple of Ikov is complete and your pile is collected.

            Coins: about 1,420,000 gp · Slots: about 24/28 at the end; the roots ride in a planned deathpile · Requirements: 42 Thieving for Temple of Ikov; 40 Ranged for the Ranging Guild

            Wiki references Temple of Ikov Limpwurt root Tool Leprechaun Kandarin Diary

            Desert Treasure

            Gate in 53 Thieving, 50 Firemaking, 50 Magic and 10 Slayer; The Dig Site, Temple of Ikov, The Tourist Trap, Troll Stronghold, Priest in Peril and Waterfall Quest; 30 Smithing for steel bars.
            Carry in about 24/28.
            Exit Desert Treasure done (the Ancient spellbook unlocked, not switched on); about 1,420,000 gp; about 22/28.
            Carries forward the ring of visibility.

            Eblis's list, made in Al Kharid

            Talk to the Archaeologist at the Bedabin Camp to start Desert Treasure and run his errand to Terry Balando at the Digsite's Exam Centre and back. Eblis, east of the Bandit Camp, then wants 12 magic logs, 6 steel bars, 6 molten glass, ashes, charcoal, a blood rune and bones. The wiki says an Ultimate Ironman can deliver these in several trips by using each item on him, so no deathpile is needed: make them in Al Kharid and carry them out in two loads. The Archaeologist is Asgarnia Smith; when you bring back Terry's translation and he offers you the book, choose "Don't read book" and then agree to help. Before Eblis talks about the diamonds, buy a drink from the Bandit Camp's bartender for 650 coins. Take off any Saradomin or Zamorak item before you walk into the camp, or the bandits attack, and don't wear Zamorak gear while you hand items to Eblis.

            • The magic logs are the noted stack from the Reward Cart, and stay noted. Magic logs come out of the cart 10 to 20 at a time, so the stack should cover the twelve; if it falls short, search the cart again, because you can't chop them yourself yet (magic trees need 75 Woodcutting and you have 70).
            • The steel bars: mine one iron and two coal in the Al Kharid mine and smelt a bar at the furnace, six times.
            • The molten glass: on the Port Sarim docks, buy six buckets of sand and six soda ash from the charter ship's trader, smelt them at the Falador furnace, and use the ring of dueling to get back to the desert.
            • Ashes from any fire, bones from anything you kill, a charcoal from the specimen trays at the Digsite while you are with Terry Balando (or from Jiminua's by ring CKR), and a blood rune from Ali Morrisane's rune shop or Regath's Wares in Arceuus.

            The bars and glass are twelve slots, so take the bars and the small items first and the glass second.

            Done when Eblis has everything and the mirrors are up.

            The four diamonds

            Then do the rest of the quest with the wiki's list: a silver bar, garlic powder, spice, a cake or chocolate bar, spiked boots, climbing boots, a facemask (200 gp from any Slayer master), a tinderbox and lockpicks. Buy each where its diamond needs it. The UIM notes:

            • Blood: the silver pot made for Dessous has to be blessed by the High Priest on Entrana, and Entrana bans weapons and armour. Make it a Falador Castle deathpile trip like the earlier ones, and take only the silver pot and your coins from the pile. Dessous is best met with Protect from Melee, and his combined magic and ranged attack always hits double 5s, so keep your Hitpoints above 16. Catch a swamp lizard on the way for the Morytania diary. Talk to Malak in Canifis before you take the silver bar to Ruantun in the Draynor Sewers, or Ruantun won't make the pot. After the blessing, Malak fills it with your blood (5 Hitpoints, never fatal); add the garlic powder and the spice, and check that its examine text names blood, garlic and spices, because an incomplete pot lets Dessous heal to full. Pour it on the tomb in the Morytania graveyard. If he teleports you away three times he leaves and you refill the pot.
            • Smoke: wear the facemask in the Smoke Dungeon. Fareed can be kited inside the room; you need ice gloves or he disarms you, so fight him with Water Bolt, not a weapon. The dungeon is west of Pollnivneach, and without the mask you take damage every 12 seconds. Light its four torches with your tinderbox (a bruma torch doesn't work) and move fast, because they burn out. The warm key for Fareed's room is in the burnt chest.
            • Shadow: Rasolo, west of the Fishing Guild, gives you a ring of visibility for the Shadow Dungeon: keep it, because Chapter 2's Curse of the Empty Lord and The General's Shadow need it (he replaces a lost one). Damis can be safespotted on a rat in the north-east of the room. Kill a shadow hound in the dungeon for the diary, and start Curse of the Empty Lord while you're there. Rasolo trades the ring for the gilded cross, which is in the secure chest in the southernmost tent of the Bandit Camp: it has three locks, a failed lock resets the others and breaks a lockpick, which is why the list says lockpicks. Keep the ring on for the whole fight, or Damis never appears. He has two forms, level 103 and then level 174, which drains Prayer. If you teleport out after the kill, the diamond stays on the floor for you to collect.
            • Ice: Kamil is the hard one. Dunstan in Burthorpe makes the spiked boots from climbing boots and an iron bar. Bring Fire Bolt runes and as much cooked swordfish as you can carry, and keep moving on the ice path. The troll child is on the north-western path from Trollheim; give him the cake or chocolate bar and talk to him again. The ice path drains your stats and run energy, so between attempts take the ring of dueling to Ferox and use a Pool of Refreshment. Kill the five ice trolls at the Ice Gate (they hit up to 25; Protect from Melee or a safespot), and fight Kamil under Protect from Melee with your Hitpoints at 12 or more, because he freezes you often. If you lose the ice diamond, the troll child gives you another.

            Carrying a diamond can bring out a level 95 Stranger with a poisoned dragon dagger. The wiki's advice for an Ultimate Ironman is to put each diamond into its obelisk around the pyramid south-east of Eblis as soon as you have it (50 Magic).

            Desert Treasure unlocks the Ancient spellbook, and completing the quest in the altar room at the heart of the pyramid switches you to it. Pray at the altar before you leave to switch back to the standard spellbook, which every later block in this guide expects. The switch drains your Prayer to 0, so do it on the way out, not before a fight. Praying at the altar twice after the quest also completes a Desert hard diary task.

            Done when Desert Treasure is complete.

            Coins: about 1,420,000 gp · Slots: about 22/28 at the end; about 26/28 on each trip to Eblis · Requirements: 53 Thieving, 50 Firemaking, 50 Magic, 10 Slayer

            Wiki references Desert Treasure I Molten glass Ring of visibility

            Varlamore and Arceuus: the last loose ends

            The chapter ends without making Paddewwa tablets or charging earth orbs with repeated Wilderness deaths.

            Gate in each quest's requirements.
            Carry in about 22/28.
            Exit Twilight's Promise and Scrambled! done; Bear Your Soul started; about 1,420,000 gp; about 23/28.
            Carries forward the damaged soul bearer (2.1).

            Teleport to Varrock and take the quetzal to Civitas illa Fortis; do Twilight's Promise, which needs two combat styles (magic and melee). From now on your tree runs can include the Kastori fruit tree and calquat patches. Take the quetzal to Tal Teklan and, with your 46 Construction (it also wants 36 Cooking and 35 Smithing, which you have), do Scrambled!.

            Twilight's Promise starts with Ennius or Furia Tullus in front of the Sunrise Palace. The two styles are for Mezan, the knight you spar in the Colosseum: he prays against your current style every fourth attack, so switch between spells and melee on that rhythm and his prayers never catch up. Later, Azali has to follow you, and an active pet or follower stops her, so leave any follower behind. Scrambled! starts with Alan at the temple of Tal Teok, north of Tal Teklan. Its saw, nails, planks and bowl of water spawn in and just outside the town (the planks are south of Tal Teklan by the water), and your own hammer does for the hammer. It includes three fights: a level 16 large chicken, a level 88 black jaguar (safespottable) and a level 106 red dragon, so carry the anti-dragon shield in, or safespot it with the eggs as the wiki shows.

            Cast Teleport to House, take the spade from the Hosidius leprechaun if you stored one (Horace's store sells one otherwise) and ride the minecart to Arceuus to start Bear Your Soul: read Soul Journey in the Arceuus Library, talk to Aretha at the Soul Altar in northern Arceuus ("I've been reading your book..."), then go into the Arceuus church crypt and dig in its centre for the damaged soul bearer. Aretha also mentions the Key Master, who repairs the soul bearer; Chapter 2 visits him. The book is on one of the library's bookshelves, and Biblia, on the top floor, can narrow down which. The church is east of the library: climb the steps into it, then go down the stairs inside to the centre area and dig anywhere there. If you lose the damaged soul bearer, dig there again for another. Chapter 2 hands it to the Key Master in Cerberus' Lair, in the hellhound area of Taverley Dungeon, which completes the quest.

            Temple of the Eye is not started here any more: its eye amulet would ride in your bag into Chapter 2, which starts the quest itself in its Al Kharid loop.

            Done when Twilight's Promise and Scrambled! are complete and you hold the damaged soul bearer.

            Coins: about 1,420,000 gp · Slots: about 23/28 · Requirements: as each quest lists

            Wiki references Twilight's Promise Scrambled! Bear Your Soul

            Port Sarim: Dragon Slayer I's map and ship, and The Corsair Curse

            Chapter 2 opens on Crandor, so the chapter's last block collects Dragon Slayer I's three map pieces in one loop, joins them into a single map, and repairs the ship. Done here, the map is one slot for one block; done where the old route had it, the pieces were three slots for half the section.

            Gate in Dragon Slayer I started (32 quest points); 33 Magic for Telekinetic Grab or 10,000 gp for the goblin's piece.
            Carry in about 23/28.
            Exit the Crandor map; the Lady Lumbridge bought and repaired; The Corsair Curse done; about 1,415,000 gp; about 24/28.
            Carries forward the Crandor map and the anti-dragon shield (Elvarg, in Chapter 2's first block).

            The Oracle's door

            Break a Chronicle card and ask the Guildmaster in the Champions' Guild for the maze key again. Collect the four things the Dwarven Mine's magic door wants, in the order you pass them: a silk from the silk stall in Ardougne market or the Al Kharid silk trader, an unfired bowl spun at the Barbarian Village pottery wheel from a soft clay (mine the clay south-west of Varrock and wet it with a jug of water), a wizard's mind bomb from the Rising Sun Inn in Falador, and a lobster pot from Gerrant's in Port Sarim. Ask the Oracle on Ice Mountain about the map ("I seek a piece of the map to the island of Crandor"), go down into the Dwarven Mine and use the four items, one at a time, on the magic door at the mine's north-east side, and open the chest inside for Thalzar's map piece. The key needs a free slot when he hands it over. While you are with the Guildmaster, also ask him about each of the three map pieces, including the one the goblin Lozar had: the wiki says you can't attack Wormbrain until you have asked about it. At the door, use the mind bomb rather than clicking it, or you drink it.

            Done when you hold the first map piece.

            Melzar's Maze and Wormbrain

            Walk to Rimmington and go through Melzar's Maze with the maze key, your rune sword, Fire Bolt and cooked swordfish: each floor's key comes from one marked monster (a rat with a longer tail, a ghost without a cape, a skeleton with a round shield, then a zombie, Melzar the Mad and a lesser demon you can safespot from beside the magenta door). Open the chest at the end for the second piece and drop the keys. The maze is the building north-west of Rimmington. The lesser demon is weak to water spells, so Water Bolt from your rune pouch works there; the wiki's ranged and magic safespot is one square to the right of the magenta door as you face north.

            In the Port Sarim jail, Wormbrain the goblin has the third piece: cast Telekinetic Grab on it through the bars after killing him (one law rune), or buy it from him for 10,000 gp. Take the security book from the guard upstairs for the diary and drop it. Use the three pieces on each other for the Crandor map. Wormbrain is in the third cell and has only 5 Hitpoints, so a spell or two through the bars is enough.

            Done when you hold the Crandor map.

            The Lady Lumbridge and The Corsair Curse

            Buy the Lady Lumbridge from Klarense on the Port Sarim docks for 2,000 gp. Repair the hull below decks with three planks, unnoted at the nearest bank or bought new, 90 steel nails from a sawmill operator, and your hammer. Ned in Draynor captains her in Chapter 2. Klarense is on the second dock from the south. Below decks, click the hole three times.

            Talk to Captain Tock at the crossroads north of Port Sarim to start The Corsair Curse, sail with him from Rimmington's western dock to Corsair Cove, and follow the wiki's short investigation: it needs a spade and a tinderbox, and ends with Ithoi the Navigator (level 35) upstairs in his hut. Report back to Tock to complete it for 2 quest points. The tinderbox is for the driftwood under Ithoi's hut, which smokes him out of bed when he refuses to get up, and the spade digs up the doll in the sand lump west of his hut. Fight him with Fire Bolt rather than your knives, so the knife stack comes home whole.

            UIM risk The final fight with Ithoi is instanced: anything you drop there is lost, and the wiki warns that ranged ammunition is lost at the end of the fight.
            Done when the ship is repaired and The Corsair Curse is complete.

            Coins: about 1,415,000 gp · Slots: about 24/28 · Requirements: 32 quest points for Dragon Slayer I; 33 Magic for Telekinetic Grab

            Wiki references Dragon Slayer I Melzar's Maze Crandor map The Corsair Curse

            End of Chapter 1

            You finish Chapter 1 with about 1.42 million gp, a house in Hosidius with a costume room and a workshop, a rune pouch, a seed box, a log basket, 250 Tithe Farm points held back for the herb sack, farming contracts running, Desert Treasure done (the Ancient spellbook is unlocked but not switched on, so you finish on the standard spellbook), Twilight's Promise and Scrambled! behind you, and the Crandor map in your bag. Your end-of-chapter stats:

            SkillLevelNotes
            Thieving85From blackjacking
            Construction46
            Craftingabout 46Quest XP; Chapter 2's glassblowing takes it past 50
            Magicabout 63
            Prayer43
            Ranged43+
            Hunter46
            Farming62+
            Woodcutting70
            Firemaking85+
            Herbloreabout 34Jungle Potion and Eadgar's Ruse
            Smithing40Quest XP and the Blast Furnace

            Agilityabout 62Canifis for the graceful outfit
            Where your things are
            StorageContents
            Armour caseRogue outfit, angler's outfit
            Magic wardrobeThe graceful outfit whenever a step needs other gear
            Seed boxBird house seeds, herb seeds from batch 3 and farming
            Tool leprechaunRake, seed dibber, trowel, watering can, Gricoller's can, the magic secateurs, compost and ultracompost, buckets
            Log basketLogs for bird houses
            HouseHosidius, with parlour, kitchen, costume room and workshop

            Carried at the end (about 25 slots): coins, the rune pouch (law, nature and water runes), fire runes, chaos runes, hammer, chisel, knife, tinderbox, adamant pickaxe, rune axe, earth runes (Teleport to House), the noted swordfish, the noted planks, the barcrawl card, rune sword, dramen staff, seed box, log basket, the throwing knives, the spare ring of dueling and the games necklace, the noted mahogany logs (22 if the cart gave that many), the anti-dragon shield, the Crandor map, the ring of visibility from Rasolo (he replaces a lost one), the damaged soul bearer and the stardust from the shooting stars (one stackable slot, toward the 2,000 for the celestial ring). Worn: graceful, the staff of air, the Chronicle, a ring of dueling or the ring of charos (a), the amulet of accuracy. There is no plank sack yet: Chapter 2's Mahogany Homes block earns it. The ghostspeak amulet isn't on this list on purpose: Chapter 2 collects a new one from Father Urhney before Ghosts Ahoy and the other quests that need it.

            Done in Chapter 1 that Chapter 2 builds on: Children of the Sun (Regulus Cento outside Varrock's east gate flies you to Civitas illa Fortis), Twilight's Promise and Scrambled! (so the quetzal network is open), The Grand Tree, The Eyes of Glouphrie, Garden of Tranquillity, Creature of Fenkenstrain, The Shadow of the Storm, The Feud, Romeo & Juliet, Prince Ali Rescue, The Lost Tribe, The Giant Dwarf, Forgettable Tale of a Drunken Dwarf, Eadgar's Ruse, Watchtower, Plague City, Biohazard, Temple of Ikov, Desert Treasure, Enter the Abyss, Jungle Potion, and Dragon Slayer I's map and ship.

            Started and waiting for Chapter 2: Dragon Slayer I (Elvarg), Recipe for Disaster (Another Cook's Quest is done; Freeing Pirate Pete needs a fishbowl and at least three raw cod), Bear Your Soul, Underground Pass, Curse of the Empty Lord, Tai Bwo Wannai Trio, Death to the Dorgeshuun, Fairytale II, Olaf's Quest, Alfred Grimhand's Barcrawl, Family Crest, Rag and Bone Man I, Below Ice Mountain and Rogue Trader.

            Moved out of Chapter 1 by the inventory audit. Each of these was in an earlier version of this chapter and failed the same test: it either needs more unnoted items at once than a UIM can hold beside a kit, or it leaves something in the bag for a whole chapter. Chapter 2 picks them up: Family Crest's three brothers (Caleb's five fish, Avan's perfect jewellery and the crest pieces they hand out); Rag and Bone Man I (eight bones, eight pots of vinegar); Below Ice Mountain and Black Knights' Fortress; Troll Romance's sled and trollweiss; My Arm's Big Adventure (seven buckets of supercompost); Enlightened Journey and the balloon routes (about 48 unnoted items); Monkey Madness I; Temple of the Eye's start (the eye amulet); the holy symbol; and the crystal saw, which Chapter 2 collects again when its lectern needs it. Not started, because it needs 49 Crafting: The Hand in the Sand.

            Verification and sources

            Verified against the wiki

            • [x] Section 1.1, checked on the wiki (6 Nov 2026). Marlo's crate (25 planks, 10 oak planks, 8 bolts of cloth and 5 steel bars, all noted; 50 mithril nails; 5 house tabs and a Falador tab). Daddy's Home's 544 + 400 Construction XP, which is level 8, not 9. Torn curtains at 132 XP. Monk's Friend, Sheep Herder (now also 1,000 Hunter XP), Tribal Totem (1,775 Thieving XP, 5 swordfish) and Witch's Potion rewards. Tower of Life needs no gloves; its rewards are 1,000 Construction, 500 Crafting and 500 Thieving XP. Sea Slug's and Tribal Totem's steps were rewritten from the wiki's quick guides. The Reward Cart's coin roll (2,000–4,999, 5/25), kindling's 0.6 × Fletching level XP, Diango's prices (Chronicle 300, card 150, scarf 120) and steel nails at 3 gp from the sawmill are all confirmed. The oak and teak counts were recomputed from the XP table.
            • [x] Section 1.2, checked on the wiki. The Food Store's stock and the Rimmington jug are confirmed. The iron bar spawns just outside Ferox Enclave, so it isn't in the safe zone. Harry's shop sells no fly fishing rods or feathers, so both now come from Gerrant's. Murder Mystery's steps (empty pot, flypaper, silver items, poison salesman) and its rewards (1,406 Crafting XP, 2,000 gp) are confirmed. Tourist Trap gives 2 × 4,650 XP, which takes Agility to 26, not 27. The fruit stall gives 28.5 XP. The Feud accepts clue hunter gloves.
            • [x] Section 1.3, checked on the wiki. The Woodcutting Guild has 4 oaks and a sawmill. The saw spawn in the Varrock estate agent's house is confirmed; saws also cost 13 gp at any sawmill. Ali stocks 100 nature and 100 law runes. The Rogues' Den gives a crate you choose a piece from, so there are no duplicates. White Knights are not aggressive and max hit 6. Rock crabs max hit 1 and turn tolerant after 10 minutes. Every death to Koschei is safe and needs no food. The Dig Site needs 2 ropes plus an opal. Merlin's Crystal's ritual is in Camelot's garden, not at the altar. Each elemental bar takes 4 coal, so mine 13. The Shades of Mort'ton item list has been replaced (no planks). The Fremennik Trials need 7 votes. The jade necklace needs 25 Crafting and Lvl-2 Enchant. The Graveyard pays 1 point per 16 bananas, so ten free slots give about 0.6 points per nature rune, consistent with the 2,300 target.
            • [x] Section 1.4, checked on the wiki. Tenzing takes all 21 items at once. Eblis accepts noted items, but a UIM cannot note bars or glass, so the hand-in takes about 13 slots. The balloon log storage takes noted logs. The Monastery scorpion is upstairs behind 31 Prayer and kills by stinging for 3 when you try to pick it up. Sleeping Giants needs its repair materials plus 20 free slots. The evil disguise needs three black pieces and 30 Crafting. Gricoller's can costs 200 points.
            • [x] Deathpile rule applied everywhere: the wiki's UIM guide says un-noted food and potions disappear from a deathpile. Every planned death now keeps fish noted and cooks after the pickup, on the Falador range east of the east bank or the Edgeville stove west of the bank.
            • [x] Raw fish in planned deaths: the wiki's UIM item management page says un-noted food and potions disappear from a deathpile, and neither it nor the Items Kept on Death page says whether raw fish counts as food. The guide therefore keeps fish noted in every planned death and cooks after the pickup, on the Falador range east of the east bank or the Edgeville stove west of the bank; nothing in the route depends on testing it.
            • [x] Tempoross target set: 56 Fishing is the exit, not a permit count, because the pool's loot is random. The 1.2 block gives the expected loot for about 200 permits as a reference, and 1.3 tops up planks from the sawmill if the rolls fall short.

            About this guide

            Written by Marcos (UK Guard).

            The early route was first inspired by the BRUHsailer Ironman guide by So Iron BRUH and ParasailerOSRS; this guide is an independent rewrite for Ultimate Ironman.

            Mechanics are checked against the Old School RuneScape Wiki, and every block links the wiki pages it relies on.
